When to Align, When to Predict: A Phase Diagram for Multimodal Learning
Authors: Ilay Kamai, Hugues Van Assel, Aviv Regev, Hagai B. Perets, Randall Balestriero
First: 2026-06-09T17:59:58+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-09T17:59:58+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Cross-modal alignment (CA) and cross-modal prediction (CP) are the dominant paradigms for multimodal representation learning, yet there is no systematic understanding of when each succeeds, when each fails, and when cross-modal training helps at all -- a gap that leaves practitioners, especially in scientific domains like biomedicine or astrophysics, with heterogeneous instruments and multiple levels of organization and measurement, unable to diagnose why standard methods underperform the best single modality. We develop a unified linear framework that addresses both questions. Under a spiked signal-plus-noise model with structured cross-modal nuisance correlation, we derive separation ratios for both objectives that expose complementary failure modes: alignment whitens each modality and fails when nuisance is strongly correlated across views; prediction encodes whatever is cross-predictable through a one-sided whitening, with recovery governed by source-modality quality. The resulting phase diagram partitions multimodal problems into four regimes: Both, CA only, CP only, and Neither. We present a data-driven procedure to locate real-world datasets in this diagram using a small labeled subsample, identifying the preferred objective and prediction direction before any cross-modal training. Experiments on synthetic data, stereo-vision benchmarks, image-caption pairs, and real astrophysical data validate the predictions in the nonlinear regime, including the Neither regime where cross-modal training is actively harmful. Our framework lets practitioners diagnose their multimodal problem and choose the right objective before committing to training. Code to reproduce the results is available at https://github.com/IlayMalinyak/mm_align_vs_pred.
中文标题/摘要
标题：何时对齐，何时预测：多模态学习的相图
跨模态对齐（CA）和跨模态预测（CP）是多模态表征学习的主流范式，但目前尚无系统性理解每种方法在何种情况下成功、何种情况下失败，以及跨模态训练何时有益——这一空白使从业者，特别是在生物医学或天体物理学等科学领域，面对异构仪器和多层次组织与测量时，无法诊断为何标准方法表现劣于最佳单模态方法。我们构建了一个统一的线性框架，同时解决这两个问题。在具有结构化跨模态干扰相关性的尖峰信号加噪声模型下，我们推导出两种目标的分离比率，揭示了互补的失败模式：对齐通过白化各模态而失效，当干扰在不同视角间强相关时；预测通过单侧白化编码跨可预测信息，其恢复由源模态质量决定。由此得到的相图将多模态问题划分为四个区域：两者皆可、仅CA、仅CP、两者皆不可。我们提出一种基于数据的程序，仅需少量标注子样本即可将真实数据集定位到该相图中，从而在任何跨模态训练前确定优选目标和预测方向。在合成数据、立体视觉基准、图像-文本对及真实天体物理数据上的实验验证了非线性区域（包括“两者皆不可”区域）的预测结果，其中跨模态训练甚至有害。该框架使从业者可在投入训练前诊断其多模态问题并选择合适目标。代码可在 https://github.com/IlayMalinyak/mm_align_vs_pred 获取。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the lack of systematic guidance on when cross-modal alignment or prediction succeeds in multimodal learning, especially in scientific domains with heterogeneous data, the paper introduces a unified linear framework under a spiked signal-plus-noise model with structured cross-modal noise. It derives separation ratios that reveal distinct failure modes: alignment fails when modalities share strong nuisance correlations, while prediction succeeds only if source modalities are of sufficient quality. The framework generates a phase diagram dividing problems into four regimes—Both, CA only, CP only, and Neither—and proposes a data-driven method to classify real-world datasets using a small labeled subset, guiding practitioners to choose the optimal objective before training. Experiments across synthetic, vision, image-caption, and astrophysical datasets confirm the framework’s predictions, including identifying cases where cross-modal training is harmful.
针对多模态学习中缺乏系统指导以判断何时应进行跨模态对齐或预测的问题，尤其是在生物医学或天文学等科学领域，该研究提出了一种统一的线性框架，基于带结构化跨模态干扰相关性的尖峰信号加噪声模型。该框架推导出分离比，揭示了两种方法不同的失效模式：对齐在模态间干扰强时失效，而预测仅在源模态质量足够时有效。框架生成一个四象限相图（Both、CA only、CP only、Neither），并提出一种仅需少量标注子样本即可在训练前定位真实数据集所属象限的数据驱动方法。在合成数据、立体视觉基准、图文对及真实天文数据上的实验验证了该框架在非线性情形下的预测能力，包括识别跨模态训练有害的Neither情形。
A Unifying Lens on Supervised Fine-Tuning Through Target Distribution Design
Authors: Tong Xie, Yuanhao Ban, Yunqi Hong, Sohyun An, Yihang Chen, Cho-Jui Hsieh
First: 2026-06-09T17:59:54+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-09T17:59:54+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Supervised fine-tuning (SFT) typically maximizes the likelihood of every token in a demonstrated trajectory. However, an observed token can be non-unique, noisy, or misaligned with the model prior. Strictly fitting toward this one-hot target may be suboptimal, especially when the pretrained model encodes a rich knowledge prior. In this work, we reinterpret SFT as target distribution design: instead of studying only the loss objective, we analyze the token-level target that the loss drives the model to match. We introduce the Q-target framework, which decomposes SFT supervision into two explicit choices: (1) how strongly to rely on the observed token, and (2) how to allocate the remaining probability mass over alternatives. This perspective unifies many existing SFT variants as implicit choices of the target distribution Q. Building on this view, we propose Target-SFT which constructs the training objective directly from the desired target distribution. This method consistently outperforms across the ten reasoning dataset-model settings evaluated, showing the effectiveness of this target-based approach. Overall, our formulation reveals a more fundamental design principle for SFT training and opens a broader search space for SFT objectives.
中文标题/摘要
标题：通过目标分布设计统一监督微调视角
监督微调（SFT）通常最大化演示轨迹中每个token的似然。然而，观察到的token可能非唯一、含噪声或与模型先验不一致。严格拟合这一one-hot目标可能次优，尤其当预训练模型编码丰富知识先验时。本文将SFT重新诠释为目标分布设计：不仅研究损失目标，还分析损失驱动模型匹配的token级目标。我们提出Q-target框架，将SFT监督分解为两个明确选择：(1) 对观察到的token依赖程度，(2) 如何在备选token上分配剩余概率质量。该视角将许多现有SFT变体统一为隐式目标分布Q的选择。基于此观点，我们提出Target-SFT，直接从期望目标分布构建训练目标。该方法在评估的十组推理数据集-模型设置中始终表现更优，验证了目标导向方法的有效性。总体而言，我们的框架揭示了SFT训练更根本的设计原则，并为SFT目标开辟了更广阔探索空间。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitations of standard supervised fine-tuning (SFT) that rigidly targets observed tokens while ignoring model priors, this work reinterprets SFT as a problem of designing target distributions over tokens. It introduces the Q-target framework, which explicitly separates the degree of reliance on observed tokens from the allocation of probability mass to alternatives, unifying various SFT variants as implicit choices of such distributions. The proposed Target-SFT method constructs the training objective directly from a desired target distribution, and experiments across ten reasoning dataset-model pairs show it consistently outperforms baseline SFT methods, demonstrating the effectiveness of this target-based approach.
为了解决传统监督微调（SFT）过度依赖观察到的单个token而忽视模型先验知识的问题，本文将SFT重新诠释为目标分布设计，提出Q-target框架，显式控制对观察token的依赖程度以及剩余概率质量在替代token上的分配。该框架将现有多种SFT变体统一为隐式目标分布的选择。基于此视角提出的Target-SFT方法直接从期望的目标分布构建训练目标，在十组推理数据集-模型设置中均优于基线方法，验证了目标导向方法在提升模型性能方面的有效性。
ARM: An AutoRegressive Large Multimodal Model with Unified Discrete Representations
Authors: Junke Wang, Xiao Wang, Jiacheng Pan, Xuefeng Hu, Feng Li, Jingxiang Sun, Chaorui Deng, Zilong Chen, Yunpeng Chen, Kaibin Tian, Matthew Gwilliam, Hao Chen, Danhui Guan, Kun Xu, Weilin Huang, Zuxuan Wu, Haoqi Fan, Yu-Gang Jiang, Zhenheng Yang
First: 2026-06-09T17:59:28+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-09T17:59:28+00:00
Comments: technical report
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
This paper introduces ARM, a discrete representation-based AutoRegressive Model that unifies image understanding, generation, and editing within a next-token prediction framework. ARM is built on three efforts: first, we train a discrete semantic visual tokenizer that maps images into compact token sequences. Our tokenizer is supervised with multiple objectives that jointly promote semantic discriminability, language alignment and faithful reconstruction, thereby supporting diverse tasks in a shared latent space. With this, we train a 7B autoregressive model over large-scale text and image token sequences, seamlessly developing vision-language perception and generation capabilities. Finally, to further improve preference-aligned behavior for text-to-image generation and instruction-guided editing, ARM applies reinforcement learning (RL) to optimize task-level objectives such as visual quality, instruction adherence, and edit consistency. Surprisingly, the results show that RL not only substantially improves performance on the target tasks (e.g., raising WISE overall from 0.50 to 0.56, GEdit-Bench-EN G_O from 5.75 to 6.68), but also induces cross-task synergy between text-to-image generation and editing. Collectively, these findings highlight autoregressive modeling, when paired with strong representations and preference optimization, as a scalable foundation for multimodal intelligence. Code: https://github.com/wdrink/ARM.
中文标题/摘要
标题：ARM：一种基于统一离散表示的自回归大尺度多模态模型
本文介绍了ARM，一种基于离散表示的自回归模型，它在下一个词预测框架内统一了图像理解、生成与编辑。ARM基于三项工作构建：首先，我们训练了一个离散语义视觉分词器，将图像映射为紧凑的词序列。该分词器通过多个目标监督训练，共同促进语义可区分性、语言对齐和忠实重建，从而支持共享潜在空间中的多样化任务。在此基础上，我们在大规模文本和图像词序列上训练了一个7B参数的自回归模型，无缝发展视觉-语言感知与生成能力。最后，为进一步提升文本到图像生成和指令引导编辑的偏好对齐行为，ARM应用强化学习（RL）优化视觉质量、指令遵循和编辑一致性等任务级目标。令人惊讶的是，结果表明RL不仅显著提升了目标任务性能（例如，WISE整体分数从0.50提升至0.56，GEdit-Bench-EN G_O从5.75提升至6.68），还诱导了文本到图像生成与编辑之间的跨任务协同效应。综合来看，这些发现突显了自回归建模在结合强表示与偏好优化时，作为多模态智能可扩展基础的潜力。代码：https://github.com/wdrink/ARM。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for a unified framework for multimodal tasks, ARM introduces an autoregressive model that leverages discrete visual tokens to jointly handle image understanding, generation, and editing through next-token prediction. The method begins by training a semantic visual tokenizer aligned with language and reconstruction objectives, then scales a 7B autoregressive model across text and image tokens to enable vision-language capabilities. Preference-aligned behavior is further enhanced via reinforcement learning, optimizing for visual quality, instruction adherence, and edit consistency. Experimental results show significant performance gains: WISE score improves from 0.50 to 0.56, and GEdit-Bench-EN G_O rises from 5.75 to 6.68, with additional cross-task synergy observed between generation and editing.
为实现图像理解、生成与编辑的统一处理，ARM提出一种基于离散表示的自回归模型，通过下一个token预测框架联合处理多模态任务。模型构建于一个语义视觉分词器之上，该分词器通过多目标监督训练，确保语义可区分性、语言对齐与重建保真度，从而在共享潜在空间中支持多样化任务。随后，一个7B规模的自回归模型在大规模图文token序列上训练，实现视觉-语言感知与生成能力。为提升偏好对齐行为，模型引入强化学习优化视觉质量、指令遵循与编辑一致性，显著提升性能：WISE整体得分从0.50提升至0.56，GEdit-Bench-EN G_O从5.75提升至6.68。值得注意的是，强化学习还促进了文本生成与编辑任务间的协同效应，验证了自回归建模结合强表示与偏好优化在多模态智能中的可扩展性。
Next Forcing: Causal World Modeling with Multi-Chunk Prediction
Authors: Gangwei Xu, Qihang Zhang, Jiaming Zhou, Xing Zhu, Yujun Shen, Xin Yang, Yinghao Xu
First: 2026-06-09T17:59:22+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-09T17:59:22+00:00
Comments: Project page: https://gangweix.github.io/next-forcing/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Autoregressive video generation has emerged as a powerful paradigm for World Action Models (WAMs). However, existing approaches suffer from slow training convergence and limited converged accuracy, particularly at high frame rates, as the training supervision is confined to the current chunk without explicit signals about future dynamics; they also suffer from slow inference due to iterative video denoising. In this paper, we present Next Forcing, a multi-chunk prediction (MCP) framework for causal world modeling that enables faster training, higher accuracy, and accelerated inference. Inspired by multi-token prediction in large language models, Next Forcing introduces an MCP training objective that augments the main model with lightweight auxiliary MCP modules to simultaneously denoise video chunks at multiple future temporal horizons (next$^1$, next$^2$, next$^3$ chunks). These MCP modules form a causal chain across prediction depths, where intermediate features fused from multiple layers of the main model are leveraged to predict future dynamics, allowing near-future predictions to inform farther-future ones and providing dense multi-scale temporal supervision back to the main model. During training, the MCP modules significantly accelerate convergence and improve converged accuracy, especially at high frame rates: at 50 fps, Next Forcing achieves a 93.1% relative improvement over LingBot-VA at 5k training steps and 2.3x faster convergence, and establishes new state-of-the-art results on the RoboTwin benchmark (94.1/93.5% on Clean/Random). At inference, the MCP modules can be retained to predict the next video chunk in parallel with the current one, achieving 2x inference acceleration. Next Forcing also demonstrates significant improvements on PhyWorld, a benchmark evaluating adherence to physical laws in video generation, and over 50% FVD reduction on general video pretraining.
中文标题/摘要
标题：下一代强制：基于多块预测的因果世界建模
自回归视频生成已成为世界动作模型（WAMs）的强大范式。然而，现有方法在高帧率下训练收敛缓慢、最终精度有限，因为训练监督仅限于当前块，缺乏对未来动态的明确信号；同时，由于需要迭代视频去噪，推理速度也较慢。本文提出Next Forcing，一种用于因果世界建模的多块预测（MCP）框架，可实现更快的训练、更高的精度和加速的推理。受大语言模型中多token预测的启发，Next Forcing引入MCP训练目标，通过轻量级辅助MCP模块增强主模型，同时在多个未来时间尺度（next¹、next²、next³块）上去噪视频块。这些MCP模块在预测深度上形成因果链，利用主模型多层融合的中间特征预测未来动态，使近未来预测指导远未来预测，并为主模型提供密集的多尺度时间监督。训练期间，MCP模块显著加速收敛并提升最终精度，尤其在高帧率下：在50 fps时，Next Forcing在5k训练步数下相较LingBot-VA实现93.1%相对提升，收敛速度提升2.3倍，并在RoboTwin基准上创下新SOTA（Clean/Random分别为94.1%/93.5%）。推理时，可保留MCP模块并行预测下一视频块，实现2倍推理加速。Next Forcing还在评估视频生成物理规律遵循度的PhyWorld基准上表现显著提升，并在通用视频预训练中实现超过50%的FVD降低。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the inefficiencies in autoregressive video generation, including slow training, limited accuracy at high frame rates, and sluggish inference, Next Forcing introduces a multi-chunk prediction (MCP) framework that augments the main model with lightweight auxiliary modules to predict video chunks at multiple future temporal horizons simultaneously. These modules form a causal chain using fused intermediate features to enable future dynamics to inform each other and provide dense multi-scale temporal supervision back to the main model. Experimentally, Next Forcing achieves 93.1% relative improvement over LingBot-VA at 5k steps and 2.3x faster convergence at 50 fps, sets new state-of-the-art results on RoboTwin (94.1/93.5% on Clean/Random), and delivers 2x inference speedup by parallelizing next-chunk prediction. It also improves physical consistency on PhyWorld and reduces FVD by over 50% on general video pretraining.
为解决自回归视频生成在世界动作模型中训练缓慢、高帧率下精度不足及推理效率低的问题，Next Forcing提出多块预测框架，通过轻量级辅助模块同时预测未来多个时间步的视频块（next^1、next^2、next^3），形成因果链式结构，利用主模型多层融合特征反向提供密集多尺度时间监督。该方法显著加速训练收敛并提升最终精度，尤其在50fps下相较LingBot-VA提升93.1%相对性能，于RoboTwin基准上刷新SOTA（94.1%/93.5%）。推理阶段保留MCP模块可并行预测下一帧块，实现推理速度翻倍，并在PhyWorld物理一致性任务及通用视频预训练中分别提升50%以上FVD表现。
Going with the Flow: Koopman Behavioral Models as Pseudo Planners for Visuo-Motor Dexterity
Authors: Yunhai Han, Jiaqi Fu, Linhao Bai, Ziyu Xiao, Zhaodong Yang, Yogita Choudhary, Krishna Jha, Chuizheng Kong, Shreyas Kousik, Harish Ravichandar
First: 2026-02-07T07:18:00+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-09T17:59:12+00:00
Comments: Website: https://k-ubm.github.io/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Contemporary visuo-motor dexterity models often rely on expressive policy classes with diffusion and transformer backbones to achieve strong performance. However, these architectures require significant data and computational resources, and remain far from reliable, particularly for multi-fingered dexterity. Importantly, they model skills as reactive mappings and rely on fixed-horizon action chunking, creating a rigid trade-off between temporal coherence and reactivity. To address these issues, we first introduce Unified Behavioral Models (UBMs), a framework to represent dexterous skills as coupled dynamical systems that capture how visual features of the environment (visual flow) and proprioceptive states of the robot (action flow) co-evolve. As such, UBMs ensure temporal coherence by construction rather than heuristic averaging. Unlike world models that attempt to predict the impact of arbitrary robot actions on the environment, UBMs target behavioral dynamics that encode how demonstrated robot behavior is related to desired impacts on the environment. A UBM can be viewed as a pseudo planner: given an initial condition, it computes the desired robot behavior over the entire skill horizon, while simultaneously ``imagining" the resulting flow of visual features. To operationalize UBMs, we propose Koopman-UBM, a first instantiation of UBMs as a structured latent linear system. K-UBM is computationally efficient, enabling reactivity and adaptation via an online replanning strategy: the model acts as its own runtime monitor, automatically triggering replanning when predicted and observed visual flow diverge beyond a threshold. Across seven simulated tasks and four real-world tasks, our approach matches or exceeds the performance of state-of-the-art baselines, while offering considerably faster inference, smooth execution, robustness to occlusions, and flexible replanning.
中文标题/摘要
标题：顺势而为：Koopman行为模型作为视觉-运动灵巧性的伪规划器
当前视觉-运动灵巧性模型常依赖扩散与Transformer架构的表达性策略类以实现强性能。然而，这些架构需大量数据和计算资源，且在多指灵巧性任务中仍不够可靠。更重要的是，它们将技能建模为反应式映射，并依赖固定时域动作分块，导致时序连贯性与反应性之间存在刚性权衡。为解决这些问题，我们首先提出统一行为模型（UBMs），一种将灵巧技能表示为耦合动力系统的框架，捕捉环境视觉特征（视觉流）与机器人本体状态（动作流）如何协同演化。因此，UBMs通过构造而非启发式平均确保时序连贯性。与试图预测任意机器人动作对环境影响的世界模型不同，UBMs聚焦于编码已演示机器人行为与环境期望影响之间关系的行为动力学。UBM可视为伪规划器：给定初始条件，它计算整个技能时域内期望的机器人行为，同时“想象”由此产生的视觉特征流。为实现UBMs，我们提出Koopman-UBM，作为UBMs的首个结构化潜在线性系统实例。K-UBM计算高效，支持通过在线重规划策略实现反应性与自适应：模型作为自身运行时监控器，当预测与观测视觉流偏离阈值时自动触发重规划。在七个仿真任务和四个真实任务中，我们的方法性能匹配或超越现有最先进基线，同时提供更快推理、平滑执行、对遮挡鲁棒性及灵活重规划。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the high resource demands and lack of temporal coherence in current visuo-motor dexterity models, this work introduces Unified Behavioral Models (UBMs) that represent dexterous skills as coupled dynamical systems capturing co-evolution of visual and proprioceptive flows. The method, instantiated as Koopman-UBM, models behavior as a structured latent linear system enabling efficient online replanning triggered by visual flow divergence. Experiments across seven simulated and four real-world tasks show that Koopman-UBM matches or surpasses state-of-the-art baselines while offering faster inference, smoother execution, occlusion robustness, and flexible replanning.
为解决现有视觉-运动灵巧模型资源消耗高、时间一致性差的问题，本文提出统一行为模型（UBM），将灵巧技能建模为视觉流与本体感觉流共同演化的耦合动力系统。其具体实现Koopman-UBM采用结构化潜在线性系统，支持在线重规划，通过监测视觉流偏差自动触发重规划。在七项仿真与四项真实任务中，该方法性能不逊于当前最优基线，同时具备推理更快、执行更平滑、对遮挡鲁棒、重规划更灵活等优势。
AnyMod-LLVE: Low-Light Video Enhancement with Modality-Agnostic Inference
Authors: Hangfeng Liang, Yutao Hu, Yanhan Hu, Xiaohan Wu, Wenqi Shao, Ying Fu
Venue: ICML 2026
First: 2026-06-09T17:59:05+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-09T17:59:05+00:00
Comments: Accepted at ICML 2026; Project page and code: https://lhfgghc.github.io/LLVE-AMNet
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Low-light video enhancement (LLVE) remains a challenging task due to severe information degradation under low-illumination conditions. Recent multimodal approaches have significantly improved enhancement performance by incorporating auxiliary modalities, such as event streams and infrared images. However, these methods typically assume the availability of these modalities at inference, which is often not feasible in real-world scenarios. To solve this problem, in this work, we propose AMNet, a unified multimodal framework for LLVE, to support flexible modality-agnostic inference, where auxiliary modalities may be unavailable. To address the issue of modality absence, we introduce a Spatial-Spectral Dual-Gated Translator that learns the correspondence between auxiliary modalities and RGB inputs, producing implicit auxiliary representations to support the robust enhancement. Additionally, to fully facilitate the learning of cross-modal correspondence, we conduct large-scale multimodal pretraining based on the RGB-only dataset with synthetic auxiliary modalities. Extensive experiments demonstrate that AMNet could handle arbitrary inference-time modality combinations and exhibits superior performance for LLVE under modality absence conditions. Code and models are available on the project page.
中文标题/摘要
标题：AnyMod-LLVE：基于模态无关推理的低光照视频增强
由于低光照条件下信息严重退化，低光照视频增强（LLVE）仍是一项具有挑战性的任务。近期的多模态方法通过引入辅助模态（如事件流和红外图像）显著提升了增强性能。然而，这些方法通常假设推理时这些模态可用，这在现实场景中往往不可行。为解决此问题，本文提出AMNet，一种统一的多模态框架用于LLVE，支持灵活的模态无关推理，即使辅助模态不可用。为应对模态缺失问题，我们引入空间-频谱双门控翻译器，学习辅助模态与RGB输入之间的对应关系，生成隐式辅助表示以支持鲁棒增强。此外，为充分促进跨模态对应关系的学习，我们基于仅RGB数据集并使用合成辅助模态进行大规模多模态预训练。大量实验表明，AMNet可处理任意推理时模态组合，并在模态缺失条件下表现出优越的LLVE性能。代码和模型可在项目页面获取。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the practical limitation that auxiliary modalities like event streams or infrared images are often unavailable during real-time low-light video enhancement, the authors propose AMNet, a modality-agnostic framework that enables flexible inference even when such modalities are missing. The method introduces a Spatial-Spectral Dual-Gated Translator to learn implicit cross-modal representations from RGB inputs alone, and leverages large-scale multimodal pretraining using synthetic auxiliary data to strengthen cross-modal correspondence learning. Experimental results show that AMNet consistently outperforms existing methods across various modality combinations, including full absence of auxiliary modalities, demonstrating robust enhancement under real-world constraints.
为了解决低光照视频增强中辅助模态（如事件流或红外图像）在推理时通常不可用的实际问题，作者提出了AMNet统一多模态框架，支持模态无关推理。该方法引入空间-频谱双门控翻译器，学习跨模态对应关系并生成隐式辅助表示，即使模态缺失也能增强效果；同时通过在合成辅助模态下对RGB数据进行大规模多模态预训练，强化跨模态学习能力。实验表明，AMNet在不同模态组合下表现稳健，尤其在辅助模态缺失时优于现有方法。
TacForeSight: Force-Guided Tactile World Model for Contact-Rich Manipulation
Authors: Yujie Zang, Yuhang Zheng, Xian Nie, Yupeng Zheng, Shuai Tian, Songen Gu, Chen Gao, Zining Wang, Shuicheng Yan, Wenchao Ding
First: 2026-06-09T17:59:03+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-09T17:59:03+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Contact-rich manipulation requires robots to continuously perceive and regulate evolving physical interactions under dynamic contact transitions or complex surface geometries. Recent imitation learning methods improve contact-aware control by incorporating tactile or force feedback, but they rarely model the asymmetric spatiotemporal roles of global force and local tactile sensing. To address this, we propose TacForeSight, a lightweight force-conditioned tactile foresight framework for real-time manipulation. The core component is TacForceWM, a tactile world model that predicts short-horizon tactile latent dynamics from dual-finger tactile observations conditioned on high-frequency wrist force and torque signals. Another key component, the Predictive Tactile-Conditioned Policy, leverages the predicted latents as anticipatory contact priors, models the current-to-future tactile evolution via cross-attention, and adaptively fuses visuo-tactile features through a tactile-guided gating module. By forecasting purely within a compact latent space, TacForeSight enables proactive contact reasoning with efficient real-time inference suitable for high-frequency manipulation control. Real-robot experiments on five representative tasks and three in-process perturbation settings show that TacForeSight consistently outperforms existing baselines, particularly under dynamic contact disturbances. All models and datasets will be made publicly available on the project website at https://tacforesight.github.io/ProjectPage.
中文标题/摘要
标题：TacForeSight：面向接触密集操作的力引导触觉世界模型
接触密集操作要求机器人在动态接触转换或复杂表面几何下持续感知并调节不断演化的物理交互。近期的模仿学习方法通过整合触觉或力反馈提升接触感知控制，但很少建模全局力与局部触觉感知在时空上的非对称角色。为此，我们提出TacForeSight，一种轻量级的力条件触觉前瞻框架，用于实时操作。核心组件TacForceWM是一种触觉世界模型，它根据高频手腕力与扭矩信号，从双指触觉观测中预测短时程触觉潜在动态。另一关键组件预测性触觉条件策略，利用预测潜变量作为前瞻接触先验，通过交叉注意力建模当前至未来触觉演化，并通过触觉引导门控模块自适应融合视觉-触觉特征。通过在紧凑潜在空间内预测，TacForeSight实现高效实时推理，支持高频操作控制中的主动接触推理。在五个代表性任务及三种过程扰动设置下的真实机器人实验表明，TacForeSight持续优于现有基线方法，尤其在动态接触扰动下表现突出。所有模型与数据集将在项目网站https://tacforesight.github.io/ProjectPage/公开。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for robots to handle complex physical interactions involving frequent contact transitions, this work introduces TacForeSight, a force-guided tactile foresight framework. It employs TacForceWM, a tactile world model that predicts short-term tactile latent dynamics from dual-finger tactile data, conditioned on high-frequency wrist force and torque. A predictive policy then uses these forecasts as contact priors, models tactile evolution via cross-attention, and fuses visuo-tactile features through a tactile-guided gating mechanism. Experiments on five manipulation tasks with three perturbation scenarios show TacForeSight consistently outperforms baselines, especially under dynamic contact disturbances, demonstrating robust real-time performance.
为使机器人在动态接触环境下完成复杂触觉操作，TacForeSight提出一种基于力反馈的触觉前瞻框架，建模全局力与局部触觉感知的非对称时空角色。其核心模块TacForceWM从双指触觉数据和高频腕部力矩信号中预测短期触觉潜在动态。预测策略利用该动态作为接触先验，通过交叉注意力建模触觉演化，并通过触觉引导门控模块融合视觉与触觉特征。在五个任务及三种扰动场景的实验中，该方法显著优于现有基线，尤其在动态接触干扰下表现更优，验证了其实时性与鲁棒性。
Evaluation Cards: An Interpretive Layer for AI Evaluation Reporting
Authors: Avijit Ghosh, Anka Reuel, Jenny Chim, Wm. Matthew Kennedy, Srishti Yadav, Jennifer Mickel, Yanan Long, Andrew Tran, Anastassia Kornilova, Damian Stachura, Kevin Klyman, Felix Friedrich, Jeba Sania, Jan Batzner, Anoop Mishra, Eliya Habba, Yixiong Hao, Nathan Heath, Shalaleh Rismani, Usman Gohar, Andrea Loehr, David Manheim, Ruchira Dhar, Sree Harsha Nelaturu, Aarush Sinha, Leshem Choshen, Drishti Sharma, Ishan Khire, Amit Saha, Subramanyam Sahoo, Michael Hardy, Michael Alexander Riegler, Kabir Manghnani, Michelle Lin, Yanan Jiang, Yilin Huang, Asaf Yehudai, Jessica Ji, Aris Hofmann, Mubashara Akhtar, Max Lamparth, Nuno Moniz, Yacine Jernite, Stella Biderman, Zeerak Talat, Sanmi Koyejo, Mykel Kochenderfer, Irene Solaiman
First: 2026-06-08T17:55:02+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-09T17:58:20+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
AI evaluation results are produced at scale but reported inconsistently across leaderboards, model cards, benchmark papers, and company blogs. The cost is interpretive: readers cannot reliably compare results across sources, identify what a report omits, or trace an aggregate claim to its underlying evidence. Recent efforts address isolated components but leave three gaps: they cover only narrow slices of the evaluation lifecycle and do not compose into a single interpretable record; they specify static representations that do not differentiate the questions different stakeholders bring to the same evidence; and they remain proposals on paper, lacking the extraction infrastructure required for adoption at scale. We present \EvalCards{}, an operational reporting layer that composes benchmark metadata, evaluation run data, and model metadata into a unified record. We (1) derive a reporting schema from a structured review of 52 papers and 10 stakeholder interviews, (2) implement four interpretive signals (reproducibility, documentation completeness, provenance and risk, and score comparability), rendered through reader modes calibrated to research and non-research audiences, and (3) deploy a monitoring tool that applies \EvalCards{} across 5,816 models, 635 benchmarks, and 101,843 results, surfacing systematic gaps in current reporting practice.
中文标题/摘要
标题：评估卡片：AI评估报告的解释性层
AI评估结果虽大规模生成，但在排行榜、模型卡片、基准论文和公司博客中报告方式不一致。其代价是解释性缺失：读者无法可靠比较不同来源的结果，无法识别报告遗漏的内容，也无法追溯综合声明背后的证据。近期努力仅解决孤立组件，留下三个缺口：仅覆盖评估生命周期的狭窄部分，无法组合成单一可解释记录；仅指定静态表示，未区分不同利益相关者对同一证据提出的问题；仍停留在纸面提案，缺乏规模化采用所需的提取基础设施。我们提出EvalCards，一个可操作的报告层，将基准元数据、评估运行数据和模型元数据整合为统一记录。我们（1）从52篇论文和10次利益相关者访谈的结构化审查中推导出报告模式；（2）实现四种解释性信号（可复现性、文档完整性、溯源与风险、分数可比性），通过针对研究与非研究受众校准的读者模式呈现；（3）部署监控工具，将EvalCards应用于5,816个模型、635个基准和101,843个结果，揭示当前报告实践中的系统性缺陷。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the inconsistent reporting of AI evaluation results across platforms, the authors introduce EvalCards, an operational layer that unifies benchmark metadata, evaluation runs, and model metadata into a single interpretable record. They derive a reporting schema from 52 papers and 10 stakeholder interviews, implement four interpretive signals—reproducibility, documentation completeness, provenance and risk, and score comparability—tailored to different reader audiences, and deploy a monitoring tool across 5,816 models, 635 benchmarks, and 101,843 results, revealing systematic gaps in current reporting practices.
为解决AI评估结果在不同平台间报告不一致的问题，作者提出了EvalCards，一个统一的解释性层，将基准元数据、评估运行数据和模型元数据整合为单一可报告记录。他们从52篇论文和10次利益相关者访谈中提炼出报告结构，实现了四个解释性信号——可复现性、文档完整性、溯源与风险、分数可比性，并针对不同读者群体进行适配，最终在5816个模型、635个基准和101843个结果上部署监控工具，揭示了当前评估报告中存在的系统性缺失。
EEVEE: Towards Test-time Prompt Learning in the Real World for Self-Improving Agents
Authors: Weixian Xu, Shilong Liu, Mengdi Wang
First: 2026-06-09T17:57:16+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-09T17:57:16+00:00
Comments: 19 pages, 6 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
In this paper, we propose EEVEE, the first multi-dataset test-time prompt learning framework for LLM agents, enabling test-time prompt learning under real-world task streams. Existing methods are largely designed for single-dataset settings, while real-world applications require models to handle heterogeneous input streams drawn from multiple datasets, domains, and task distributions, limiting their practical applicability. To mitigate cross-dataset interference, EEVEE introduces a router that partitions incoming inputs into task clusters and assigns them to suitable prompt configurations. This design is optimized via a router-prompt co-evolution strategy, which employs interleaved router and prompt learning phases to address their mutual dependency. Experiments across multiple datasets demonstrate that the framework improves robustness under heterogeneous data streams while maintaining single-benchmark learning capability and efficiency. Specifically, EEVEE improves average multi-benchmark scores by 10.38 and 24.32 points over Qwen3-4B-Instruct and DeepSeek-V3.2, surpassing SOTA methods GEPA and ACE by up to 37.2% and 48.2%.
中文标题/摘要
标题：EEVEE：面向真实世界自改进代理的测试时提示学习框架
本文提出EEVEE，这是首个面向多数据集的测试时提示学习框架，适用于LLM代理在真实世界任务流中的测试时提示学习。现有方法大多针对单数据集设计，而真实世界应用要求模型处理来自多个数据集、领域和任务分布的异构输入流，限制了其实际适用性。为减轻跨数据集干扰，EEVEE引入路由器，将输入划分为任务簇并分配至合适的提示配置。该设计通过路由器-提示协同进化策略优化，采用交替学习路由器与提示的阶段以解决二者相互依赖问题。多数据集实验表明，该框架在异构数据流下提升鲁棒性，同时保持单基准学习能力与效率。具体而言，EEVEE在Qwen3-4B-Instruct和DeepSeek-V3.2上分别提升平均多基准得分10.38和24.32分，超越当前最优方法GEPA和ACE高达37.2%和48.2%。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for LLM agents to adapt to real-world heterogeneous task streams across multiple datasets, EEVEE introduces a test-time prompt learning framework designed for multi-dataset environments. It employs a router to cluster incoming inputs and assign them to appropriate prompt configurations, with a co-evolution strategy that interleaves router and prompt learning to resolve their mutual dependency. Experimental results across multiple benchmarks show EEVEE significantly improves robustness under diverse data streams while preserving single-benchmark performance, achieving average score improvements of 10.38 and 24.32 points over Qwen3-4B-Instruct and DeepSeek-V3.2, and outperforming state-of-the-art methods GEPA and ACE by up to 37.2% and 48.2%.
为应对现实世界中多数据集、异构任务流的挑战，EEVEE提出首个支持多数据集测试时提示学习的框架。该框架通过路由器对输入进行聚类并分配适配的提示配置，减少跨数据集干扰，同时采用路由器与提示的协同进化策略解决二者依赖问题。实验表明，EEVEE在多个基准上显著优于现有方法，相较Qwen3-4B-Instruct和DeepSeek-V3.2平均得分提升10.38和24.32分，超越SOTA方法GEPA和ACE最高达37.2%和48.2%。
Lip Forcing: Few-Step Autoregressive Diffusion for Real-time Lip Synchronization
Authors: Paul Hyunbin Cho, Jinhyuk Jang, SeokYoung Lee, Joungbin Lee, Siyoon Jin, Heeseong Shin, Jung Yi, Yunjin Park, Chulmin Park, Seungryong Kim
First: 2026-06-09T17:56:36+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-09T17:56:36+00:00
Comments: Project Page: https://cvlab-kaist.github.io/LipForcing/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Diffusion-based lip synchronization models achieve strong visual quality and audio-visual alignment, but full-sequence bidirectional attention and many denoising steps make them impractical for real-time inference. We present Lip Forcing, to our knowledge the first autoregressive diffusion method for video-to-video (V2V) lip synchronization, which distills a 14B audio-conditioned bidirectional video diffusion teacher into causal students. At inference, the students generate each chunk in only two denoising steps without inference-time CFG, enabling real-time lip synchronization. A lip-sync-specific teacher-trajectory analysis reveals a CFG fidelity-sync tradeoff: no-CFG predictions favor reference fidelity, whereas CFG-guided predictions favor synchronization within a mid-trajectory band. Lip Forcing translates this finding into three analysis-derived components: Sync-Window DMD, a two-step inference schedule, and a SyncNet-based reward. We validate Lip Forcing at two student scales, both distilled from the 14B teacher. The 1.3B student crosses into real-time streaming at 31 FPS, $17.6\times$ faster than its same-scale bidirectional model. The 14B student, the largest diffusion model reported for V2V lip synchronization, runs $39.8\times$ faster than its teacher at comparable reference fidelity. Time-to-first-frame is sub-millisecond at both scales, far below every diffusion baseline.
中文标题/摘要
标题：唇部强制：用于实时唇同步的少步自回归扩散模型
基于扩散的唇同步模型在视觉质量和视听对齐方面表现优异，但双向注意力机制和大量去噪步骤使其难以满足实时推理需求。我们提出Lip Forcing，这是首个面向视频到视频（V2V）唇同步的自回归扩散方法，将一个140亿参数的音频条件双向视频扩散教师模型蒸馏为因果结构的学生模型。推理时，学生模型仅需两步去噪即可生成每个片段，且无需推理时CFG，从而实现实时唇同步。通过针对唇同步的教师轨迹分析，我们发现CFG存在保真度与同步性之间的权衡：无CFG预测更注重参考帧保真度，而CFG引导预测则更倾向于中段轨迹带内的同步性。Lip Forcing将这一发现转化为三个分析衍生组件：同步窗口DMD、两步推理调度和基于SyncNet的奖励函数。我们在两个学生规模上验证了Lip Forcing，均蒸馏自140亿参数教师模型。13亿参数学生模型在31 FPS下实现实时流式传输，比同规模双向模型快17.6倍。140亿参数学生模型是目前报道的用于V2V唇同步的最大扩散模型，在保持相近参考帧保真度的前提下，比教师模型快39.8倍。两个规模的首帧响应时间均低于毫秒级，远优于所有扩散基线模型。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for real-time lip synchronization without sacrificing visual quality, the authors introduce Lip Forcing, an autoregressive diffusion method that distills a 14B audio-conditioned bidirectional video diffusion model into causal student models. These students generate each video chunk in just two denoising steps without using inference-time CFG, enabling real-time performance. Experimental results show that the 1.3B student achieves 31 FPS, 17.6x faster than its bidirectional counterpart, while the 14B student runs 39.8x faster than the teacher at comparable fidelity, with sub-millisecond time-to-first-frame at both scales.
为实现实时唇形同步而不牺牲视觉质量，作者提出Lip Forcing方法，将一个14B参数的音频条件双向视频扩散模型蒸馏为因果学生模型。这些学生模型在推理时仅需两步去噪即可生成每个视频片段，无需条件引导，从而实现实时性能。实验表明，1.3B学生模型达到31帧/秒，比同类双向模型快17.6倍；14B学生模型比教师模型快39.8倍，且保持相似参考保真度，两者首帧响应时间均低于毫秒级。
Data Journalist Agent: Transforming Data into Verifiable Multimodal Stories
Authors: Kevin Qinghong Lin, Batu EI, Yuhong Shi, Pan Lu, Philip Torr, James Zou
First: 2026-06-09T17:51:55+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-09T17:51:55+00:00
Comments: Project page: https://data2story.github.io Github: https://github.com/QinghongLin/data2story-skill
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3 · Project1
Abstract
Data tells stories that shape society; the data journalist's job is to turn raw information into stories non-experts can trust. A high-quality news feature takes a newsroom team weeks: hunting for context, running statistics, choosing an angle, and designing visuals. Recent agents handle individual steps well: data-science agents close the analysis loop, while design agents synthesize beautiful websites. But can an agent serve as a data journalist end to end? We introduce Data Journalist Agent (Data2Story), a multi-agent framework that orchestrates specialized roles into a single virtual newsroom. Data2Story contributes two innovations. (i) Claims are evidence-grounded: an Inspector links every number, angle, and asset back to data, code, or an external reference. (ii) Articles are multimodally generative: rather than defaulting to plain text and static charts, Data2Story reasons about what readers will want to see, then deploys multimodal tools, such as interactive maps for geography and audio for music. We evaluate Data2Story on 18 articles, each paired with the originally published expert piece, along four axes: (a) human-agent angle coverage; (b) rubric evaluation with 53 participants across five dimensions; (c) computer-use agents as judges, a cost-saving proxy for how readers navigate interactive articles; and (d) verifiability, where a coding verifier re-executes statements against the data and checks claims against references. Data2Story produces competitive, evidence-traceable multimedia stories, with particular strength in transparency and auditability. Human articles retain an edge in editorial angle, creative design, and presentation. We position Data2Story as a collaborator for journalists, enabling more evidence-based, transparent, and verifiable reporting. Code and demos are available at https://data2story.github.io.
中文标题/摘要
标题：数据记者代理：将数据转化为可验证的多模态故事
数据讲述塑造社会的故事；数据记者的任务是将原始信息转化为非专业人士可信赖的故事。一篇高质量的新闻特写通常需要新闻团队数周时间：寻找背景、运行统计、选择角度、设计视觉。近期的代理工具能很好地处理单个步骤：数据科学代理完成分析闭环，设计代理合成美观的网站。但能否有一个代理全程扮演数据记者？我们引入了数据记者代理（Data2Story），一个多代理框架，将专门角色编排成一个虚拟新闻室。Data2Story贡献两项创新：(i) 声明基于证据：检查员将每个数字、角度和资产链接回数据、代码或外部参考；(ii) 文章多模态生成：而非默认为纯文本和静态图表，Data2Story会推理读者希望看到什么，再部署多模态工具，如地理交互地图和音乐音频。我们在18篇文章上评估Data2Story，每篇均配对原专家作品，从四个维度评估：(a) 人机角度覆盖；(b) 53名参与者在五个维度的评分；(c) 计算机代理作为裁判，作为读者导航交互文章的成本节约代理；(d) 可验证性，由编码验证器重新执行陈述并核对声明与参考。Data2Story生成具有竞争力、可追溯证据的多媒体故事，尤其在透明度和可审计性方面表现突出。人类文章在编辑角度、创意设计和呈现方面仍具优势。我们将Data2Story定位为记者的协作伙伴，助力更基于证据、透明和可验证的报道。代码和演示可在 https://data2story.github.io 获取。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to automate high-quality, verifiable data journalism, the authors introduce Data Journalist Agent (Data2Story), a multi-agent system that orchestrates specialized roles to produce end-to-end multimedia news stories. The method integrates an Inspector agent that grounds every claim in data or references, and multimodal generation tools that select interactive visuals like maps or audio based on reader intent. Evaluated across 18 articles against expert-produced pieces, Data2Story achieved competitive results in verifiability, transparency, and auditability, as measured by human rubric scoring, computer-use agent navigation, and automated claim verification. While human-written articles still outperformed in editorial angle and design, Data2Story demonstrated strong potential as a journalistic collaborator for evidence-based reporting.
为实现高质量、可验证的数据新闻自动化，作者提出了数据记者代理（Data2Story），一个协调多个专业角色生成端到端多媒体新闻故事的多代理框架。系统通过“检查员”确保每个论断都可追溯至数据、代码或外部参考，并根据读者需求生成交互式地图、音频等多模态内容。在18篇文章的评估中，Data2Story在可验证性、透明度和可审计性方面表现优异，但在编辑角度与创意设计上仍不及人类记者。该系统定位为记者的协作工具，旨在提升报道的严谨性，而非替代人类判断。
The Role of Feedback Alignment in Self-Distillation
Authors: Semih Kara, Oğuzhan Ersoy
Venue: ICML 2026
First: 2026-06-09T17:50:09+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-09T17:50:09+00:00
Comments: Accepted to the ICML 2026 Workshop on RL from World Feedback (RLxF)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Conditioning a language model on additional context, such as feedback on a previous attempt, typically improves its response. Self-distillation trains the model to retain this improvement when the context is not present. The method works by matching the model's output distribution under two settings: a student that sees only the question, and a self-teacher that also sees the context. What the model learns therefore depends on what context the self-teacher receives, yet the design of this context remains largely unexplored. We study context design for self-distillation by training a solver on feedback from a frozen critic. We compare three conditions: (i) a binary reward (GRPO), (ii) the reference solution, and (iii) a step-by-step critique aligned to the solver's reasoning trace. Step-aligned critique yields the largest gains, outperforming GRPO by 16.11 points and reference-solution-conditioned self-distillation by 5.27 points (Avg@12). Per-token advantage analysis reveals why: step-aligned feedback targets only the tokens where reasoning fails, leaving correct behavior intact. Conditioning on the reference solution, by contrast, pressures the model to change its behavior at every token (even correct steps) because an alternative derivation inevitably differs in phrasing and approach. This suggests that structural alignment between feedback and the solver's reasoning is a key driver of self-distillation effectiveness.
中文标题/摘要
标题：反馈对齐在自蒸馏中的作用
在语言模型中加入额外上下文（如对先前尝试的反馈）通常能提升其响应质量。自蒸馏训练模型在无上下文时仍能保留这种改进。该方法通过匹配两种情境下模型的输出分布实现：一种是仅看到问题的学生模型，另一种是同时看到上下文的自教师模型。模型所学内容取决于自教师所接收的上下文，但该上下文的设计尚未被充分探索。我们通过训练一个求解器，使其从冻结的批评者获得反馈，研究自蒸馏的上下文设计。比较三种条件：(i) 二元奖励（GRPO），(ii) 参考解，(iii) 与求解器推理轨迹对齐的逐步批评。逐步对齐的批评效果最佳，比GRPO高出16.11分，比参考解条件下的自蒸馏高出5.27分（Avg@12）。按词优势分析揭示了原因：逐步对齐的反馈仅针对推理失败的词，保留正确行为。相比之下，参考解条件迫使模型在每个词上都改变行为（即使正确步骤），因为任何替代推导必然在措辞和方法上有所不同。这表明，反馈与求解器推理之间的结构对齐是自蒸馏有效性的关键驱动因素。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to improve language models through self-distillation—where models retain performance gains from contextual feedback even without it—the paper investigates how feedback design affects learning. It trains a solver using feedback from a frozen critic under three conditions: binary reward, reference solution, and step-aligned critique matching the solver’s reasoning trace. Results show step-aligned critique delivers the largest improvement, outperforming binary reward by 16.11 points and reference solution conditioning by 5.27 points on Avg@12. Per-token analysis reveals that step-aligned feedback selectively corrects flawed reasoning without disrupting correct behavior, whereas reference solution conditioning forces uniform rephrasing across all tokens, including correct ones.
为提升语言模型在无上下文时仍能保留反馈带来的性能增益，该研究探讨了反馈结构对自蒸馏效果的影响。实验在三种反馈条件下训练求解器：二元奖励、参考解和与求解器推理轨迹对齐的分步批评。结果表明，分步对齐批评效果最佳，比二元奖励高出16.11分、比参考解条件蒸馏高出5.27分（Avg@12）。逐词分析显示，分步批评仅修正推理错误部分，保留正确行为；而参考解条件则迫使模型在所有词位上调整行为，包括正确步骤，因替代推导必然在表述与方法上不同。
Predicting Future Behaviors in Reasoning Models Enables Better Steering
Authors: Evgenii Kortukov, Piotr Komorowski, Florian Klein, Paula Engl, Gabriele Sarti, Seong Joon Oh, Sebastian Lapuschkin, Wojciech Samek
First: 2026-06-09T17:49:24+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-09T17:49:24+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Deployed large reasoning models (LRMs) often behave unexpectedly. Test-time steering controls LRM outputs by intervening on their hidden representations, but it can degrade output quality. We argue that prior steering work implicitly relies on internal features that detect behavior in already generated text. We show that these detection features are poor predictors of future behavioral outcomes, and thus not the natural intervention target. Instead, we train activation probes to predict future behavior likelihoods from intermediate reasoning steps. These probes predict the most likely behavior with 64%-91% accuracy, revealing a separate type of internal prediction features. Building on these prediction features, we introduce a text-level steering method, Future Probe Controlled Generation. FPCG samples multiple candidate sentences and chooses the best one according to a probe predicting the future behavior likelihood. This enables steering with almost no output quality degradation. FPCG also enables steering in several evaluations where activation steering fails. These results show that distinguishing detection and prediction features enables a more nuanced approach to controlling LRM behaviors.
中文标题/摘要
标题：在推理模型中预测未来行为可实现更优的引导
部署的大规模推理模型（LRMs）常表现出意外行为。测试时引导通过干预其隐藏表示来控制LRM输出，但可能降低输出质量。我们认为，先前的引导工作隐式依赖于能检测已生成文本行为的内部特征。我们表明，这些检测特征对预测未来行为结果效果不佳，因此并非自然的干预目标。相反，我们训练激活探针，从中间推理步骤预测未来行为可能性。这些探针以64%-91%的准确率预测最可能的行为，揭示了一种新的内部预测特征。基于这些预测特征，我们提出一种文本级引导方法——未来探针控制生成（FPCG）。FPCG采样多个候选句子，并根据预测未来行为可能性的探针选择最优句，从而实现几乎无输出质量损失的引导。FPCG还在激活引导失败的多个评估中实现引导。这些结果表明，区分检测与预测特征可实现对LRM行为更精细的控制。
Summary / 总结
Large reasoning models often behave unpredictably, prompting the need for better steering methods. The authors argue that existing steering techniques rely on detection features that poorly predict future behavior, making them suboptimal intervention targets. Instead, they train activation probes to predict future behavioral likelihoods from intermediate reasoning steps, achieving 64%-91% accuracy. Using these prediction features, they introduce Future Probe Controlled Generation, a text-level steering method that samples multiple sentence candidates and selects the best one based on predicted future behavior, enabling effective steering without significant output quality loss and outperforming activation steering in several evaluations.
大型推理模型常表现出不可预测的行为，现有通过干预隐藏表示进行的测试时引导方法常导致输出质量下降。作者认为当前方法依赖的检测特征无法有效预测未来行为，不适合作为干预目标。他们训练激活探针从中间推理步骤预测未来行为可能性，准确率可达64%-91%。基于这些预测特征，提出Future Probe Controlled Generation方法，通过采样多个句子候选并依据探针预测的未来行为选择最优句，实现几乎无质量损失的引导，并在多个评估中优于激活引导方法。
Algorithmic and Minimax Complexities in Kernel Bandits
Authors: Yunbei Xu
First: 2026-06-09T17:49:09+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-09T17:49:09+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Gaussian-process upper confidence bound (GP-UCB) and decision-estimation-coefficient (DEC) methods may appear, at first sight, to belong to different theories. This paper places the two viewpoints in a common algorithmic-information language for frequentist RKHS bandits. GP-UCB fixes an algorithmic, rather than true, Gaussian-process prior and exploits realized-trajectory complexity together with computational tractability, whereas MAMS optimizes a robust class-wide MAIR/DEC envelope. Through the unified MAIR framework and heterogeneous positive-semidefinite algorithmic priors, we generalize both the GP-UCB analysis and the MAMS algorithm, propose a safeguarded master that combines their advantages, and provide a kernel-bandit construction showing that algorithmic complexity can be more informative than class-wide minimax or DEC certificates in overparameterized models. The resulting message is that algorithmic information and class-wide minimax coefficients answer different questions and can lead to different gaps; kernel bandits provide a clean setting in which this distinction becomes mathematically visible.
中文标题/摘要
标题：核带宽中的算法与最小最大复杂度
高斯过程置信上界（GP-UCB）与决策估计系数（DEC）方法初看似乎属于不同理论。本文将二者置于频率主义RKHS带宽的统一算法-信息语言中。GP-UCB设定的是算法而非真实高斯过程先验，并结合实现轨迹复杂度与计算可行性；而MAMS则优化一个鲁棒的类宽MAIR/DEC包络。通过统一的MAIR框架与异质正半定算法先验，我们推广了GP-UCB分析与MAMS算法，提出一种结合二者优势的保护性主算法，并提供一个核带宽构造，表明在过参数化模型中，算法复杂度比类宽最小最大或DEC证书更具信息量。其核心信息是：算法信息与类宽最小最大系数回答不同问题，可能导致不同间隙；核带宽提供了一个清晰的场景，使这一区别在数学上显而易见。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to unify disparate theoretical frameworks in kernel bandits, this paper bridges the algorithmic perspective of GP-UCB and the minimax/DEC viewpoint via the MAIR framework, introducing heterogeneous positive-semidefinite priors. It generalizes both methods, proposes a safeguarded master algorithm that combines their strengths, and demonstrates through kernel-bandit experiments that algorithmic complexity can yield tighter regret bounds than class-wide minimax or DEC certificates, especially in overparameterized settings. The key finding is that algorithmic information and minimax coefficients address distinct optimization questions and produce different regret gaps, with kernel bandits offering a clear mathematical lens to reveal this distinction.
为统一核带宽中看似不同的理论框架，本文在算法信息视角下将高斯过程上置信界（GP-UCB）与决策估计系数（DEC）方法整合。通过MAIR框架与异质正定算法先验，提出一种结合GP-UCB轨迹复杂度利用与MAMS鲁棒性的保护型主算法。实验表明，在过参数化核带宽场景中，算法复杂度可比全局最小最大或DEC证书提供更紧的遗憾界，揭示两类范式回答不同问题并产生不同性能差距。
V-REX: Benchmarking Exploratory Visual Reasoning via Chain-of-Questions
Authors: Chenrui Fan, Yijun Liang, Shweta Bhardwaj, Kwesi Cobbina, Ming Li, Tianyi Zhou
First: 2025-12-12T19:18:41+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-09T17:48:42+00:00
Comments: 28 pages
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
While many vision-language models (VLMs) are developed to answer well-defined, straightforward questions with highly specified targets, as in most benchmarks, they often struggle in practice with complex open-ended tasks, which usually require multiple rounds of exploration and reasoning in the visual space. Such visual thinking paths not only provide step-by-step exploration and verification as an AI detective but also produce better interpretations of the final answers. However, these paths are challenging to evaluate due to the large exploration space of intermediate steps. To bridge the gap, we develop an evaluation suite, ``Visual Reasoning with multi-step EXploration (V-REX)'', which is composed of a benchmark of challenging visual reasoning tasks requiring native multi-step exploration and an evaluation protocol. V-REX covers rich application scenarios across diverse domains. V-REX casts the multi-step exploratory reasoning into a Chain-of-Questions (CoQ) and disentangles VLMs' capability to (1) Planning: breaking down an open-ended task by selecting a chain of exploratory questions; and (2) Following: answering curated CoQ sequentially to collect information for deriving the final answer. By curating finite options of questions and answers per step, V-REX achieves a reliable quantitative and fine-grained analysis of the intermediate steps. By assessing SOTA proprietary and open-sourced VLMs, we reveal consistent scaling trends, significant differences between planning and following abilities, and substantial room for improvement in multi-step exploratory reasoning.
中文标题/摘要
标题：V-REX：通过问题链基准测试探索性视觉推理
尽管许多视觉-语言模型（VLMs）旨在回答定义明确、目标高度具体的问答，如大多数基准测试中所示，但在实践中它们往往难以应对复杂的开放性任务，这类任务通常需要在视觉空间中进行多轮探索和推理。此类视觉思维路径不仅像AI侦探一样提供逐步探索与验证，还能生成对最终答案更优的解释。然而，由于中间步骤的探索空间庞大，这些路径难以评估。为弥合这一差距，我们开发了评估套件“多步探索视觉推理（V-REX）”，包含一组需要原生多步探索的挑战性视觉推理任务基准及评估协议。V-REX覆盖了跨多个领域的丰富应用场景。V-REX将多步探索性推理转化为问题链（CoQ），并解耦VLMs的能力：（1）规划：通过选择探索性问题链将开放性任务分解；（2）执行：按顺序回答精选的CoQ以收集信息并推导最终答案。通过每步限定有限的问题与答案选项，V-REX实现了对中间步骤的可靠量化与细粒度分析。通过对当前最先进专有及开源VLMs的评估，我们揭示了其规模趋势的一致性、规划与执行能力的显著差异，以及多步探索性推理仍有巨大提升空间。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the gap between VLMs' performance on well-defined benchmarks and their struggles with complex, open-ended visual reasoning tasks requiring multi-step exploration, the authors introduce V-REX, a benchmark and evaluation protocol that formalizes such reasoning as a Chain-of-Questions (CoQ). The method disentangles two key abilities: planning (selecting a sequence of exploratory questions) and following (sequentially answering them to derive a final answer). By constraining question and answer options at each step, V-REX enables fine-grained, quantitative evaluation of intermediate reasoning steps. Experimental results across SOTA VLMs reveal consistent scaling trends, significant disparities between planning and following capabilities, and substantial room for improvement in multi-step visual reasoning.
为弥补视觉语言模型在结构化基准上表现良好但难以应对复杂开放性视觉推理任务的不足，作者提出V-REX评估框架，将多步探索性推理形式化为“问题链”（CoQ）结构，从而解耦出“规划”（选择探索性问题）与“执行”（按顺序回答问题以推导答案）两大能力。通过限制每步的问题与答案选项，V-REX实现了对中间推理步骤的精细量化评估。在主流视觉语言模型上的实验表明，模型能力随规模呈现一致增长趋势，规划与执行能力存在显著差异，且多步探索性推理仍有较大提升空间。
Piper: A Programmable Distributed Training System
Authors: Megan Frisella, Shubham Tiwari, Andy Ruan, Yi Pan, Parker Gustafson, Mat Jacob, Gilbert Bernstein, Stephanie Wang
First: 2026-06-09T17:48:41+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-09T17:48:41+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large-scale model training increasingly relies on composing multiple parallelism strategies, such as data, pipeline, and expert parallelism, together with memory-saving optimizations like ZeRO. Deployed systems for foundation model pretraining often rely on human experts to manually design a high-level parallelism strategy then implement the corresponding low-level execution strategy, making it difficult to adapt the system to new strategies. Meanwhile, many general-purpose frameworks are more flexible but their implementations are still tied to a fixed set of common parallelism strategies, making it challenging to integrate state-of-the-art strategies. We present Piper, a user-controllable distributed training system that decouples the strategy from the runtime implementation. Piper allows users to declare a comprehensive distributed training strategy with a small set of model annotations and scheduling directives. Each directive applies a transformation on Piper's intermediate representation (IR), a unified global training DAG that represents all computation and communication. Using this IR, Piper compiles per-device execution plans and executes them with a distributed runtime agnostic to the strategy. We show that the combined system maintains performance parity on commonly available strategies such as ZeRO, while also enabling additional performance and memory efficiency gains through joint scheduling of compute and communication in composed parallelism strategies such as DeepSeek-V3's DualPipe.
中文标题/摘要
标题：Piper：一个可编程的分布式训练系统
大规模模型训练日益依赖组合多种并行策略（如数据、流水线和专家并行），以及内存优化技术（如ZeRO）。现有基础模型预训练系统通常依赖人工专家手动设计高层并行策略，再实现对应的底层执行策略，难以适应新策略。同时，许多通用框架虽更灵活，但其实现仍受限于固定并行策略集，难以集成最新策略。 我们提出Piper，一个用户可控的分布式训练系统，将策略与运行时实现解耦。用户仅需少量模型注解和调度指令即可声明完整的分布式训练策略。每条指令对Piper的中间表示（IR）进行变换，IR是统一的全局训练DAG，表示所有计算与通信。基于此IR，Piper编译每设备执行计划，并通过与策略无关的分布式运行时执行。我们证明该系统在常见策略（如ZeRO）上保持性能持平，同时通过组合并行策略（如DeepSeek-V3的DualPipe）中计算与通信联合调度，实现额外的性能与内存效率提升。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the difficulty of adapting distributed training systems to new parallelism strategies and the rigidity of general-purpose frameworks, Piper introduces a user-controllable system that decouples strategy design from runtime implementation. Users define strategies via model annotations and scheduling directives that transform Piper’s intermediate representation — a unified global training DAG — enabling flexible composition of parallelism techniques like data, pipeline, and expert parallelism. Piper then compiles device-specific execution plans and runs them via a strategy-agnostic runtime. Experiments show Piper matches or exceeds performance of established methods like ZeRO while achieving additional gains in memory efficiency and throughput through optimized joint scheduling in complex strategies such as DeepSeek-V3’s DualPipe.
为了解决分布式训练系统难以适应新并行策略、通用框架又受限于固定策略的问题，Piper提出一种用户可控的系统，将策略设计与运行时实现解耦。用户通过模型注解和调度指令修改Piper的统一全局训练DAG中间表示，从而编译出设备级执行计划并由策略无关的运行时执行。实验表明，Piper在ZeRO等常见策略上保持性能持平，同时在如DeepSeek-V3的DualPipe等组合策略中实现更优的计算与通信联合调度，提升效率。
Multi-Faceted Interactivity Alignment in Full-Duplex Speech Models
Authors: Atsumoto Ohashi, Neil Zeghidour, Alexandre Défossez, Eugene Kharitonov
First: 2026-06-09T17:46:55+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-09T17:46:55+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Full-duplex spoken dialogue models can listen and speak simultaneously, making them a promising architecture for natural conversation. However, current models are trained solely with supervised learning through token-level likelihood maximization, which does not directly optimize interaction-level behaviors, causing interactivity issues such as excessive silence and ill-timed turn-taking. Recent work has applied reinforcement learning (RL) to improve interactivity, but existing methods address only a limited set of interactive behaviors in their rewards. In this work, we propose a post-training alignment method that comprehensively improves the interactivity of full-duplex spoken dialogue models through RL. We address the four canonical axes of interactivity: pause handling, turn-taking, backchanneling, and user interruption. For each axis, we extract short audio segments from human conversation corpora and optimize the model with axis-specific reward functions. An extra LLM-based reward for response quality prevents semantic degradation. We apply our method to two open-source models, Moshi and PersonaPlex, demonstrating consistent improvements in interactivity on both offline evaluation with pre-recorded audio and real-time multi-turn dialogue evaluation.
中文标题/摘要
标题：全双工语音模型中的多维度交互对齐
全双工语音对话模型可同时听与说，是自然对话的有前景架构。但当前模型仅通过基于词元似然最大化的监督学习训练，未直接优化交互层面行为，导致沉默过多、轮次切换不当等交互问题。近期工作已应用强化学习（RL）提升交互性，但现有方法仅覆盖有限交互行为。本文提出一种后训练对齐方法，通过RL全面优化全双工语音对话模型的交互性。我们针对交互性的四大经典维度：停顿处理、轮次切换、插入反馈、用户打断，分别从人类对话语料中提取短音频片段，并使用维度特异性奖励函数优化模型。额外引入基于大语言模型的响应质量奖励，防止语义退化。该方法应用于两个开源模型Moshi和PersonaPlex，在离线音频评估和实时多轮对话评估中均取得一致交互性提升。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitations of supervised training in full-duplex speech models that lead to poor interactivity like excessive silence and misaligned turn-taking, this work proposes a post-training reinforcement learning alignment method targeting four core interactive behaviors: pause handling, turn-taking, backchanneling, and user interruption. The method extracts short audio segments from human conversation corpora and trains the model using axis-specific reward functions for each behavior, while an additional LLM-based reward ensures semantic quality. Experiments on Moshi and PersonaPlex show consistent improvements in interactivity across both offline audio evaluations and real-time multi-turn dialogue interactions.
为了解决监督学习无法直接优化对话交互行为的问题，本文提出一种后训练强化学习对齐方法，针对对话中的四个核心交互维度——停顿处理、轮次切换、插入回应和用户打断进行优化。该方法从人类对话语料中提取短音频片段，并为每个维度设计专用奖励函数，同时引入基于大语言模型的奖励机制以保证语义质量。在Moshi和PersonaPlex两个开源模型上的实验表明，该方法在离线音频评估和实时多轮对话中均显著提升了交互表现。
Flaws in the LLM Automation Narrative
Authors: George Perrett, Javae Elliott, Jennifer Hill, Marc Scott
First: 2026-06-09T17:46:10+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-09T17:46:10+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly described as performing at the level of human experts on knowledge economy tasks. These claims are primarily based on how LLMs perform on benchmarking tasks that measure average performance across standardized datasets. Primary limitations of many benchmarking tasks are that they often measure performance based on content directly included in LLM training data, and they frequently do not assess the reliability of LLM performance or the magnitude of LLM errors. However, in high stakes contexts, these qualities are critically important. Through a novel LLM benchmarking task that requires writing computer code to complete a data analysis task, we compare the performance of a frontier LLM against submissions from human experts and explicitly measure the variance of responses and the magnitude of errors. Our study reveals that the human experts perform better on average on a range of metrics and demonstrate less variability in performance. Our results provide evidence that LLMs do not consistently perform at the level of human experts and demonstrate the importance of measuring variance and assessing error magnitude in LLM benchmark evaluations.
中文标题/摘要
标题：大型语言模型自动化叙事中的缺陷
大型语言模型（LLMs）越来越多地被描述为在知识经济任务上达到人类专家水平。这些说法主要基于LLMs在衡量标准化数据集平均表现的基准测试中的表现。许多基准测试的主要局限在于，它们通常基于直接包含在LLM训练数据中的内容来评估表现，且很少评估LLM表现的可靠性或错误幅度。然而，在高风险情境下，这些品质至关重要。通过一项新颖的LLM基准测试任务——要求编写计算机代码完成数据分析任务，我们比较了前沿LLM与人类专家提交结果的表现，并明确测量了响应的变异性及错误幅度。研究发现，人类专家在多项指标上平均表现更优，且表现更稳定。我们的结果表明，LLMs并未始终如一地达到人类专家水平，强调了在LLM基准评估中测量变异性及评估错误幅度的重要性。
Summary / 总结
The paper challenges the prevailing narrative that large language models (LLMs) match human experts in knowledge economy tasks, arguing that existing benchmarks often fail to measure reliability or error magnitude, which are crucial in high-stakes contexts. To address this, the authors introduce a novel benchmark requiring LLMs to write code for data analysis tasks, comparing them against human experts while explicitly tracking response variance and error size. Results show human experts outperform LLMs on average across multiple metrics and exhibit far less variability, indicating that LLMs do not consistently match expert-level performance and underscoring the need for more rigorous evaluation that includes error assessment and performance stability.
该研究质疑当前将大语言模型（LLM）视为与人类专家相当的主流观点，指出现有基准测试方法存在严重缺陷。作者设计了一项新颖的评估任务，要求LLM编写用于数据分析的代码，并与人类专家进行对比，同时明确测量响应的变异性与错误幅度。结果表明，人类专家在多项指标上平均表现更优，且性能波动远小于LLM，说明LLM在高风险场景中表现不稳定且易出错。
ReasonAlloc: Hierarchical Decoding-Time KV Cache Budget Allocation for Reasoning Models
Authors: Wenhao Liu, Hao Shi, Yunhe Li, Weizhi Fei, Xiangyuan Wang, Mengzhe Ruan, Hanxu Hou, Peisong Wang, Linqi Song, Shuang Qiu
First: 2026-06-09T17:44:23+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-09T17:44:23+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Long chain-of-thought (CoT) trajectories in large language model (LLM) reasoning cause severe inference bottlenecks due to rapid key-value (KV) cache growth. Current decoding-time compression methods mitigate this issue via token eviction, but typically assume a uniform budget distribution across all layers and heads. In contrast, existing non-uniform budget allocation methods are predominantly designed for the static prompt prefill phase, and they do not capture the stepwise context demands of autoregressive reasoning. To bridge this gap, we propose ReasonAlloc, a training-free framework that recasts decoding-time KV compression as a hierarchical budget allocation problem. ReasonAlloc operates at two complementary levels: an offline layer-wise preallocation strategy captures an architecture-driven demand pattern which we call ``\textit{Reasoning Wave}'', while an online head-wise strategy reallocates resources during decoding to information-rich heads based on real-time utility. Evaluations on mathematical reasoning benchmarks (MATH-500, AIME~2024) using DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Llama-8B, DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Qwen-14B, and AceReason-14B show that ReasonAlloc outperforms uniform-budget R-KV, SnapKV, and Pyramid-RKV (a baseline enforcing a static, monotonically decreasing layer budget), with the largest gains at small budgets (128-512 tokens). ReasonAlloc is plug-and-play with existing token-eviction policies and introduces negligible inference-time overhead.
中文标题/摘要
标题：ReasonAlloc：面向推理模型的解码时KV缓存预算分层分配
大语言模型（LLM）推理中的长链式思维（CoT）轨迹因键值（KV）缓存快速增长导致严重推理瓶颈。现有解码时压缩方法通过令牌驱逐缓解该问题，但通常假设预算在所有层和头之间均匀分布。相比之下，现有非均匀预算分配方法主要针对静态提示预填充阶段设计，无法捕捉自回归推理的逐步上下文需求。为弥合此差距，我们提出ReasonAlloc，一个无需训练的框架，将解码时KV压缩重构为分层预算分配问题。ReasonAlloc在两个互补层级运作：离线层级预分配策略捕捉我们称之为“推理波”的架构驱动需求模式；在线头级策略根据实时效用动态重分配资源至信息丰富的头。在数学推理基准（MATH-500、AIME 2024）上使用DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Llama-8B、DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Qwen-14B和AceReason-14B评估表明，ReasonAlloc优于均匀预算R-KV、SnapKV及Pyramid-RKV（强制静态单调递减层预算）基线，尤其在小预算（128-512个令牌）下收益最大。ReasonAlloc可与现有令牌驱逐策略无缝集成，且引入的推理时开销可忽略不计。
Summary / 总结
To address the inference bottlenecks caused by rapid KV cache growth during long chain-of-thought reasoning in LLMs, ReasonAlloc introduces a training-free, hierarchical budget allocation framework that dynamically manages KV cache during decoding. It combines an offline layer-wise preallocation strategy that identifies an architecture-driven demand pattern called 'Reasoning Wave' with an online head-wise reallocation mechanism that prioritizes resources toward information-rich transformer heads based on real-time utility. Evaluated on mathematical reasoning benchmarks using multiple models, ReasonAlloc consistently outperforms uniform-budget and static baseline methods, especially under tight cache budgets (128–512 tokens), while remaining compatible with existing token eviction policies and introducing minimal inference overhead.
为解决大语言模型在长链式推理过程中因KV缓存快速增长导致的推理瓶颈，ReasonAlloc提出一种无需训练的分层预算分配框架，动态管理解码时的KV缓存压缩。该框架结合离线层级预分配策略（识别出名为“推理波”的架构驱动需求模式）与在线头级资源重分配机制（根据实时效用优先分配给信息密集的Transformer头）。在多个数学推理基准测试中，ReasonAlloc在小预算（128–512 tokens）下显著优于统一预算及静态基线方法，同时兼容现有令牌驱逐策略，且引入的推理开销极小。
COGENT: Continuous Graph Emulators with Neural Ordinary Differential Equations for Long-Term Physical Forecasting
Authors: Zesheng Liu, Maryam Rahnemoonfar
First: 2026-06-09T17:43:30+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-09T17:43:30+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
In this work, we present COGENT, a continuous graph emulator with Neural Ordinary Differential Equations for long-term physical forecasting on irregular geospatial meshes. COGENT encodes a finite history of system states and associated forcing fields and external forcings with a graph-based history encoder, producing node-wise context vectors that capture both local spatial interactions and temporal evolution. These context vectors initialize and condition a latent Neural Ordinary Differential Equation whose dynamics are driven by interpolated future forcings and explicit relative rollout time. By modeling the forecast trajectory as a continuous latent dynamical system, COGENT can generate predictions at arbitrary future times rather than being restricted to a fixed temporal discretization. A residual decoder maps the resulting latent trajectories back to future physical states, enabling direct multi-step forecasting without repeatedly feeding predicted states back into the model. This formulation combines graph-based spatial representation, history-conditioned latent dynamics, and continuous-time rollout in a unified framework for mesh-based physical simulation emulation. In order to stabilize training with long-horizon supervision, we also propose effective rollout-horizon sampling and a progressive rollout-horizon scheduling strategy. We evaluate COGENT on transient ice-sheet simulations generated by the Ice-sheet and Sea-level System Model, demonstrating improved long-range stability over autoregressive graph baselines. These results suggest that continuous graph Neural ODEs provide a promising methodology for scalable physical forecasting on irregular geospatial meshes, particularly in applications that require stable long-horizon predictions and the ability to query system states at arbitrary times.

标题：COGENT：基于神经常微分方程的连续图模拟器，用于长期物理预测

It encodes historical system states and forcings via a graph-based encoder to produce spatial-temporal context vectors, which initialize a latent Neural ODE conditioned on interpolated future forcings and rollout time. The model generates continuous trajectories through this ODE, then decodes them into future physical states without autoregressive feedback. Experimental results on ice-sheet simulations show COGENT outperforms autoregressive graph baselines in long-horizon stability, validating the efficacy of continuous latent dynamics for scalable, time-flexible physical forecasting.
为解决在不规则地理网格上进行长期物理模拟时对任意时间点稳定预测的需求，COGENT采用图结构历史编码器捕捉过去状态与强迫项的时空动态，初始化一个由插值未来强迫项和滚动时间驱动的潜在神经常微分方程（Neural ODE），并通过残差解码器将潜在轨迹映射回未来物理状态，实现无需自回归反馈的多步预测。在冰盖模拟实验中，COGENT在长时程稳定性上优于自回归图模型，验证了其在需要稳定长时程预测和任意时间点查询的应用中具有良好的可扩展性。
Geometric Formulation of Unified Force-Impedance Control on SE(3) for Robotic Manipulators
Authors: Joohwan Seo, Nikhil Potu Surya Prakash, Soomi Lee, Arvind Kruthiventy, Megan Teng, Jongeun Choi, Roberto Horowitz
First: 2025-04-23T20:06:09+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-09T17:41:55+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
In this paper, we present an impedance control framework on the SE(3) manifold, which enables force tracking while guaranteeing passivity. Building upon the unified force-impedance control (UFIC) and our previous work on geometric impedance control (GIC), we develop the geometric unified force impedance control (GUFIC) to account for the SE(3) manifold structure in the controller formulation using a differential geometric perspective. As in the case of the UFIC, the GUFIC utilizes energy tank augmentation for both force-tracking and impedance control to guarantee the manipulator's passivity relative to external forces. This ensures that the end effector maintains safe contact interaction with uncertain environments and tracks a desired interaction force. Moreover, we resolve a non-causal implementation problem in the UFIC formulation by introducing velocity and force fields. Due to its formulation on SE(3), the proposed GUFIC inherits the desirable SE(3) invariance and equivariance properties of the GIC, which helps increase sample efficiency in machine learning applications where a learning algorithm is incorporated into the control law. The proposed control law is validated in a simulation environment under scenarios requiring tracking an SE(3) trajectory, incorporating both position and orientation, while exerting a force on a surface. The codes are available at https://github.com/Joohwan-Seo/GUFIC_mujoco.

本文提出了一种在SE(3)流形上的阻抗控制框架，实现力跟踪并保证被动性。基于统一力阻抗控制（UFIC）及我们先前的几何阻抗控制（GIC）工作，我们开发了几何统一力阻抗控制（GUFIC），从微分几何视角在控制器设计中考虑SE(3)流形结构。与UFIC类似，GUFIC采用能量罐增强方法，确保操作臂相对于外力的被动性，从而保证末端执行器在不确定环境中安全接触并跟踪期望的交互力。此外，我们通过引入速度场和力场解决了UFIC公式中的非因果实现问题。由于其在SE(3)上的表述，所提出的GUFIC继承了GIC的优良SE(3)不变性和等变性，有助于在结合学习算法的机器学习应用中提升样本效率。该控制律在仿真环境中得到验证，场景要求跟踪包含位置和姿态的SE(3)轨迹，同时对表面施加力。代码可在https://github.com/Joohwan-Seo/GUFIC_mujoco获取。
