Motivated by the need for passivity and geometric robustness in robotic manipulation under uncertain environments, this paper introduces geometric unified force-impedance control (GUFIC) on the SE(3) manifold, extending prior unified force-impedance control (UFIC) with differential geometric tools. The method leverages energy tank augmentation to ensure passivity during force tracking and integrates velocity and force fields to resolve non-causal implementation issues in UFIC. Experimental validation in simulation demonstrates that GUFIC successfully tracks desired SE(3) trajectories while maintaining safe contact forces, and inherits SE(3) invariance and equivariance properties that enhance sample efficiency in learning-based control applications.
为实现机器人在不确定环境中安全的力跟踪控制，本文在SE(3)流形上提出几何统一力阻抗控制（GUFIC），扩展了统一力阻抗控制（UFIC）与几何阻抗控制（GIC）框架。该方法采用微分几何视角将SE(3)结构融入控制器，通过能量罐增强确保系统耗散性，并引入速度场与力场解决UFIC的非因果实现问题。仿真实验表明，GUFIC可同时跟踪含位姿的SE(3)轨迹并施加控制力于表面，且继承SE(3)不变性与等变性，提升学习控制中的样本效率。
AMEL: Accumulated Message Effects on LLM Judgments
Authors: Sid-Ali Temkit
First: 2026-05-21T16:51:04+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-09T17:40:55+00:00
Comments: 24 pages, 14 figures, 8 tables. Single author. Code, data (84,088 deduplicated API responses), and analysis pipeline at https://github.com/chutapp/amel
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Large language models are routinely used as automated evaluators: to review code, moderate content, or score outputs, often with many items passing through one conversation. We ask whether the polarity of prior conversation history biases subsequent judgments, an effect we call the accumulated message effect on LLM judgments (AMEL). Across 84,088 API calls to 12 models from 5 providers (OpenAI, Anthropic, Google, DeepSeek, and four open-source models), we present identical test items in isolation or following histories saturated with predominantly positive or negative evaluations. Models shift toward the conversation's prevailing polarity (d = -0.17, p < 10^-53). The effect concentrates on items where the model is genuinely uncertain at baseline (d = -0.36 for high-entropy items, vs d = -0.15 when the baseline is deterministic). Bias does not grow with context length: 5 prior turns and 50 produce the same shift (Spearman |r| < 0.01; OLS slope p = 0.80). And there is a negativity asymmetry: paired per item, negative histories induce 1.52x more bias than positive (t = 13.03, p < 10^-36, n = 2,733). Scaling helps but does not solve it (Anthropic: Haiku -0.22 to Opus -0.17; OpenAI: Nano -0.34 to GPT-5.2 -0.17). Three follow-ups narrow the mechanism. The token probability distribution shifts continuously, not at a threshold. The negativity asymmetry has both token-level and semantic components, though attributing the balance is exploratory at our sample sizes. Position does not matter: five biased turns anywhere in a 50-turn history produce the same shift. The simplest fix for evaluation pipelines is a fresh context per item; when batching is unavoidable, balancing the history helps.
中文标题/摘要
标题：AMEL：累积消息效应对大语言模型判断的影响
大型语言模型常被用作自动化评估器，用于审查代码、内容审核或评分输出，通常有大量项目通过同一对话。我们探讨先前对话历史的极性是否会影响后续判断，这一效应称为累积消息效应对大语言模型判断的影响（AMEL）。通过对5家供应商（OpenAI、Anthropic、Google、DeepSeek及四个开源模型）的12个模型进行84,088次API调用，我们展示了相同测试项目在孤立呈现或跟随充满主要正面或负面评价的历史后的情况。模型会趋向于对话的主流极性（d = -0.17，p < 10^-53）。该效应主要集中在模型基线时真正不确定的项目上（高熵项目d = -0.36，而基线确定性项目d = -0.15）。偏倚不随上下文长度增加：5轮与50轮前序对话产生相同偏移（Spearman |r| < 0.01；OLS斜率p = 0.80）。且存在负面偏向不对称性：每项配对比较，负面历史诱导的偏倚是正面历史的1.52倍（t = 13.03，p < 10^-36，n = 2,733）。扩展规模有助于但无法完全解决该问题（Anthropic：Haiku -0.22至Opus -0.17；OpenAI：Nano -0.34至GPT-5.2 -0.17）。三个后续研究缩小了机制。词元概率分布连续变化，而非阈值触发。负面偏向不对称性同时包含词元级和语义成分，但因样本量限制，归因尚属探索性。位置无关紧要：50轮历史中任何位置的5轮偏倚对话均产生相同偏移。评估流程中最简单的解决方案是每项独立上下文；若必须批处理，平衡历史有助于缓解问题。
Summary / 总结
This study investigates whether prior conversation history biases LLM judgments, termed the accumulated message effect (AMEL). Using 84,088 API calls across 12 models from five providers, identical test items were presented either in isolation or after histories saturated with positive or negative evaluations. Results show models consistently shift toward the prevailing polarity in history (effect size d = -0.17, p < 10^-53), especially on uncertain items (d = -0.36 vs. d = -0.15 for deterministic ones). The bias is unaffected by context length (5 vs. 50 turns) and exhibits a strong negativity asymmetry: negative histories induce 1.52x more bias than positive (t = 13.03, p < 10^-36). Scaling improves but does not eliminate the effect. Mechanisms involve continuous token probability shifts, not thresholds, and position in history does not matter. The simplest mitigation is resetting context per item or balancing history when batching.
本研究探讨先前对话历史是否会影响大语言模型的判断，这一现象称为累积消息效应（AMEL）。通过84,088次API调用，覆盖12个来自五家厂商的模型，相同测试项在孤立呈现或跟随大量正/负评价历史后进行测试。结果显示模型会持续偏向对话中主导的情感极性（效应量d = -0.17，p < 10^-53），在基线不确定的项目上偏差更显著（d = -0.36），而非确定性项目（d = -0.15）。偏差不随上下文长度增加而增强（5轮与50轮效果无差异），负面历史引发的偏差是正面历史的1.52倍。模型规模增大虽能缓解但无法根除该效应。机制分析表明，词元概率分布呈连续变化而非阈值切换，历史中位置不影响偏差，最简单解决方案是每项独立重置上下文或平衡历史内容。
Itô maps for any-step SDEs
Authors: Zhengkai Pan, Peter Potaptchik, Wenxi Yao, Michael S. Albergo, Jakiw Pidstrigach
First: 2026-06-09T17:39:22+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-09T17:39:22+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Recent one-step generative models accelerate sampling by learning deterministic flow maps of the underlying dynamics. These methods rely on learning from ordinary differential equations, leaving open how to define an exact distillation procedure for stochastic dynamics. We introduce the Itô map, an any-step stochastic flow map that takes an intermediate state and Brownian path and predicts future states in a single pass. The Itô map formulation yields novel estimators for inference-time control by providing cheap, differentiable access to posterior samples. Empirically, Itô maps produce diverse, conditionally valid endpoint samples from fixed intermediate states and support strong steering performance on synthetic and image-generation benchmarks. These results establish any-step SDE integration as a useful primitive for posterior sampling and stochastic control.
中文标题/摘要
标题：伊藤映射用于任意步长的随机微分方程
近期的单步生成模型通过学习底层动力学的确定性流映射来加速采样。这些方法依赖于常微分方程的学习，尚未解决如何为随机动力学定义精确的蒸馏过程。我们引入伊藤映射，这是一种任意步长的随机流映射，它根据中间状态和布朗路径在单次传递中预测未来状态。伊藤映射的表述提供了推理时控制的新估计器，通过提供廉价且可微分的后验样本访问。实证结果表明，伊藤映射从固定中间状态生成多样且条件有效的终点样本，并在合成和图像生成基准上支持强大的引导性能。这些结果确立了任意步长SDE积分作为后验采样和随机控制的有用基本构件。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for efficient sampling in stochastic systems, this work introduces the Itô map, a deterministic flow map that predicts future states from an intermediate state and Brownian path in a single step, enabling any-step integration of stochastic differential equations. Unlike prior one-step methods, the Itô map provides differentiable, low-cost access to posterior samples, facilitating inference-time control. Experiments show it generates diverse and conditionally valid endpoint samples from fixed intermediates and achieves strong steering performance on synthetic and image-generation tasks, demonstrating its utility as a primitive for posterior sampling and stochastic control.
为提升随机系统采样效率，本文提出Itô映射，这是一种从中间状态和布朗路径单次预测未来状态的确定性流映射，支持任意步长的随机微分方程积分。该方法利用Itô过程结构，构建可微、低成本的后验采样与控制估计器。实验表明，Itô映射能从固定中间状态生成多样且条件有效的终点样本，并在合成与图像生成基准上展现出强大的引导能力，验证其作为后验采样与随机控制基础构件的有效性。
Mean Flow Distillation: Robust and Stable Distillation for Flow Matching Models
Authors: An Zhao, Shengyuan Zhang, Zhongjian Sun, Yixiang Zhou, Zejian Li, Ling Yang, Tianrun Chen, Lingyun Sun
First: 2026-06-09T17:39:19+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-09T17:39:19+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Flow Matching models have demonstrated strong performance across a wide range of generative tasks. However, their reliance on ODE-based iterative sampling incurs substantial computational overhead in inference, which limits their applicability in real-time scenes. While distillation is a promising solution, existing approaches largely borrow from diffusion-based score matching, often failing to exploit the intrinsic geometric structure of flows and suffering from training instability, high variance, and degraded generation quality. In this paper, we propose Mean Flow Distillation (MFD), a novel distillation framework tailored for flow matching models. We theoretically demonstrate that MFD acts as a temporal low-pass filter, effectively suppressing the high-frequency optimization noise inherent in variational score distillation (VSD) while ensuring global trajectory consistency. We further prove the Mean Flow Matching Theorem, establishing that matching expected average velocities is sufficient for strict distribution alignment. Empirically, on challenging tasks of high-dimensional manifolds including 4D occupancy forecasting and text-to-image generation, MFD achieves state-of-the-art performance, enabling high-fidelity single-step generation.
中文标题/摘要
标题：均值流蒸馏：面向流匹配模型的鲁棒稳定蒸馏方法
流匹配模型在多种生成任务中表现出色，但其依赖基于ODE的迭代采样，在推理时带来显著计算开销，限制了其在实时场景中的应用。尽管蒸馏是一种有前景的解决方案，现有方法多借鉴扩散模型的分数匹配，往往未能充分利用流的内在几何结构，且存在训练不稳定、方差高、生成质量下降等问题。本文提出均值流蒸馏（MFD），一种专为流匹配模型设计的蒸馏框架。我们从理论上证明MFD作为时域低通滤波器，能有效抑制变分分数蒸馏（VSD）固有的高频优化噪声，同时确保全局轨迹一致性。我们进一步证明了均值流匹配定理，表明匹配期望平均速度足以实现严格分布对齐。实验表明，在高维流形的挑战性任务（如4D占据预测与文本到图像生成）中，MFD达到最先进性能，支持高保真单步生成。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the computational inefficiency of ODE-based sampling in flow matching models, this paper introduces Mean Flow Distillation (MFD), a distillation method designed to improve stability and efficiency. MFD leverages the geometric structure of flows by acting as a temporal low-pass filter, reducing optimization noise from variational score distillation while preserving global trajectory consistency. Theoretical analysis supports this via the Mean Flow Matching Theorem, which shows that aligning expected average velocities suffices for distribution alignment. Experimentally, MFD achieves state-of-the-art results on high-dimensional tasks like 4D occupancy forecasting and text-to-image generation, enabling high-fidelity single-step generation without iterative sampling.
为解决流匹配模型因ODE采样导致的计算开销问题，本文提出Mean Flow Distillation（MFD）方法，通过匹配期望平均速度而非瞬时得分，利用流的几何结构提升训练稳定性与生成质量。理论证明MFD可作为时域低通滤波器抑制优化噪声，实验表明其在4D占据预测与文本到图像生成等高维任务上达到最先进水平，实现无需迭代采样的高保真单步生成。
When Do Attention Circuits Form? Developmental Trajectories of Capability and Attention-Sink Emergence Across Three 1B-ClassArchitectures
Authors: Yongzhong Xu
First: 2026-06-01T15:26:23+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-09T17:39:17+00:00
Comments: 27pages, 3 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We track the developmental trajectory of attention-head circuit formation across three 1B-class language models spanning two architecture families (dense transformer, mixture-of-experts) and two pretraining corpora (The Pile, DCLM): Pythia 1B, OLMo 1B-0724-hf, and OLMoE 1B-7B-0924. At each of 10 log-spaced revisions per model -- 30 mechanistic-interpretability runs in total -- we apply a participation-ratio (PR) spectral signal and an all-head capability-specific selectivity screen to track induction, previous-token, and BOS-attractor heads as they emerge. Five findings. (F1) Layers 0 and 1 produce zero BOS-classified heads at every revision in every model: the L0/L1 zero-BOS floor is an architectural property, not a learned outcome. (F2) The whole-model BOS-attractor fraction follows three distinct emergence shapes -- a gradual ramp in Pythia 1B, a sharp phase transition in OLMo 1B (7% to 70% between adjacent checkpoints), and a gradual ramp in OLMoE 1B-7B. (F3) In DCLM models, induction-circuit formation precedes BOS-attractor formation by 10-20x in tokens; capability-circuit formation and attention-sink formation are two transitions, not one. (F4) The capability-specific screen converges to the final induction circuit within 0.3-2% of total training tokens -- circuit identification does not require the final model. (F5) For every final-checkpoint induction head sampled across all three models, per-head PR is elevated at or before the first revision at which that head crosses its capability-selectivity threshold. The results refine the induction-phase-transition framing: in 1B-class models trained on DCLM, the induction transition and the attention-sink transition are separated by an order of magnitude in tokens and have qualitatively different shapes.
中文标题/摘要
标题：注意力电路何时形成？三大1B级架构中能力与注意力沉降的发育轨迹
我们追踪了三个1B级语言模型（涵盖密集Transformer与专家混合两类架构，以及The Pile与DCLM两类预训练语料）中注意力头电路形成的发育轨迹：Pythia 1B、OLMo 1B-0724-hf 和 OLMoE 1B-7B-0924。在每个模型的10个对数间隔修订点（共30次机制可解释性运行）中，我们应用参与比（PR）频谱信号与全头能力特异性选择性筛选，追踪诱导头、前序词头与BOS吸引头的形成过程。 五大发现：(F1) 所有模型在每一轮修订中，第0层和第1层均不产生任何BOS分类头：L0/L1零BOS基线是架构固有属性，非学习结果。(F2) 整体模型BOS吸引头比例呈现三种不同形成形态——Pythia 1B渐进式上升，OLMo 1B突变式跃迁（相邻检查点间从7%至70%），OLMoE 1B-7B渐进式上升。(F3) 在DCLM模型中，诱导电路形成比BOS吸引头早10-20倍token；能力电路形成与注意力沉降是两个独立过渡，非单一过程。(F4) 能力特异性筛选在总训练token的0.3%-2%内即可收敛至最终诱导电路——电路识别无需最终模型。(F5) 对所有三模型最终检查点诱导头的采样显示，每头PR在首次跨越其能力选择性阈值的修订点前或当时即已升高。 结果细化了诱导相变框架：在DCLM训练的1B级模型中，诱导相变与注意力沉降相变在token数量上相差一个数量级，且形态质性不同。
Summary / 总结
This study investigates how attention circuits form during training in three 1B-class language models (Pythia 1B, OLMo 1B-0724-hf, OLMoE 1B-7B-0924) across two architectures and two training corpora, tracking 30 mechanistic runs via participation-ratio spectral analysis and capability-specific selectivity screens. It finds that layers 0 and 1 never produce BOS-classified heads, indicating an architectural constraint. BOS-attractor formation varies: gradual in Pythia, abrupt in OLMo 1B, and gradual again in OLMoE. Induction circuits emerge much earlier than attention sinks, especially in DCLM-trained models, and capability circuits and attention sinks are distinct transitions. The capability-specific screen identifies final induction circuits within 0.3–2% of total tokens, and induction heads show elevated PR before crossing their selectivity threshold. These results show that induction and attention-sink transitions are separable and occur at different scales in token usage.
本研究追踪了三个1B级语言模型（Pythia 1B、OLMo 1B-0724-hf、OLMoE 1B-7B）在不同语料库训练过程中注意力电路的形成轨迹，通过参与率谱分析和头级选择性筛选，在30次机制可解释性运行中观察电路演化。研究发现，第0和第1层从不产生BOS分类头，表明这是架构固有属性；BOS吸引子形成模式各异：Pythia呈渐进式，OLMo呈突变式（相邻检查点从7%跃升至70%），OLMoE则再次渐进。诱导电路形成比注意力沉降早10-20倍训练步数，且电路识别可在总训练步数的0.3%-2%内完成。更重要的是，每个诱导头在跨越选择性阈值前即表现出参与率升高，表明早期即有电路特征。这些结果表明，诱导过渡与注意力沉降是不同形态、数量级分离的独立过程。
Representational Alignment with Chemical Induced Fit for Molecular Relational Learning
Authors: Peiliang Zhang, Jingling Yuan, Qing Xie, Yongjun Zhu, Chao Che, Lin Li
First: 2025-02-07T09:29:21+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-09T17:37:19+00:00
Comments: Accepted by SIGKDD2026 AI for Science Track
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Molecular Relational Learning (MRL) is widely applied in natural sciences to predict relationships between molecular pairs by extracting structural features. The representational similarity between substructure pairs determines the functional compatibility of molecular binding sites. Nevertheless, aligning substructure representations by attention mechanisms lacks guidance from chemical knowledge, resulting in unstable model performance in chemical space (\textit{e.g.}, functional group, scaffold) shifted data. With theoretical justification, we propose the \textbf{Re}presentational \textbf{Align}ment with Chemical Induced \textbf{Fit} (ReAlignFit) to enhance the stability of MRL. ReAlignFit dynamically aligns substructure representation in MRL by introducing chemical Induced Fit-based inductive bias. In the induction process, we design the Bias Correction Function based on substructure edge reconstruction to align representations between substructure pairs by simulating chemical conformational changes (dynamic combination of substructures). ReAlignFit further integrates the Subgraph Information Bottleneck during fit process to refine and optimize substructure pairs exhibiting high chemical functional compatibility, leveraging them to generate molecular embeddings. Experimental results on nine datasets demonstrate that ReAlignFit outperforms state-of-the-art models in two tasks and significantly enhances model's stability in both rule-shifted and scaffold-shifted data distributions.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于化学诱导契合的分子关系学习表示对齐
分子关系学习（MRL）广泛应用于自然科学，通过提取结构特征预测分子对之间的关系。子结构对的表示相似性决定了分子结合位点的功能兼容性。然而，通过注意力机制对子结构表示进行对齐缺乏化学知识的引导，导致在化学空间（如官能团、骨架）发生偏移的数据中模型性能不稳定。基于理论依据，我们提出**Re**presentational **Align**ment with Chemical Induced **Fit**（ReAlignFit），以增强MRL的稳定性。ReAlignFit通过引入基于化学诱导契合的归纳偏置，动态对齐MRL中的子结构表示。在归纳过程中，我们设计基于子结构边重建的偏置校正函数，通过模拟化学构象变化（子结构的动态组合）对齐子结构对的表示。ReAlignFit进一步在拟合过程中整合子图信息瓶颈，优化高化学功能兼容性的子结构对，利用其生成分子嵌入。在九个数据集上的实验结果表明，ReAlignFit在两项任务中优于现有最先进模型，并显著提升了模型在规则偏移和骨架偏移数据分布中的稳定性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the instability of molecular relational learning (MRL) models when faced with chemical space shifts, this work introduces ReAlignFit, a method that dynamically aligns substructure representations using chemical induced fit principles. It incorporates a Bias Correction Function that reconstructs substructure edges to simulate conformational changes, guiding attention-based alignment with chemical knowledge. Additionally, it employs a Subgraph Information Bottleneck to refine compatible substructure pairs and generate molecular embeddings. Experiments across nine datasets show ReAlignFit outperforms existing models on two tasks and significantly improves stability under rule-shifted and scaffold-shifted data distributions.
为解决分子关系学习（MRL）模型在化学空间变化时性能不稳定的问题，本文提出ReAlignFit方法，通过引入化学诱导契合机制动态对齐子结构表示。该方法设计偏差校正函数，通过子结构边重建模拟构象变化以对齐表示，并结合子图信息瓶颈优化高化学功能兼容性子结构对，生成分子嵌入。在九个数据集上的实验表明，ReAlignFit在两项任务上优于现有最先进模型，并显著提升模型在规则迁移和骨架迁移数据分布下的稳定性。
P3D-Bench: Benchmarking MLLMs for Parametric 3D Generation and Structural Reasoning
Authors: Yikang Yang, Zhanpeng Hu, Youtian Lin, Mengqi Zhou, Jingxi Xu, Feihu Zhang, Jiaheng Liu, Yao Yao
First: 2026-06-09T17:36:34+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-09T17:36:34+00:00
Comments: Project page: https://lucasqaq.github.io/p3d/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Multimodal large language models can write code to produce complex programs as well as use programs to do 3D modeling, which opens up a new avenue for 3D generation powered by their priors, world knowledge and reasoning. Yet existing benchmarks rarely evaluate 3D modeling through code. Such modeling demands more than runnable code: from a text or visual specification, a model must generate a parametric 3D program that is geometrically precise, semantically aligned and assembly-consistent. We introduce P3D-Bench, a benchmark for parametric 3D generation. Unlike a 3D mesh, a parametric 3D program exposes explicit dimensions, construction operations and part relations, revealing whether a model recovers a design's structure, not just its appearance. Under a unified protocol, P3D-Bench covers three task families (Text-to-3D, Image-to-3D and Assembly-3D) and scores each output for executability, geometric fidelity, topology, text-grounded constraints, multiview semantic alignment and part-level structure. We evaluate frontier MLLMs and text-only LLMs on 400 text cases, 400 image cases and 203 annotated assemblies, with domain-specific models as reference points. Our extensive evaluation yields three findings. First, assemblies are the hardest setting, where models still fail to compose multiple parts into a coherent structure. Second, models can often recover the global shape and semantic identity of the target object, yet fail to reproduce the precise parametric geometry specified by the input. Third, part-level modeling remains weak on assemblies, where models recover neither the geometry of each part nor the right number of parts. These results position P3D-Bench as a benchmark for evaluating precise parametric geometry and part-level structure in parametric 3D generation.
中文标题/摘要
标题：P3D-Bench：用于参数化3D生成与结构推理的多模态大语言模型基准
多模态大语言模型不仅能编写代码生成复杂程序，还能利用程序进行3D建模，这为基于其先验知识、世界知识和推理能力的3D生成开辟了新途径。然而，现有基准很少通过代码评估3D建模能力。此类建模不仅需要可运行的代码：从文本或视觉规范出发，模型必须生成几何精确、语义一致且装配一致的参数化3D程序。我们引入P3D-Bench，一个参数化3D生成基准。与3D网格不同，参数化3D程序显式暴露尺寸、构造操作和部件关系，揭示模型是否恢复设计的结构，而不仅是外观。在统一协议下，P3D-Bench涵盖三大任务家族（文本到3D、图像到3D和装配3D），并针对每个输出评估可执行性、几何保真度、拓扑结构、文本约束、多视角语义对齐及部件级结构。我们在400个文本案例、400个图像案例和203个标注装配案例上评估前沿多模态大语言模型和纯文本大语言模型，以领域特定模型为参考基准。我们的全面评估得出三项发现：第一，装配场景最难，模型仍无法将多个部件组合成连贯结构；第二，模型常能恢复目标对象的全局形状和语义身份，但无法复现输入所指定的精确参数化几何；第三，装配场景中部件级建模能力仍弱，模型既无法恢复各部件几何，也难以确定正确部件数量。这些结果确立了P3D-Bench作为评估参数化3D生成中精确参数几何与部件级结构的基准地位。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to evaluate multimodal large language models' ability to generate precise parametric 3D models through code, the authors introduce P3D-Bench, a benchmark that assesses models across text-to-3D, image-to-3D, and assembly-3D tasks using explicit geometric and structural criteria. The benchmark evaluates outputs for executability, geometric fidelity, topology, semantic alignment, and part-level structure under a unified protocol. Experimental results on 400 text cases, 400 image cases, and 203 assemblies reveal that models struggle most with assembly tasks, often failing to correctly compose parts or reproduce exact parametric geometry, despite successfully capturing global shape and semantic identity.
为评估多模态大语言模型通过代码生成精确参数化3D模型的能力，作者提出了P3D-Bench基准，涵盖文本到3D、图像到3D和装配到3D三类任务，并通过可执行性、几何保真度、拓扑结构、语义一致性及部件级结构等指标进行评估。实验结果表明，尽管模型常能捕捉目标物体的整体形状与语义身份，但在参数化几何精度和部件级结构上表现薄弱，尤其在装配任务中，模型难以正确组合部件或保持部件数量与几何准确性。
JOIN: Anchor-Grasp-Conditioned Joining via Opposition, Inference, and Navigation for Bimanual Assistive Manipulation
Authors: Drake Moore, Matt Cheng, Xiang Zhi Tan, Taşkın Padır
First: 2026-06-09T17:36:06+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-09T17:36:06+00:00
Comments: Xiang Zhi Tan and Taşkın Padır share equal advising
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Assistive mobility and manipulation platforms have received increasing attention as a means of restoring independence to individuals with disabilities. While effective for many basic activities of daily living (ADLs), a significant percentage of everyday tasks such as opening a jar, pouring a liquid, lifting a tray, or basic meal preparation, is fundamentally bimanual and remains out of reach for any single-arm system. Adding a second arm to a wheelchair is impractical, due to the additional power draw, cost, and the loss of space required for transfers and mobility. We instead propose a heterogeneous, on-demand bimanual system, in which a wheelchair-mounted anchor arm is joined when needed by a summoned mobile manipulator that serves as a complement arm. The central technical problem, which we call bimanual joining, is conditional: the anchor has already committed to a grasp, and the complement arm must choose where to stand and what to grasp to complete the task. We formulate bimanual joining as a three-phase decomposition (plan, drive, grasp) and show that a vision-language model (VLM), coupled with standard geometric tools, provides task-level knowledge sufficient to solve a representative class of bimanual ADLs. Our system JOIN, contributes (i) a wheelchair-referenced opposition score, and (ii) task-conditioned directional manipulability. We evaluate JOIN on a Kinova Gen3 anchor and a Hello Robot Stretch~3 complement on representative same-object and different-object tasks. JOIN accomplished more attempts (19/20) than state-of-the-art methods (14/20) and required markedly less correction by the operator.
中文标题/摘要
标题：JOIN：通过对抗、推理与导航实现锚定抓取条件下的双臂协同操作
辅助移动与操作平台作为恢复残障人士独立性的手段，受到越来越多关注。尽管对许多日常生活基本活动（ADLs）有效，但诸如开罐头、倒液体、端托盘或基础餐食准备等日常任务本质上需双臂协同，单臂系统仍无法胜任。在轮椅上增加第二只手臂因耗电、成本高及占用转移与移动空间而不切实际。我们提出一种异构、按需双臂系统：轮椅上安装锚定臂，当需要时由召唤的移动操作臂作为辅助臂协同工作。核心技术问题——双臂协同连接——具有条件约束：锚定臂已锁定抓取，辅助臂需选择站立位置与抓取目标以完成任务。我们将双臂协同连接分解为三阶段（规划、驱动、抓取），并证明视觉-语言模型（VLM）结合标准几何工具，即可提供解决代表性双臂ADL任务所需的任务级知识。我们的系统JOIN贡献：（i）轮椅参考系下的对抗评分；（ii）任务条件下的方向可操作性。我们在Kinova Gen3锚定臂与Hello Robot Stretch~3辅助臂上评估JOIN，针对同类与异类物体任务，JOIN完成尝试次数（19/20）高于现有最先进方法（14/20），且操作员所需修正显著减少。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitations of single-arm robotic systems in performing bimanual daily living tasks, this work proposes JOIN, a heterogeneous on-demand bimanual system that pairs a wheelchair-mounted anchor arm with a mobile complement arm. The method decomposes bimanual joining into three phases—plan, drive, grasp—and leverages a vision-language model with geometric tools to guide the complement arm’s positioning and grasping. Experimental results show JOIN successfully completed 19 out of 20 tasks, outperforming state-of-the-art methods (14/20) and requiring significantly less operator correction.
为帮助残障人士完成需要双手协同的日常活动，该研究提出JOIN系统，通过将轮椅上固定的锚定机械臂与可召唤的移动机械臂协同工作，实现空间高效、实用的双臂操作。方法将双臂协同任务分解为规划、移动、抓取三阶段，结合视觉语言模型与几何工具，引导移动机械臂定位与抓取。实验表明，JOIN在20项任务中成功完成19项，优于现有方法（14/20），且需操作者修正次数显著减少。
ABC-Bench: An Agentic Bio-Capabilities Benchmark for Biosecurity
Authors: Andrew Bo Liu, Samira Nedungadi, Bryce Cai, Alex Kleinman, Harmon Bhasin, Seth Donoughe
Venue: ICML 2026
First: 2026-06-09T17:35:37+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-09T17:35:37+00:00
Comments: 18 pages. To be published in ICML 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large language models (LLMs) are rapidly acquiring capabilities relevant to biological research, from literature synthesis to interpretation of experimental data. Increasingly, LLM agents can also perform in silico biology tasks that previously required experienced human biologists. These emerging AI capabilities offer new opportunities for scientific discovery and biomedical advances, but they also shift the landscape of biosecurity risks. To address this, we introduce the Agentic Bio-Capabilities Benchmark (ABC-Bench), a suite of tasks to measure agentic biosecurity-relevant capabilities. ABC-Bench evaluates LLM agents on both benign and dual-use biology tasks: writing code to operate liquid handling robots, designing DNA fragments for in vitro assembly, and evading DNA synthesis screening. These tasks require a combination of biology and software expertise. All tested LLM agents outperformed the median expert human baseliner on all three tasks. Agents performed highly on tasks drawing on published knowledge and well-documented protocols, and more weakly on a task requiring novel bioinformatics reasoning. In three wet-lab validation experiments, we found that OpenAI's o4-mini-high produced scripts that, when run on an OpenTrons liquid handling robot, successfully assembled DNA with expected sequences.
中文标题/摘要
标题：ABC-Bench：面向生物安全的代理生物能力基准
大型语言模型（LLMs）正迅速获得与生物研究相关的多种能力，从文献综述到实验数据解读。越来越多的LLM代理能够执行此前需经验丰富的生物学家才能完成的体外生物学任务。这些新兴AI能力为科学发现和生物医学进步带来新机遇，但也改变了生物安全风险格局。为此，我们提出代理生物能力基准（ABC-Bench），用于评估代理在生物安全相关任务中的能力。ABC-Bench评估LLM代理在良性与双重用途生物学任务中的表现：编写操控液体处理机器人的代码、设计体外组装的DNA片段、以及规避DNA合成筛查。这些任务需结合生物学与软件专业知识。所有测试的LLM代理在三项任务中均优于中位数人类专家基线。代理在依赖已发表知识和明确协议的任务中表现优异，在需新颖生物信息学推理的任务中表现较弱。在三个湿实验验证中，我们发现OpenAI的o4-mini-high生成的脚本在OpenTrons液体处理机器人上成功组装出预期序列的DNA。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the growing biosecurity risks posed by LLMs capable of performing complex biological tasks, the authors introduce ABC-Bench, a benchmark designed to evaluate agentic capabilities in biosecurity-relevant domains. The benchmark assesses LLM agents on three tasks: writing code to control liquid handling robots, designing DNA fragments for in vitro assembly, and evading DNA synthesis screening, requiring integrated biology and software knowledge. All tested LLM agents outperformed median human experts on all tasks, excelling in those based on published protocols but struggling with novel bioinformatics reasoning. Wet-lab validation confirmed that o4-mini-high successfully generated executable scripts that assembled target DNA sequences on a physical robot.
为应对大型语言模型代理在生物领域能力提升所引发的生物安全风险，本文提出ABC-Bench基准测试，用于评估代理在生物安全相关任务中的能力。该基准包含三项任务：编写控制液体处理机器人代码、设计体外组装DNA片段、规避DNA合成筛查，需融合生物学与软件知识。所有测试的LLM代理在三项任务中均优于中位数人类专家，擅长基于已发表知识和标准流程的任务，但在需创新生物信息学推理的任务上表现较弱。湿实验验证表明，o4-mini-high生成的脚本在实际机器人上成功组装出预期DNA序列。
Efficiently Learning Drifting Halfspaces with Massart Noise
Authors: Mingchen Ma, Guyang Cao, Jelena Diakonikolas, Ilias Diakonikolas
Venue: ICML 2026
First: 2026-06-09T17:35:18+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-09T17:35:18+00:00
Comments: To appear at ICML 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We study the problem of learning a drifting concept in the presence of Massart noise. In this framework, an online learner has access to a history of independent samples whose labels are noisy versions of a target concept that may change from round to round. The goal is to output, in each round, a hypothesis with small prediction error. We study the complexity of this learning problem for the fundamental class of margin-separable linear classifiers (halfspaces). On the positive side, we give a computationally efficient learner achieving error $η+ \tilde O(Δ^{1/3}/γ)$, where $η$ upper bounds the Massart noise rate, $Δ$ is the drift rate, and $γ$ is the margin. Interestingly, in the realizable setting, an adaptation of our techniques yields an efficient learner with an improved error rate over prior work. On the lower-bound side, we provide formal evidence of an information-computation tradeoff, strongly suggesting that our algorithm's performance is essentially optimal. Specifically, while the information-theoretically optimal error scales with $Δ^{1/2}$, we prove that $Δ^{1/3}$-scaling is unavoidable for low-degree polynomial tests, even in the special case of random classification noise.
中文标题/摘要
标题：在Massart噪声下高效学习漂移半空间
我们研究在Massart噪声存在下学习漂移概念的问题。在此框架中，在线学习者可访问一系列独立样本，其标签是目标概念的噪声版本，且该目标概念可能在每轮之间发生变化。目标是在每轮输出一个预测误差较小的假设。我们研究了这一学习问题在基本的边距可分线性分类器（半空间）类中的复杂性。正面来看，我们提出了一种计算高效的算法，其误差为$η+ \tilde O(Δ^{1/3}/γ)$，其中$η$上界Massart噪声率，$Δ$为漂移率，$γ$为边距。有趣的是，在可实现设定下，我们技术的改进版本可获得优于先前工作的误差率。在下界方面，我们提供了信息-计算权衡的正式证据，强烈表明我们的算法性能本质上是最优的。具体而言，虽然信息论最优误差随$Δ^{1/2}$缩放，我们证明即使在随机分类噪声的特殊情况下，低次多项式测试也必须遵循$Δ^{1/3}$的缩放，这是不可避免的。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of learning concepts that drift over time under Massart noise, this work develops an efficient online algorithm for learning drifting halfspaces. The method leverages a novel analysis of margin-separable linear classifiers to achieve a prediction error of η + Õ(Δ^{1/3}/γ), where η bounds noise, Δ is drift rate, and γ is margin. Experimentally, the algorithm demonstrates improved error rates over prior methods in the realizable setting and establishes a theoretical lower bound showing that Δ^{1/3} scaling is unavoidable for low-degree polynomial tests, indicating near-optimality.
为解决在Massart噪声下随时间漂移的概念学习问题，本文提出了一种针对半空间（线性可分分类器）的高效在线学习算法。该方法通过新颖分析实现预测误差上界η + Õ(Δ^{1/3}/γ)，其中η为噪声上界，Δ为漂移速率，γ为边际。实验与理论结果表明，该算法在可实现设定下优于先前方法，且接近最优，因为下界证明低次多项式测试的误差必须随Δ^{1/3}增长，而信息论最优为Δ^{1/2}。
MOFA-VTON: More Fashion Possibilities with Fine-Grained Adaptations in Virtual Try-On
Authors: Xiaoyu Han, Chenyang Wang, Jing Wang, Shunyuan Zheng, Quanling Meng, Shengping Zhang
Venue: CVPR 2026 Highlight
First: 2026-06-09T17:34:25+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-09T17:34:25+00:00
Comments: Accepted to CVPR 2026 (Highlight)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Virtual try-on aims to fit an in-shop clothing image onto a specific human body. An optimal virtual try-on method should provide diverse and flexible dressing options, accurately reflecting the varied wearing styles encountered in real-life scenarios, tailored to individual preferences and fashion aspirations. However, current methods predominantly perform a direct replacement of the original clothing with the target clothing, following the same dressing pattern. This limited control over clothing adaptation may result in fixed and monotonous try-on outputs. To delve into More Fashion Possibilities with Fine-Grained Adaptations in Virtual Try-On, we propose a novel virtual try-on method, termed MOFA-VTON, which allows adjustment for clothing adaptations in try-on results through simple sketches by users. Specifically, we first design a mask construction strategy that transforms user-drawn curve sketches into a dual-region mask, replacing the traditional clothing-agnostic mask and providing fine-grained layout guidance for the subsequent generation process. Further, we propose layout adjustment blocks that utilize the cross-attention mechanism to independently learn layout correspondences for upper and lower regions of the human body, refining the spatial arrangement of the two regions. With these implementations, our method enables flexible and fine-grained adaptations of target clothing, overcoming the constraints of a fixed layout. Extensive experiments on VITON-HD and DressCode datasets demonstrate that our proposed MOFA-VTON outperforms previous state-of-the-art methods and provides more fashion possibilities for virtual try-on.
中文标题/摘要
标题：MOFA-VTON：虚拟试衣中的细粒度适配以实现更多时尚可能性
虚拟试衣旨在将商店中的服装图像适配到特定人体上。理想的虚拟试衣方法应提供多样且灵活的穿搭选项，准确反映现实生活中遇到的各种穿着风格，满足个人偏好与时尚追求。然而，当前方法主要直接替换原始服装为目标服装，遵循相同的穿搭模式，对服装适配的控制有限，可能导致试衣结果固定且单调。为探索虚拟试衣中的更多时尚可能性，我们提出一种新颖的虚拟试衣方法——MOFA-VTON，允许用户通过简单草图调整试衣结果中的服装适配。具体而言，我们首先设计了一种掩码构建策略，将用户绘制的曲线草图转换为双区域掩码，替代传统服装无关掩码，为后续生成过程提供细粒度布局指导。此外，我们提出布局调整模块，利用交叉注意力机制独立学习人体上下区域的布局对应关系，优化两区域的空间排列。通过这些实现，我们的方法实现了目标服装的灵活且细粒度适配，突破了固定布局的限制。在VITON-HD和DressCode数据集上的大量实验表明，我们的MOFA-VTON方法优于现有最先进方法，为虚拟试衣提供了更多时尚可能性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for more flexible and personalized virtual try-on results that reflect diverse real-world fashion styles, MOFA-VTON introduces a method enabling users to adjust clothing layout via simple sketches. It achieves this by converting user-drawn curves into a dual-region mask for fine-grained spatial guidance and employs layout adjustment blocks with cross-attention to independently refine upper and lower body adaptations. Experimental results on VITON-HD and DressCode show that MOFA-VTON significantly outperforms prior methods in generating varied, fashion-forward try-on outputs with improved layout control.
为实现更灵活、个性化的虚拟试衣效果，MOFA-VTON提出一种通过用户简笔画实现细粒度服装布局调整的方法。该方法首先将用户手绘曲线转化为双区域掩码以引导空间布局，再通过基于交叉注意力的布局调整模块独立优化上半身与下半身的对应关系。在VITON-HD和DressCode数据集上的实验表明，MOFA-VTON显著优于现有方法，能生成更具时尚多样性的试衣结果，且布局精度与适应性大幅提升。
CoTAL: Human-in-the-Loop Prompt Engineering for Generalizable Formative Assessment Scoring and Feedback
Authors: Clayton Cohn, Ashwin T S, Naveeduddin Mohammed, Gautam Biswas
First: 2025-04-03T06:53:34+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-09T17:33:56+00:00
Comments: Submitted to Computers and Education: Artificial Intelligence. Currently under review
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large language models (LLMs) have created new opportunities to assist teachers and support student learning. While researchers have explored various prompt engineering approaches in educational contexts, the degree to which these approaches generalize across domains--such as science, computing, and engineering--remains underexplored. In this paper, we introduce Chain-of-Thought Prompting + Active Learning (CoTAL), an LLM-based approach to formative assessment scoring that (1) leverages Evidence-Centered Design (ECD) to align assessments and rubrics with curriculum goals, (2) applies human-in-the-loop prompt engineering to automate response scoring, and (3) incorporates chain-of-thought (CoT) prompting and teacher and student feedback to iteratively refine questions, rubrics, and LLM prompts. Our findings demonstrate that CoTAL improves GPT-4's scoring performance across domains, achieving gains of up to 38.9% over a non-prompt-engineered baseline (i.e., without labeled examples, chain-of-thought prompting, or iterative refinement). Teachers and students judge CoTAL to be effective at scoring and explaining responses, and their feedback produces valuable insights that enhance grading accuracy and explanation quality.
中文标题/摘要
标题：CoTAL：面向可泛化形成性评估评分与反馈的人类参与提示工程
大语言模型（LLMs）为辅助教师和支持学生学习创造了新机遇。尽管研究人员已在教育场景中探索了多种提示工程技术，但这些方法在不同领域（如科学、计算与工程）中的泛化程度仍缺乏充分研究。本文提出链式思维提示+主动学习（CoTAL），一种基于LLM的形成性评估评分方法：（1）利用证据中心设计（ECD）使评估与评分标准与课程目标对齐；（2）采用人类参与的提示工程自动化评分；（3）结合链式思维（CoT）提示及教师与学生反馈，迭代优化题目、评分标准和LLM提示。研究发现，CoTAL显著提升GPT-4在多领域的评分表现，相较无提示工程基线（无标注示例、链式思维提示或迭代优化）提升高达38.9%。教师与学生认为CoTAL在评分与解释响应方面有效，其反馈亦为提升评分准确性与解释质量提供了宝贵洞见。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for generalizable and accurate formative assessment scoring across diverse academic domains, this study introduces CoTAL, a human-in-the-loop prompt engineering method that combines Evidence-Centered Design with chain-of-thought prompting and iterative feedback from teachers and students to refine LLM-based scoring systems. The method structures prompts to align assessments with curriculum goals, uses human input to guide automated scoring, and iteratively improves question design, rubrics, and prompt templates. Experimental results show CoTAL significantly boosts GPT-4’s scoring performance—up to 38.9% improvement over a baseline without prompt engineering—while also enhancing the quality and perceived accuracy of feedback from both teachers and students.
为解决跨学科领域通用化自动形成性评估工具不足的问题，本文提出CoTAL方法，结合证据中心设计、思维链提示与教师学生反馈，通过人机协作优化大语言模型的评分能力。该方法利用结构化提示与主动学习引导模型评分，并持续根据反馈调整题目、评分标准与提示。实验表明，CoTAL相较无提示工程的基线模型在多领域评分中提升高达38.9%，且教师与学生对其评分准确性和解释质量评价较高。
OncoTraj: a public benchmark for longitudinal resistance prediction in EGFR-mutant non-small-cell lung cancer on osimertinib
Authors: Abhijoy Sarkar, Aarchi Singh Thakur
First: 2026-06-09T17:33:24+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-09T17:33:24+00:00
Comments: 24 pages, 7 figures, 4 tables. Code, data, and trained model weights: https://github.com/span-ai-labs/oncotraj. Python package: pip install oncotraj. Dataset: https://huggingface.co/datasets/span-ai-labs/oncotraj-v1
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Resistance to first-line osimertinib in EGFR-mutant non-small-cell lung cancer (NSCLC) is the canonical example of predictable clonal evolution under therapeutic pressure, yet no public benchmark exists for training or evaluating computational models on the corresponding longitudinal patient trajectories. We introduce OncoTraj, a public benchmark of 813 EGFR-mutant NSCLC patients receiving first-line osimertinib, harmonized from three real-world clinical-genomic sources: MSK-CHORD (672 patients), AACR Project GENIE BPC NSCLC (34 patients), and the FLAURA molecular-resistance supplement (107 patients). OncoTraj defines three locked tasks: (A) binary classification of progression by a fixed 12-month landmark, (B) regression of time-to-first-progression in days, and (C) six-class classification of the dominant resistance mechanism. We release the harmonized dataset, patient-level train/validation/test splits with an audited no-leakage guarantee, an open-source evaluation harness, and six reference baselines spanning a majority-class predictor, logistic regression, random forest, XGBoost, an LSTM, and a multi-task transformer. With v1's single-timepoint snapshot features, no task clears chance on clean within-source evaluation: the uniformity of this ceiling across every model class localizes the limit to the input modality (single-snapshot tissue NGS rather than serial ctDNA), not the algorithm. The benchmark does recover a reproducible literature-consistent association: TP53 co-mutation raises the 12-month progression rate from 29% to 59% cohort-wide. OncoTraj establishes a reproducible, leakage-audited baseline and converts the modality limit into concrete design requirements for a serial-ctDNA-enriched v2.
中文标题/摘要
标题：OncoTraj：用于预测EGFR突变非小细胞肺癌患者使用奥希替尼一线治疗耐药性的公开基准
EGFR突变非小细胞肺癌（NSCLC）患者对一线奥希替尼产生耐药性是治疗压力下可预测克隆演化的经典范例，但目前尚无公开基准用于训练或评估计算模型在相应纵向患者轨迹上的表现。我们引入OncoTraj，一个整合自三个真实世界临床基因组数据源（MSK-CHORD：672例患者，AACR Project GENIE BPC NSCLC：34例患者，FLAURA分子耐药补充数据：107例患者）的813例EGFR突变NSCLC患者公开基准。OncoTraj定义了三个固定任务：（A）以固定12个月为里程碑的进展二分类，（B）以天为单位的首次进展时间回归，（C）主要耐药机制的六类分类。我们发布标准化数据集、具有审计无泄漏保证的患者级训练/验证/测试划分、开源评估框架及六种参考基线模型（多数类预测器、逻辑回归、随机森林、XGBoost、LSTM、多任务Transformer）。在v1版本单时间点快照特征下，所有任务在源内干净评估中均未超越随机水平，且该上限在各类模型中一致，表明限制因素在于输入模态（单次组织NGS而非连续ctDNA），而非算法本身。该基准重现了文献一致的关联：TP53共突变使12个月进展率从29%升至59%（全队列）。OncoTraj建立了可复现、经泄漏审计的基准，并将模态限制转化为v2版本（富集连续ctDNA）的具体设计要求。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the lack of a standardized benchmark for predicting resistance to osimertinib in EGFR-mutant NSCLC using longitudinal patient data, the authors introduce OncoTraj, a harmonized dataset of 813 patients from three clinical-genomic sources. The benchmark defines three tasks: binary progression classification at 12 months, time-to-progression regression, and six-class resistance mechanism classification. Evaluated with six baseline models including logistic regression, random forest, XGBoost, LSTM, and a transformer, all models underperformed chance on single-timepoint features, indicating the limitation stems from the input modality (tissue NGS) rather than algorithmic shortcomings. However, the benchmark successfully replicated a known clinical finding: TP53 co-mutation significantly increases 12-month progression risk from 29% to 59%. OncoTraj thus provides a leakage-audited baseline and sets clear requirements for future models using serial ctDNA data.
为解决EGFR突变型非小细胞肺癌患者使用奥希替尼后耐药预测缺乏标准化基准的问题，作者构建了OncoTraj公共数据集，整合813名患者的纵向临床基因组数据。该基准包含三项任务：12个月进展二分类、进展时间回归及六类耐药机制分类。在包括逻辑回归、随机森林、XGBoost、LSTM和Transformer在内的六种模型上测试，所有模型在单时间点特征下均未超越随机水平，表明性能瓶颈源于输入模态（组织NGS而非动态ctDNA），而非算法本身。然而，该基准成功复现了文献中已知结果：TP53共突变使12个月进展率从29%升至59%。OncoTraj因此提供了可审计无泄漏的基准，并为未来基于ctDNA动态数据的模型设计指明了明确方向。
PSEBench: A Controllable and Verifiable Benchmark for Evaluating LLMs in Patient Safety Event Triage
Authors: Keqi Han, Ryan Young, Annabel Strauss, Lindsey Hughes, Katharine M. Nesbitt, Nicole Schueler, Che Ngufor, Carl Yang, Yuan Xue, Zhijun Yin
First: 2026-06-03T21:41:39+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-09T17:31:07+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Patient safety event triage, determining whether a clinical event is reportable under jurisdiction-specific policy, is a high-stakes task typically performed manually by patient safety experts. Although LLMs may support this workflow, reliable evaluation is limited by the lack of benchmarks to capture evidence-grounded policy reasoning, proactive information seeking for incomplete reports, and principled abstention in irreducibly ambiguous cases. We address this gap with a policy-grounded construction methodology centered on the clause card, a structured representation that factorizes regulatory text into auditable decision specifications. Combining clause cards with anchor-driven instantiation and closed-loop verification, our scalable pipeline produces narratives with by-construction ground truth and naturally supports generating missing information and uncertain variants. We instantiate this method on Minnesota's 29 Reportable Adverse Health Events, producing PSEBench, a 5,074-case benchmark with an agentic evaluation environment. Evaluation on 15 representative LLMs reveals consistent capability trends, demonstrates the benchmark's utility, and identifies actionable gaps toward reliable LLM-based patient safety event triage.
中文标题/摘要
标题：PSEBench：用于评估大型语言模型在患者安全事件分诊中的可控且可验证的基准
患者安全事件分诊，即判断某一临床事件是否需根据管辖区域政策上报，是一项高风险任务，通常由患者安全专家手动完成。尽管大型语言模型可能支持该工作流程，但其可靠评估受限于缺乏能够捕捉基于证据的政策推理、对不完整报告主动寻求信息以及在不可化解的模糊情况下原则性回避的基准。我们通过以政策为基础的构建方法解决这一问题，该方法以“条款卡片”为核心，这是一种将监管文本结构化分解为可审计决策规范的表示方式。结合条款卡片、锚点驱动实例化与闭环验证，我们的可扩展流程生成具有构建内禀真实性的叙事，并自然支持生成缺失信息和不确定变体。我们以明尼苏达州的29类可报告不良健康事件为例，构建了PSEBench基准，包含5,074个案例及一个代理评估环境。在15个代表性大型语言模型上的评估揭示了其能力趋势的一致性，验证了该基准的实用性，并指出了实现可靠大型语言模型驱动的患者安全事件分诊的可操作性差距。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for reliable evaluation of LLMs in patient safety event triage—a high-stakes, policy-dependent task—this work introduces PSEBench, a benchmark built using a policy-grounded methodology centered on clause cards that decompose regulatory text into auditable decision rules. The method combines clause cards with anchor-driven instantiation and closed-loop verification to generate narratives with built-in ground truth, including cases requiring missing information or uncertain outcomes. Evaluated across 15 LLMs, PSEBench reveals consistent performance trends, validates its utility for assessing policy reasoning and proactive information seeking, and highlights specific gaps in current models’ ability to handle ambiguity and incomplete data in triage decisions.
为解决当前大语言模型在患者安全事件分诊评估中缺乏可靠基准的问题，本研究提出PSEBench基准，采用条款卡片方法将法规文本结构化为可审计的决策规则。该方法结合锚点驱动实例化与闭环验证，生成带有真实标签的叙事，支持证据推理、主动信息补充与明确的不确定性处理。在15个大语言模型上的评估显示，该基准有效揭示了模型能力趋势，验证了其评估价值，并指出了模型在处理模糊性和不完整报告时的关键短板。
SAFE: An LLM-as-Verifier Framework for Evidence-Grounded Multi-Hop Reasoning
Authors: Daeyong Kwon, Soyoung Yoon, Seung-won Hwang
First: 2026-04-02T12:59:30+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-09T17:29:55+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Multi-hop QA benchmarks often reward Large Language Models (LLMs) for spurious correctness, where models reach correct answers through invalid intermediate reasoning. We propose SAFE, an LLM-as-verifier framework for evidence-grounded multi-hop QA. Rather than judging only the final answer after generation, SAFE verifies reasoning during generation by checking intermediate steps against the provided passages and previous reasoning trajectory. To make this process checkable, SAFE decomposes reasoning into atomic, evidence-grounded units represented with Knowledge Graph (KG) triples. At train-time, SAFE verifies benchmark supervision under KG-grounded constraints and constructs reliable verifier training data. At inference-time, an external verifier checks each generated step, identifies invalid reasoning, and provides correction feedback before errors propagate. Across three multi-hop QA benchmarks, SAFE improves accuracy by 8.8 pp on average. These results show that evidence-grounded multi-hop QA benefits from shifting LLM-based evaluation from post-hoc answer judgment to stepwise reasoning verification.
中文标题/摘要
标题：SAFE：一种基于证据的多跳推理LLM验证框架
多跳问答基准常奖励大型语言模型（LLM）的虚假正确性，即模型通过无效的中间推理得出正确答案。我们提出SAFE，一种基于证据的多跳问答LLM验证框架。与仅在生成后判断最终答案不同，SAFE在生成过程中通过将中间步骤与提供的段落和先前推理轨迹进行比对来验证推理。为使该过程可验证，SAFE将推理分解为原子化的、基于证据的单元，用知识图谱（KG）三元组表示。训练时，SAFE在KG约束下验证基准监督，并构建可靠的验证器训练数据；推理时，外部验证器检查每一步生成内容，识别无效推理并提供修正反馈，防止错误传播。在三个多跳问答基准上，SAFE平均提升准确率8.8个百分点。结果表明，基于证据的多跳问答受益于将LLM评估从事后答案判断转向逐步推理验证。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the issue that LLMs often achieve correct answers in multi-hop QA through spurious reasoning, the paper introduces SAFE, an LLM-as-verifier framework that verifies reasoning steps during generation rather than only judging final outputs. SAFE decomposes reasoning into atomic, evidence-grounded units using Knowledge Graph triples, enabling step-by-step validation against provided passages and prior reasoning. During training, it constructs verifier data under KG constraints; at inference, an external verifier detects invalid steps and provides corrective feedback before errors accumulate. Experiments across three benchmarks show SAFE improves accuracy by an average of 8.8 percentage points, demonstrating that stepwise verification enhances evidence-grounded multi-hop reasoning.
为解决大语言模型在多跳问答中通过错误中间推理获得正确答案的问题，本文提出SAFE框架，利用LLM作为验证器在生成过程中逐步验证推理步骤，而非仅评估最终答案。该框架将推理分解为基于证据的原子单元，用知识图谱三元组表示，便于与原文和历史推理对比验证。训练阶段在知识图谱约束下构建验证器训练数据；推理阶段外部验证器检测无效步骤并提供修正反馈。在三个多跳问答基准上，SAFE平均提升准确率8.8个百分点，表明基于证据的多跳问答性能可通过从事后答案判断转向逐步推理验证获得显著提升。
Data assimilation for subsurface flow using latent diffusion model parameterization: performance of ensemble-Kalman and Monte Carlo techniques
Authors: Guido Di Federico, Wenchao Teng, Louis J. Durlofsky
First: 2026-06-09T17:29:47+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-09T17:29:47+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Data assimilation (DA) in subsurface flow entails calibrating model parameters to match observed data, typically at wells, while preserving geological realism. Latent diffusion models (LDMs) provide efficient mappings from high-dimensional geological model space to a low-dimensional latent variable, reducing the dimensionality of the inverse problem while maintaining plausibility in posterior geomodels. However, the high nonlinearity in the LDM mapping may degrade the performance of Kalman-gain-based ensemble updates. We present a systematic comparison of DA algorithms applied to large-scale 3D channelized geomodels with hierarchical geological uncertainty. We compare model-space and latent-space DA using the ensemble smoother with multiple data assimilation (ESMDA), and demonstrate a key trade-off: model-space updates achieve significant uncertainty reduction but produce geologically unrealistic posterior models, while latent-space updates preserve realism but exhibit limited uncertainty reduction. Motivated by this, we explore rigorous Markov chain Monte Carlo (MCMC) and Sequential Monte Carlo (SMC) algorithms in the 3D-LDM latent space. To accommodate their high computational demands, we develop a fast surrogate flow model that approximates well-rate responses. MCMC and SMC are evaluated against ESMDA across three synthetic test cases, with DA performed in the LDM latent space. All models maintain geological realism due to the LDM parameterization. MCMC and SMC are consistent with one another and achieve lower data mismatch and more uncertainty reduction than latent-space ESMDA. Our overall results demonstrate that ensemble Kalman methods may provide overestimated posterior uncertainty with highly nonlinear parameterizations, while rigorous Monte Carlo sampling, enabled by fast surrogate models, can provide a more reliable alternative.
中文标题/摘要
标题：利用潜在扩散模型参数化进行地下流体流动数据同化：集合卡尔曼与蒙特卡洛方法的性能比较
地下流体流动中的数据同化（DA）旨在校准模型参数以匹配观测数据（通常为井点数据），同时保持地质现实性。潜在扩散模型（LDMs）可高效地将高维地质模型空间映射到低维潜在变量，降低反演问题的维度，同时保持后验地质模型的合理性。然而，LDM映射的高非线性可能降低基于卡尔曼增益的集合更新性能。本文系统比较了应用于具有分层地质不确定性的大型三维通道化地质模型的DA算法，比较了模型空间与潜在空间DA的集合平滑多重数据同化（ESMDA）方法，并揭示了关键权衡：模型空间更新显著降低不确定性，但产生地质上不现实的后验模型；潜在空间更新保持现实性，但不确定性降低有限。为此，我们在三维LDM潜在空间中探索严谨的马尔可夫链蒙特卡洛（MCMC）和序贯蒙特卡洛（SMC）算法。为应对其高计算需求，我们开发了一种快速代理流模型，近似井流量响应。MCMC和SMC在三个合成测试案例中与ESMDA对比，DA均在LDM潜在空间中进行。所有模型因LDM参数化而保持地质现实性。MCMC和SMC结果一致，且数据不匹配更小、不确定性降低更显著，优于潜在空间ESMDA。整体结果表明，集合卡尔曼方法在高度非线性参数化下可能高估后验不确定性，而借助快速代理模型的严谨蒙特卡洛采样可提供更可靠的替代方案。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of calibrating subsurface flow models while preserving geological realism, this study evaluates data assimilation methods using latent diffusion models that map high-dimensional geological spaces to low-dimensional latent variables. It compares ensemble-Kalman and Monte Carlo techniques—specifically ESMDA in both model and latent spaces—and finds that model-space updates reduce uncertainty more but produce unrealistic models, while latent-space updates preserve realism at the cost of limited uncertainty reduction. To address this, the paper applies rigorous MCMC and SMC methods in the latent space, supported by a fast surrogate flow model to handle computational demands. Results show that MCMC and SMC outperform latent-space ESMDA in both data mismatch and uncertainty reduction, while maintaining geological plausibility, suggesting Monte Carlo methods are more reliable for highly nonlinear parameterizations.
为解决在高非线性条件下校准含水层模型并保持地质真实性的难题，本研究比较了基于潜在扩散模型（LDM）的数据同化方法，旨在降低维度。实验对比了模型空间与潜在空间中的集合平滑（ESMDA）及蒙特卡洛（MCMC/SMC）方法，发现模型空间更新虽显著降低不确定性，但导致地质不真实；潜在空间更新则保持地质合理性但不确定性降低有限。为支持蒙特卡洛方法的计算可行性，研究开发了快速代理流模型。在三个合成三维通道化模型测试中，MCMC与SMC在数据拟合误差和不确定性降低方面均优于潜在空间ESMDA，同时保持地质合理性，表明在非线性LDM参数化下，蒙特卡洛方法可提供更可靠的不确定性量化。
First-Order Trajectory Matching: Fast Ensemble Predictions of Chaotic, Turbulent, Stochastic Systems
Authors: Shreya Jha, Timo Schorlepp, Nicholas Geissler, Jules Berman, Benjamin Peherstorfer
First: 2026-06-09T17:29:05+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-09T17:29:05+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We introduce First-Order Trajectory Matching (FTM), a surrogate-modeling method that learns the first-order local transport of probability mass from trajectories of stochastic systems. By matching the symmetric first-order motion of trajectories, FTM learns the probability current velocity, whose flow preserves time marginals to match ensemble averages, while also capturing current-like trajectory quantities such as fluxes, circulations, and barrier-crossing currents. FTM learns the current velocity directly from trajectories, avoiding drift, diffusion, and score estimation. Our stability analysis separates discretization error from sampling variance and shows that the one-step simulation-free FTM loss is stable when temporal resolution and sample size are properly balanced. Across stochastic dynamical systems and PDE examples, we empirically demonstrate that FTM provides trajectory-aware ensemble predictions at low, deterministic-rollout cost.
中文标题/摘要
标题：一阶轨迹匹配：混沌、湍流、随机系统的快速集合预测
我们提出了第一阶轨迹匹配（FTM），一种代理建模方法，通过学习随机系统轨迹的局部一阶概率质量传输来实现。通过匹配轨迹的对称一阶运动，FTM学习概率电流速度，其流动保持时间边缘分布以匹配集合平均值，同时也能捕捉类似电流的轨迹量，如通量、环流和屏障穿越电流。FTM直接从轨迹中学习电流速度，避免漂移、扩散和得分估计。我们的稳定性分析将离散误差与采样方差分离，并表明当时间分辨率和样本量适当平衡时，一步模拟无需求的FTM损失是稳定的。在多个随机动力系统和偏微分方程示例中，我们实证表明FTM能在低确定性滚动成本下提供轨迹感知的集合预测。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for efficient, trajectory-aware predictions in chaotic and stochastic systems, First-Order Trajectory Matching (FTM) learns the probability current velocity directly from trajectories without estimating drift, diffusion, or scores. It matches symmetric first-order motion to preserve time marginals and capture fluxes, circulations, and barrier-crossing currents. Experimental results across stochastic dynamical systems and PDEs show FTM delivers accurate ensemble predictions at low computational cost, with stability confirmed when temporal resolution and sample size are balanced.
为在混沌和随机系统中实现高效、轨迹感知的预测，第一阶轨迹匹配（FTM）通过匹配轨迹的对称一阶运动直接学习概率电流速度，避免了漂移、扩散和得分估计。该方法从轨迹数据中直接推断电流速度，从而实现保持时间边缘分布并捕捉通量、环流和屏障穿越电流的集合预测。在随机动力系统和偏微分方程的实验中，FTM在无需模拟的情况下实现了低成本、高精度的确定性滚动预测，且通过平衡时间分辨率与样本量确保了稳定性。
The Model Says Walk: How Surface Heuristics Override Implicit Constraints in LLM Reasoning
Authors: Yubo Li, Lu Zhang, Tianchong Jiang, Ramayya Krishnan, Rema Padman
First: 2026-03-30T21:36:09+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-09T17:28:54+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large language models fail when a salient surface cue conflicts with an unstated feasibility constraint. We introduce the Heuristic Override Benchmark (HOB): 500 instances spanning 4 heuristic families and 5 constraint families, with minimal pairs and explicitness gradients. We pair HOB with a falsifiable behavioral characterization following a diagnose-measure-bridge-treat arc. Causal-behavioral analysis of the car wash problem across six models reveals context-independent sigmoid heuristics: the distance cue has 8.7 to 38 times more influence than the goal, and attribution better matches keyword association than compositional inference. Across 14 models, strict 10/10 evaluation shows that no model exceeds 75%, and presence constraints are hardest at 44%. A minimal hint improves performance by 15 pp, suggesting a constraint-inference failure rather than missing knowledge. However, 12 of 14 models perform worse when the constraint is removed, by up to 39 pp, revealing conservative bias. A thinking-mode ablation on Gemini 3.1 Pro drops performance from 74.6% with thinking on to 58.4% with thinking off, while explicit goal decomposition recovers it to 71.2%. Thus, internal deliberation does useful work, and explicit prompting can partially substitute for it. Reasoning models do not categorically outperform non-reasoning peers: after controlling for capability rank, the residual reasoning-mode effect is 1.8 pp and is not significant. Parametric probes show that the sigmoid pattern generalizes to cost, efficiency, and semantic-similarity heuristics. Goal-decomposition prompting improves performance by 5.0 pp, compared with 3.1 pp for generic chain-of-thought, isolating constraint enumeration as the active ingredient. Overall, heuristic override is a systematic reasoning vulnerability with a quantified locus in inference order, not knowledge, and a tested intervention.
中文标题/摘要
标题：模型建议步行：表面启发如何覆盖LLM推理中的隐式约束
大语言模型在显著表面线索与未明示的可行性约束冲突时会失败。我们引入启发式覆盖基准（HOB）：涵盖4类启发式和5类约束的500个实例，包含最小对和明确性梯度。HOB与遵循诊断-测量-桥梁-治疗弧的可证伪行为特征相结合。对六种模型的洗车问题进行因果-行为分析，揭示了与上下文无关的S型启发式：距离线索的影响是目标的8.7至38倍，归因更匹配关键词关联而非组合推理。在14个模型中，严格10/10评估显示无模型超过75%，且存在约束时最难，仅44%。最小提示可提升性能15个百分点，表明是约束推理失败而非知识缺失。然而，14个模型中有12个在移除约束后表现更差，最多下降39个百分点，揭示保守偏见。在Gemini 3.1 Pro上关闭思考模式使性能从74.6%降至58.4%，而显式目标分解可恢复至71.2%。因此，内部反思确实有效，显式提示可部分替代。推理模型并未绝对优于非推理模型：控制能力排名后，推理模式残差效应仅1.8个百分点且不显著。参数探针显示S型模式可推广至成本、效率和语义相似性启发式。目标分解提示提升性能5.0个百分点，优于通用思维链的3.1个百分点，凸显约束枚举为关键因素。总体而言，启发式覆盖是系统性推理漏洞，其量化定位在推理顺序而非知识，且有经验证的干预手段。
Summary / 总结
This study investigates how surface-level heuristics can override implicit feasibility constraints in large language model reasoning, motivated by observed failures when salient cues conflict with unstated constraints. The authors introduce the Heuristic Override Benchmark (HOB), a 500-instance dataset with minimal pairs across four heuristic and five constraint families, and apply a falsifiable behavioral framework to diagnose model behavior. Experiments across 14 models reveal that heuristic cues (e.g., distance) dominate goal-based reasoning by up to 38x, and constraint violations are most common at 44%. A minimal hint improves performance by 15 percentage points, indicating a failure in constraint inference rather than knowledge gaps, while removing constraints causes a 39-point drop in performance, revealing conservative bias. Thinking-mode ablation on Gemini 3.1 Pro shows deliberation boosts performance from 58.4% to 74.6%, and goal-decomposition prompting recovers it to 71.2%, demonstrating the value of explicit constraint enumeration. Reasoning models do not significantly outperform non-reasoning peers after controlling for capability, and parametric probes confirm the sigmoid heuristic pattern generalizes across cost, efficiency, and semantic similarity. Goal-decomposition improves performance by 5.0 pp, outperforming generic chain-of-thought by 1.9 pp, confirming constraint enumeration as the key intervention.
该研究探讨了表面启发式如何在LLM推理中覆盖隐含的可行性约束，动机源于模型在显性线索与隐性规则冲突时的失败。作者构建了包含500个实例的启发式覆盖基准（HOB），涵盖四种启发式与五类约束，采用可证伪的行为框架进行诊断。在14个模型上发现，距离等启发式比目标影响大38倍，模型存在保守偏差——移除约束反而导致性能下降最多39个百分点。Gemini 3.1 Pro的思考模式消融显示，启用思考可提升性能，显式目标分解可部分恢复，但推理模式在能力控制后仅提升1.8个百分点且不显著。参数探针验证了sigmoid模式在成本、效率和语义相似性中的普适性，目标分解提示比通用CoT提升5.0个百分点，凸显约束枚举是关键机制。
UniPET: a universal network for high-quality PET image denoising across varied dose reduction factors
Authors: Zhiwen Yang, Yang Zhou, Haowei Chen, Hui Zhang, Dan Zhao, Bingzheng Wei, Yan Xu
First: 2026-06-09T17:25:02+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-09T17:25:02+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Most existing deep learning-based PET image denoising methods assume a fixed and known dose reduction factor (DRF) for low-dose PET images. However, these methods encounter significant performance degradation when the DRF varies beyond the assumed one in practical applications. To address the challenge posed by varied DRFs, several preliminary studies focus on the task of universal PET image denoising, aiming to train a universal model over low-dose data across DRFs. Nonetheless, these vanilla universal models often struggle with misaligned styles present in different DRF data, leading to the \textit{style elimination issue} with a significant over-smoothing effect. To deal with this issue, we innovatively introduce domain generalization to PET image denoising and propose a universal PET image denoising network (UniPET) to achieve high-quality PET image denoising across diverse DRFs. UniPET comprises two primary innovations: a style alignment network (SAN) and a region-aware learning strategy (RALS). Specifically, SAN utilizes style alignment techniques derived from domain generalization to align and recover styles across different DRFs, ensuring the model's generalizability across various DRFs while effectively preserving styles. Furthermore, to enhance style recovery, RALS distinguishes between flat and stylized regions, exclusively conducting adversarial learning on the latter, thereby more effectively guiding the model's focus towards learning stylized regions. It is demonstrated that our proposed UniPET can adaptively recover different DRF styles and achieve high-quality PET image denoising across DRFs. Comprehensive experiments show that UniPET exhibits comparable performance to individual DRF-specific models at specific DRFs and realizes state-of-the-art performance in universal PET image denoising quantitatively, perceptually, and clinically.
中文标题/摘要
标题：UniPET：一种适用于不同剂量降低因子的高质量PET图像去噪通用网络
大多数现有的基于深度学习的PET图像去噪方法假设低剂量PET图像具有固定且已知的剂量降低因子（DRF）。然而，在实际应用中，当DRF超出假设范围时，这些方法性能显著下降。为应对不同DRF带来的挑战，一些初步研究聚焦于通用PET图像去噪任务，旨在训练一个能在不同DRF低剂量数据上泛化的通用模型。然而，这些基础通用模型常因不同DRF数据中存在的风格错位，导致严重的“风格消除问题”和过度平滑效应。为解决此问题，我们创新性地将领域泛化引入PET图像去噪，并提出通用PET图像去噪网络（UniPET），以实现跨多样DRF的高质量PET图像去噪。UniPET包含两项主要创新：风格对齐网络（SAN）和区域感知学习策略（RALS）。具体而言，SAN利用领域泛化衍生的风格对齐技术，在不同DRF间对齐并恢复风格，确保模型在各类DRF上的泛化能力同时有效保留风格。此外，为增强风格恢复能力，RALS区分平坦区域与风格化区域，仅对后者进行对抗学习，从而更有效地引导模型聚焦于学习风格化区域。实验表明，所提出的UniPET可自适应恢复不同DRF风格，并在各类DRF上实现高质量PET图像去噪。全面实验显示，UniPET在特定DRF下性能可媲美专用DRF模型，并在通用PET图像去噪任务中实现定量、感知及临床层面的最先进性能。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitation of existing PET denoising models that degrade under varying dose reduction factors, the authors propose UniPET, a universal network designed to handle diverse DRFs without performance loss. The method introduces a style alignment network (SAN) inspired by domain generalization to mitigate style misalignment and prevent over-smoothing, alongside a region-aware learning strategy (RALS) that selectively applies adversarial learning to stylized regions. Experimental results demonstrate that UniPET achieves performance comparable to DRF-specific models at individual DRFs and sets new state-of-the-art benchmarks in universal PET denoising across quantitative, perceptual, and clinical evaluations.
为了解决现有PET图像去噪模型在不同剂量降低因子下性能显著下降的问题，作者提出了UniPET，一种能够跨多种DRF实现高质量去噪的通用网络。该方法引入了受领域泛化启发的风格对齐网络（SAN）以缓解风格错位并避免过度平滑，同时结合区域感知学习策略（RALS），仅对风格化区域进行对抗学习，从而更精准引导模型学习。实验表明，UniPET在各特定DRF下性能可媲美专用模型，并在定量、感知及临床评估中均达到当前最优的通用PET去噪效果。
Robust Regression of General ReLUs with Queries
Authors: Ilias Diakonikolas, Daniel M. Kane, Mingchen Ma
Venue: NeurIPS 2025
First: 2026-06-09T17:24:47+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-09T17:24:47+00:00
Comments: Appeared at NeurIPS 2025
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We study the task of agnostically learning general (as opposed to homogeneous) ReLUs under the Gaussian distribution with respect to the squared loss. In the passive learning setting, recent work gave a computationally efficient algorithm that uses $poly(d,1/ε)$ labeled examples and outputs a hypothesis with error $O(opt)+ε$, where $opt$ is the squared loss of the best fit ReLU. Here we focus on the interactive setting, where the learner has some form of query access to the labels of unlabeled examples. Our main result is the first computationally efficient learner that uses $d polylog(1/ε)+\tilde{O}(\min\{1/p, 1/ε\})$ black-box label queries, where $p$ is the bias of the target function, and achieves error $O(opt)+ε$. We complement our algorithmic result by showing that its query complexity bound is qualitatively near-optimal, even ignoring computational constraints. Finally, we establish that query access is essentially necessary to improve on the label complexity of passive learning. Specifically, for pool-based active learning, any active learner requires $\tildeΩ(d/ε)$ labels, unless it draws a super-polynomial number of unlabeled examples.
中文标题/摘要
标题：带查询的通用ReLU的鲁棒回归
我们研究在高斯分布下，相对于平方损失，对一般（而非同质）ReLU进行无偏学习的任务。在被动学习设置中，近期工作给出了一种计算高效的算法，使用 $poly(d,1/ε)$ 个带标签样本，并输出一个误差为 $O(opt)+ε$ 的假设，其中 $opt$ 是最佳拟合ReLU的平方损失。本文聚焦于交互式设置，其中学习者对未标记样本的标签具有某种查询访问能力。我们的主要结果是首个计算高效的算法，使用 $d polylog(1/ε)+\tilde{O}(\min\{1/p, 1/ε\})$ 个黑盒标签查询，其中 $p$ 是目标函数的偏差，且达到误差 $O(opt)+ε$。我们通过证明其查询复杂度界限在忽略计算约束的情况下本质上接近最优，来补充我们的算法结果。最后，我们证明查询访问对于改进被动学习的标签复杂度是基本必需的。具体而言，在池式主动学习中，除非主动学习器抽取超多项式数量的未标记样本，否则任何主动学习器都需要 $\tildeΩ(d/ε)$ 个标签。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of learning general ReLUs under Gaussian noise with minimal labeled examples, this work develops a computationally efficient algorithm for the interactive learning setting. The method leverages black-box label queries to reduce label complexity, achieving error $O(opt)+ε$ using $d \polylog(1/ε) + \tilde{O}(\min\{1/p, 1/ε\})$ queries, where $p$ is the target function’s bias. Experimental and theoretical results confirm the algorithm’s efficiency and near-optimality in query usage, while also proving that query access is essential to surpass passive learning’s label complexity, as any pool-based active learner requires $\tilde{Ω}(d/ε)$ labels without super-polynomial unlabeled examples.
为在高斯噪声下以最少标注样本学习一般ReLU函数，本研究提出一种交互式学习场景下的高效算法，允许学习者查询未标注样本的标签。该方法在使用 $d \cdot \text{polylog}(1/ε) + \tilde{O}(\min\{1/p, 1/ε\})$ 个黑盒标签查询时，可达到 $O(opt)+ε$ 的误差，其中 $p$ 为目标函数的偏置。实验与理论结果表明，该查询复杂度在忽略计算限制下接近最优，且查询访问对超越被动学习的标签复杂度至关重要——任何基于池的主动学习器若不抽取超多项式数量的未标注样本，至少需要 $\tilde{Ω}(d/ε)$ 个标签。
WorldOlympiad: Can Your World Model Survive a Triathlon?
Authors: Yuke Zhao, Wangbo Zhao, Weijie Wang, Zeyu Zhang, Dakai An, Akide Liu, Yinghao Yu, Jiasheng Tang, Fan Wang, Wei Wang, Bohan Zhuang
First: 2026-06-09T17:24:36+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-09T17:24:36+00:00
Comments: Project Page: https://alibaba-damo-academy.github.io/WorldOlympiad/, Code: https://github.com/alibaba-damo-academy/WorldOlympiad
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3 · Project1
Abstract
We introduce WorldOlympiad, a benchmark for diagnosing video-based world models across physical faithfulness, geometric consistency, and interaction fidelity. While existing benchmarks often focus on visual quality, semantic alignment, or short-term temporal coherence, they provide limited insight into whether generated videos obey physical rules, preserve coherent 3D structure, and sustain controllable interactions over long horizons. To address this gap, WorldOlympiad decomposes world-model evaluation into three complementary dimensions. The physical track uses object segmentation and MLLM-as-judge to assess whether generated videos follow interpretable rules in mechanics, thermal phenomena, and material properties. The geometry track reconstructs generated videos with Gaussian splatting and evaluates structural consistency, cross-view coherence, and camera-trajectory alignment. The interaction track assesses whether generated rollouts follow complex action prompts and maintain smooth, coherent transitions across consecutive video chunks. WorldOlympiad further covers three major downstream scenarios, including gaming, robotics, and general real-world videos, capturing diverse challenges from interactive control and embodied manipulation to open-domain motion and camera dynamics. Together, these tracks and scenarios form a scalable and interpretable evaluation suite that exposes failure modes beyond generic video quality. Experiments on state-of-the-art models reveal substantial gaps in physical reasoning, 3D consistency, and long-horizon interaction, underscoring the need for more structured evaluation protocols for generative world models.
中文标题/摘要
标题：世界奥林匹克：你的世界模型能在铁人三项中存活吗？
我们提出了WorldOlympiad，一个用于诊断基于视频的世界模型的基准，涵盖物理真实性、几何一致性和交互保真度。现有基准多聚焦视觉质量、语义对齐或短期时序一致性，难以评估生成视频是否遵循物理规则、保持连贯的3D结构，并在长时域内维持可控交互。为弥补这一不足，WorldOlympiad将世界模型评估分解为三个互补维度：物理赛道通过物体分割与多模态大模型作为裁判，评估生成视频在力学、热现象和材料属性方面的可解释性规则；几何赛道通过高斯泼溅重建生成视频，评估结构一致性、跨视角连贯性及相机轨迹对齐；交互赛道评估生成轨迹是否遵循复杂动作指令，并在连续视频片段间保持平滑连贯的过渡。WorldOlympiad还覆盖三大下游场景——游戏、机器人和通用现实视频，涵盖从交互控制与具身操作到开放域运动与相机动态的多样化挑战。这些赛道与场景共同构成一个可扩展、可解释的评估体系，揭示超越通用视频质量的失败模式。在当前最先进模型上的实验表明，物理推理、3D一致性及长时域交互方面仍存在显著差距，凸显了对生成式世界模型更结构化评估协议的必要性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to evaluate video-based world models beyond superficial visual quality, WorldOlympiad introduces a structured benchmark assessing physical faithfulness, geometric consistency, and interaction fidelity. It employs three evaluation tracks: a physical track using object segmentation and MLLM-as-judge to verify mechanics and material behavior; a geometry track reconstructing videos via Gaussian splatting to check 3D structure and camera alignment; and an interaction track evaluating adherence to complex action prompts and smooth transitions. Experiments on state-of-the-art models reveal significant deficiencies in all three dimensions, particularly in long-horizon physical reasoning and geometric coherence, highlighting the inadequacy of current benchmarks for diagnosing real-world model behavior.
为弥补现有视频世界模型评估仅关注视觉质量的不足，WorldOlympiad构建了一个评估物理真实性、几何一致性和交互保真度的结构化基准。其包含三个评估维度：物理维度通过对象分割与多模态大模型作为裁判，验证力学与材料行为；几何维度利用高斯点渲染重建视频，检查三维结构与相机轨迹一致性；交互维度评估模型对复杂动作指令的遵循能力及视频片段间的平滑过渡。实验表明，当前主流模型在物理推理、几何一致性及长时交互方面存在显著缺陷，凸显现有评估体系不足以诊断真实世界模型的深层能力不足。
Provenance-Grounded Gating and Adaptive Recovery in Synthetic Post-Training Data Curation
Authors: Soham Bhattacharjee, Karun Sharma, Vinay Kumar Sankarapu, Pratinav Seth
First: 2026-06-09T17:24:27+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-09T17:24:27+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Synthetic post-training pipelines commonly filter generated samples with reward models or holistic LLM judges, yet two practices remain rarely examined together: whether the filtering signal is grounded in the source evidence that induced each generation, and whether rejected samples can be systematically recovered rather than permanently discarded. We present a controlled study of both questions across gate configurations, recovery strategies, and generator scales, using adversarially injected corpora to provide ground-truth failure labels. We find that exact source provenance improves faithfulness gating for stronger judges, that hallucination and reward gates reject largely disjoint sample populations making both necessary, and that an adaptive recovery pipeline combining failure diagnosis with targeted regeneration achieves higher yield, recovery rate, and injection recall than naive resampling. Downstream fine-tuning quality is driven primarily by generator scale, with filtration and recovery conditions contributing meaningfully but secondarily.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于溯源的门控与自适应恢复在合成后训练数据整理中的应用
合成后训练流程通常使用奖励模型或整体LLM裁判过滤生成样本，但两个实践较少被共同考察：过滤信号是否基于诱导每个生成的源证据，以及被拒样本是否能系统性恢复而非永久丢弃。我们通过对抗性注入语料提供真实失败标签，在不同门控配置、恢复策略和生成器规模下进行控制研究。发现精确的源溯源可提升强裁判的忠实度门控效果，幻觉门与奖励门拒绝的样本群体基本不重叠，两者均不可或缺；结合失败诊断与定向重生成的自适应恢复流程，相比简单重采样，可实现更高产出率、恢复率和注入召回率。下游微调质量主要由生成器规模决定，过滤与恢复条件虽有贡献，但次之。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to improve synthetic post-training data curation, this study investigates whether filtering signals should be grounded in source provenance and whether rejected samples can be adaptively recovered. Using adversarially injected corpora to provide ground-truth labels, the authors evaluate various gating configurations and recovery strategies across different generator scales. Results show that grounding filters in source evidence enhances faithfulness for stronger judges, that hallucination and reward gates target distinct sample sets necessitating both, and that adaptive recovery—combining failure diagnosis with targeted regeneration—outperforms naive resampling in yield, recovery rate, and recall. Downstream fine-tuning quality is most strongly influenced by generator scale, with filtration and recovery having secondary but meaningful effects.
该研究旨在填补合成后训练数据筛选中的两个关键空白：过滤信号是否应基于生成样本的源证据，以及被拒样本是否能被系统性恢复。通过对抗性注入语料提供真实失败标签，作者在不同生成器规模下评估了多种门控配置与恢复策略。结果表明，基于源证据的过滤可提升强判断器的忠实度，幻觉门与奖励门针对不同样本群体，两者缺一不可，而结合失败诊断与定向重生成的自适应恢复策略在产出率、恢复率和召回率上优于简单重采样。下游微调质量主要受生成器规模驱动，但筛选与恢复条件仍能提供显著的次要增益。
DMT: Demographic Conditioning, Morphology-Enhanced Transformer for Cuffless Blood Pressure Estimation from PPG Signals
Authors: Yidan Shen, Neville Mathew, Maham Rahimi, Deependra Dhakal, George Zouridakis, Xin Fu, Renjie Hu
First: 2026-06-09T17:23:07+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-09T17:23:07+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Blood pressure (BP) is a key marker for cardiovascular risk assessment and therapeutic decision-making, and Photoplethysmography (PPG) enables low-cost, wearable-friendly cuffless BP estimation. However, even with recent progress, many PPG-based models are trained with BP regression alone and may rely on amplitude-dominated shortcuts. In addition, demographic covariates that systematically modulate vascular compliance are often incorporated only via late fusion, limiting subject-specific representation learning. We propose a Transformer-based network for cuffless BP estimation from PPG signal, leveraging self-attention to capture long-range dependencies across multiple cardiac cycles. To account for subject-specific vascular differences, the model is conditioned on demographics via FiLM-style feature modulation applied through the attention and feed-forward sublayers of Transformer blocks. In addition, we add an auxiliary morphology head to guide the model to attend to BP-relevant waveform morphology associated with arterial stiffness and wave reflection. Under calibration-based evaluation protocols on the large-scale PulseDB dataset, the proposed method achieves MAE of 4.56 mmHg for systolic BP and 2.62 mmHg for diastolic BP, reducing errors by 47% and 50% compared with prior demographic-enhanced PPG baselines. The resulting lightweight, single-sensor model supports scalable and clinically grounded cuffless BP estimation in calibration-enabled deployment settings.
中文标题/摘要
标题：DMT：用于无袖带光电容积脉搏波信号血压估计的群体特征调节与形态增强变换网络
血压是心血管风险评估和治疗决策的关键指标，光电容积脉搏波（PPG）技术可实现低成本、可穿戴的无袖带血压估计。然而，尽管近期取得进展，许多基于PPG的模型仅通过血压回归训练，可能依赖幅度主导的捷径。此外，系统性调节血管顺应性的群体协变量通常仅通过后期融合引入，限制了个体化表征学习。本文提出一种基于Transformer的无袖带血压估计网络，利用自注意力机制捕捉多个心动周期间的长程依赖关系。为考虑个体血管差异，模型通过Transformer块的注意力和前馈子层中的FiLM风格特征调制，以群体特征进行条件化。此外，我们添加一个辅助形态头部，引导模型关注与动脉僵硬度和波反射相关的血压相关波形形态。在大规模PulseDB数据集上的校准评估协议下，该方法在收缩压和舒张压上的平均绝对误差分别为4.56 mmHg和2.62 mmHg，相比先前的群体特征增强PPG基线方法，误差分别降低47%和50%。该轻量级、单传感器模型支持在可校准部署环境下可扩展且临床可靠的无袖带血压估计。
