Motivated by the need for accurate, cuffless blood pressure estimation from PPG signals while addressing limitations of amplitude-focused models and weak demographic integration, the authors propose DMT: a Transformer-based architecture that conditions on demographic features via FiLM-style modulation within attention and feed-forward layers. An auxiliary morphology head is added to guide attention toward BP-relevant waveform characteristics linked to arterial stiffness. Evaluated on PulseDB under calibration protocols, DMT achieves MAE of 4.56 mmHg for systolic and 2.62 mmHg for diastolic BP, representing 47% and 50% error reductions over prior demographic-enhanced baselines, demonstrating improved subject-specific modeling and clinical scalability.
为解决PPG信号无袖带血压估计中模型依赖幅度捷径及人口统计特征整合不足的问题，作者提出DMT模型：基于Transformer架构，通过FiLM风格的特征调制在注意力与前馈层中引入人口统计条件，同时增加形态学辅助头引导模型关注与动脉僵硬度和波反射相关的血压相关波形特征。在PulseDB数据集上采用校准评估协议，DMT在收缩压和舒张压上的MAE分别为4.56 mmHg和2.62 mmHg，相较先前的人口统计增强基线分别降低47%和50%，展现出更强的个体化建模能力和临床可扩展性。
Overcoming Rank Collapse in Feedback Alignment
Authors: Gauthier Boeshertz, Razvan Pascanu, Claudia Clopath
First: 2026-06-09T17:21:25+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-09T17:21:25+00:00
Comments: 9 pages and 4 figures, 1 table for main text. Total of 28 pages and 13 figures with appendix
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Backpropagation (BP) is widely viewed as biologically implausible, in part because it requires feedback weights to be the transpose of forward weights for error propagation. Interestingly, when training a network with fixed random feedback weights to circumvent this issue, learning aligns the forward weights with the feedback weights, leading the backpropagated error signal to become an approximation of the standard gradient used by BP. This process, called Feedback Alignment (FA), occurs in MLPs and very shallow CNNs but does not scale well to deeper architectures. In this work, we first investigated differences between BP and FA models, trained on CIFAR10, specifically focusing on the effective rank of the signal. We found that the FA error has a considerably lower rank and hence is constrained to a lower-dimensional subspace compared to BP, limiting exploration of the parameter space. Motivated by this observation, we evaluated two mechanisms for increasing the effective dimensionality of FA: Muon, an optimiser that orthogonalises weight updates; and hidden activity normalisation, which promotes activation orthogonality. Across larger architectures and benchmarks, we find that these methods consistently improve over FA baselines, for example, on CIFAR100 with a Resnet-18, accuracy increases by 9 percentage points. Our results identify low-dimensional gradient dynamics as a key obstacle to scaling FA and suggest that inducing higher-dimensional update geometry is a promising route toward scaling alternatives to backpropagation.
中文标题/摘要
标题：克服反馈对齐中的秩崩溃
反向传播（BP）常被认为在生物学上不可行，部分原因是其要求反馈权重为前向权重的转置以实现误差传播。有趣的是，当使用固定随机反馈权重训练网络以规避此问题时，学习过程会使得前向权重与反馈权重对齐，从而使反向传播的误差信号近似于BP所用的标准梯度。这一过程称为反馈对齐（FA），在MLP和非常浅层的CNN中有效，但在深层架构中难以扩展。本研究首先在CIFAR10数据集上比较了BP与FA模型，特别关注信号的有效秩。我们发现FA误差的秩显著更低，因此其参数空间探索受限于更低维子空间。受此启发，我们评估了两种提升FA有效维度的方法：Muon，一种正交化权重更新的优化器；以及隐藏活动归一化，促进激活正交性。在更大规模架构和基准测试中，这些方法均显著优于FA基线，例如在CIFAR100上使用Resnet-18时，准确率提升9个百分点。我们的结果表明，低维梯度动态是FA扩展的主要障碍，而诱导更高维更新几何是替代反向传播的可行路径。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the observation that Feedback Alignment (FA) suffers from rank collapse—where error signals occupy a low-dimensional subspace compared to Backpropagation (BP)—this work investigates methods to increase the effective dimensionality of FA updates. The authors evaluate two techniques: Muon, an optimizer that orthogonalizes weight updates, and hidden activity normalization, which encourages activation orthogonality. Experiments on CIFAR100 with ResNet-18 show that both methods significantly improve FA performance, boosting accuracy by 9 percentage points over baseline FA. These results suggest that overcoming low-rank gradient dynamics is critical for scaling FA to deeper networks and that inducing higher-dimensional update geometry is a viable path toward biologically plausible alternatives to BP.
鉴于反馈对齐（FA）存在秩坍缩问题——其误差信号所处的子空间维度远低于反向传播（BP）——本研究探索了提升FA可扩展性的方法。作者提出了两种技术：Muon优化器通过正交化权重更新，以及隐藏激活归一化以促进激活正交性。在CIFAR100上使用ResNet-18的实验表明，两种方法均显著优于基础FA模型，准确率提升9个百分点。结果表明，提升梯度更新的有效维度是实现FA在深层网络中可扩展性的关键路径。
SARA: Semantically Adaptive Relational Alignment for Video Diffusion Models
Authors: Jiesong Lian, Zixiang Zhou, Ruizhe Zhong, Yuan Zhou, Qinglin Lu, Rui Wang, Long Hu, Yixue Hao, Baoru Huang
First: 2026-05-08T14:36:32+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-09T17:19:58+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Recent video diffusion models (VDMs) synthesize visually convincing clips, yet still drop entities, mis-bind attributes, and weaken the interactions specified in the prompt. Representation-alignment objectives such as VideoREPA and MoAlign improve fine-grained text following by distilling spatio-temporal token relations from a frozen visual foundation model, but their pairwise supervision budget is allocated by visual or motion cues rather than by how relevant each pair is to the prompt. We present SARA, Semantically Adaptive Relational Alignment, which keeps token-relation distillation (TRD) on a frozen VFM target and adds a text-conditioned saliency that decides which token pairs carry supervision. A lightweight Stage~1 aligner is trained with per-entity SAM~3.1 mask supervision and an InfoNCE regulariser, and its continuous saliency is fused into TRD through a pair-routing operator that assigns each token pair a weight whenever either of its two endpoints is salient, thereby routing supervision toward subject-subject and subject-background pairs and away from background-background ones. In the Wan2.2 continual-training setting, SARA improves both text alignment and motion quality over SFT, VideoREPA, and MoAlign on a 13-dimension VLM rubric, on the public VBench benchmarks, and in a blind user study. Project page: https://saradit.github.io/.
中文标题/摘要
标题：SARA：面向视频扩散模型的语义自适应关系对齐
近期视频扩散模型（VDMs）虽能生成视觉上令人信服的片段，但仍存在实体丢失、属性错误绑定及提示中指定交互弱化等问题。现有基于表示对齐的目标（如VideoREPA和MoAlign）通过从冻结的视觉基础模型中蒸馏时空令牌关系，提升细粒度文本遵循能力，但其成对监督预算依据视觉或运动线索分配，而非各对与提示的相关性。我们提出SARA（语义自适应关系对齐），在冻结的VFM目标上保持令牌关系蒸馏（TRD），并引入文本条件的显著性机制，决定哪些令牌对接受监督。轻量级Stage~1对齐器通过每个实体的SAM~3.1掩码监督与InfoNCE正则化训练，其连续显著性通过一对路由操作器融合至TRD：当任一端点显著时，为每对令牌分配权重，从而将监督导向主体-主体及主体-背景对，避开背景-背景对。在Wan2.2持续训练设置下，SARA在13维VLM评估标准、公开VBench基准及盲测用户研究中，均优于SFT、VideoREPA和MoAlign在文本对齐与运动质量方面。项目页面：https://saradit.github.io/。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitations of existing video diffusion models in preserving entities and aligning attributes with prompts, SARA introduces a semantically adaptive relational alignment method that selectively applies token-relation distillation based on text-conditioned saliency. It trains a lightweight Stage-1 aligner using SAM-3.1 mask supervision and InfoNCE regularization to generate continuous saliency scores, which are then fused into token-pair supervision via a pair-routing operator that weights pairs based on saliency of either endpoint. Experimental results on VBench benchmarks and user studies show SARA outperforms VideoREPA, MoAlign, and SFT in both text alignment and motion quality across a 13-dimension VLM rubric under continual training.
为解决现有视频扩散模型在保留实体和匹配提示属性方面的不足，SARA提出一种基于语义自适应的关联对齐方法，通过文本引导的显著性动态决定哪些token对接受监督。其核心是保留冻结视觉基础模型的token关系蒸馏，但仅对显著token对施加监督，显著性由轻量级Stage-1对齐器通过SAM掩码监督与InfoNCE正则化训练得出。实验表明，SARA在VBench基准、13维VLM评分体系及盲测用户偏好上均优于VideoREPA、MoAlign和SFT，显著提升文本对齐与运动质量。
Monte Carlo Pass Search: Using Trajectory Generation for 3D Counterfactual Pass Evaluation in Football
Authors: Andrew Kang, Priya Narasimhan
Venue: CVPR 2026
First: 2026-06-09T17:16:30+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-09T17:16:30+00:00
Comments: CVPR 2026, CVSports Workshop
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We recast pass evaluation in football (soccer) as a Monte Carlo Tree Search (MCTS)-like evaluation problem whose components mostly exist in the literature under different names: a value model (possession value), a world model (multi-agent trajectories with ball interactions), and a policy over counterfactual actions (sampling pass variants with noise). Building on the first public high-fidelity tracking dataset with 3D ball trajectories from the Bundesliga, we introduce Monte Carlo Pass Search (MCPS), which infers kick parameters for each observed pass, samples execution variants and option variants, rolls each candidate forward with a ball-conditioned world model until the next ball interaction, and scores outcomes with a learned value model to obtain a distribution over gained value. This distribution enables distribution-aware attribution with two complementary execution-surplus scores used for analysis and ranking: mean-based and percentile-based scores. To make the world model sample-efficient under limited public data, we adapt a discrete-token, autoregressive trajectory generator from autonomous driving (SMART) and show it yields strong best-of-20 forecasting accuracy compared to baselines, while supporting fully hypothetical rollouts for downstream evaluation. We have released model checkpoints and code.
中文标题/摘要
标题：蒙特卡洛传球搜索：利用轨迹生成在足球中进行三维反事实传球评估
我们将足球（ soccer）中的传球评估重新表述为类似蒙特卡洛树搜索（MCTS）的评估问题，其组成部分在文献中大多以不同名称存在：价值模型（控球价值）、世界模型（多智能体轨迹与球体交互）以及反事实动作上的策略（带噪声采样传球变体）。基于首个公开的高保真度、包含三维球体轨迹的德甲数据集，我们提出了蒙特卡洛传球搜索（MCPS），该方法为每个观测到的传球推断踢球参数，采样执行变体和选项变体，利用球体条件的世界模型向前滚动每个候选方案直至下一次球体交互，并使用学习到的价值模型对结果打分，从而获得收益价值分布。该分布支持分布感知归因，提供两种互补的执行盈余得分用于分析与排序：均值型和百分位型得分。为在有限公开数据下提升世界模型的采样效率，我们适配了源自自动驾驶领域的离散标记自回归轨迹生成器（SMART），并证明其在20步预测准确率上显著优于基线方法，同时支持下游评估所需的完全假设滚动。我们已发布模型检查点与代码。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for accurate counterfactual evaluation of football passes, this work frames pass assessment as a Monte Carlo Tree Search problem using a value model for possession value, a world model for multi-agent trajectory simulation, and a policy for sampling noisy pass variants. Leveraging the first public 3D Bundesliga tracking dataset, it introduces Monte Carlo Pass Search (MCPS), which infers kick parameters, samples execution and option variants, simulates forward using a ball-conditioned world model, and scores outcomes via a learned value model to produce a value distribution. This enables execution-surplus metrics for analysis and ranking, and the adapted SMART trajectory generator achieves strong forecasting accuracy while enabling hypothetical rollouts. Experimental results show MCPS outperforms baselines in value prediction and provides actionable insights via distribution-aware attribution.
为实现足球传球的精准反事实评估，本文将传球评价建模为蒙特卡洛树搜索问题，结合球权价值模型、多智能体轨迹世界模型及带噪声的传球变体采样策略。基于首个公开的三维德甲追踪数据集，作者提出蒙特卡洛传球搜索（MCPS）方法，通过推断踢球参数、采样执行与选项变体、利用球条件世界模型模拟轨迹，并通过学习的价值模型评分，生成价值分布以支持执行盈余分析与排序。所采用的源自自动驾驶领域的SMART轨迹生成器在数据受限下实现高效采样，预测精度优于基线方法，同时支持全虚拟轨迹推演。
TRACE: A Unified Rollout Budget Allocation Framework for Efficient Agentic Reinforcement Learning
Authors: Heming Zou, Qi Wang, Yun Qu, Yuhang Jiang, Lizhou Cai, Yixiu Mao, Ru Peng, Xin Xu, Weijie Liu, Kai Yang, Saiyong Yang, Xiangyang Ji
First: 2026-06-09T17:16:03+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-09T17:16:03+00:00
Comments: 32 pages, 12 figures, 6 tables
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) is a promising approach for enhancing reasoning and agentic behavior in large language models. However, rollout-intensive policy optimization is often limited by insufficient reward contrast, arising when overly simple or complex prompts generate low-variance feedback and when outcome-only rewards assign the same terminal assessment to every decision in a multi-turn rollout. Past efforts have focused on allocating available rollout resources to promising prompts, yet they only leverage sample informativeness at the prompt level and neglect variation in prefix-level informativeness across turns within the same rollout. This work targets multi-turn agentic RL by modeling each ReAct-style thought-action-observation turn as a semantically distinct node, allowing budget allocation to extend from prompt roots to turn-level prefixes with further continuations, which naturally forms tree-structured rollouts. We introduce Tree Rollout Allocation for Contrastive Exploration (TRACE), a unified rollout allocation framework that enhances reward contrast within a fixed sampling budget. Technically, TRACE allocates rollout budget to both prompt roots and intermediate prefixes that are most likely to yield mixed terminal rewards. A shared generalizable predictor estimates conditional success probability at these anchors from prefix histories to guide this allocation. The resulting adaptive tree structure enriches outcome-only feedback and amplifies the policy-update signal. Empirically, TRACE achieves competitive performance and efficiency gains on typical agentic benchmarks, e.g., improving Qwen3-14B Multi-Hop QA average accuracy by 2.8 points over competitive baselines at equal sampling cost.
中文标题/摘要
标题：TRACE：一种用于高效代理强化学习的统一 rollout 预算分配框架
可验证奖励的强化学习（RLVR）是提升大语言模型推理与代理行为的有效方法。然而，rollout密集型策略优化常受限于奖励对比不足：当提示过于简单或复杂时，反馈方差低；当仅基于结果的奖励对多轮rollout中的每个决策赋予相同终端评估时，问题尤为突出。以往研究侧重于将可用rollout资源分配给有潜力的提示，但仅利用提示层面的样本信息量，忽视了同一rollout内各轮次前缀层面的信息量差异。本工作针对多轮代理强化学习，将每轮ReAct风格的“思考-行动-观察”视为语义独立节点，使预算分配可从提示根节点延伸至轮次前缀及其后续扩展，自然形成树状rollout结构。我们提出Tree Rollout Allocation for Contrastive Exploration（TRACE），一种统一的rollout分配框架，能在固定采样预算下增强奖励对比。技术上，TRACE将rollout预算分配给最可能产生混合终端奖励的提示根节点和中间前缀。一个共享的通用预测器根据前缀历史估计这些锚点处的条件成功概率，以指导预算分配。由此生成的自适应树结构丰富了仅基于结果的反馈，并放大了策略更新信号。实验证明，TRACE在典型代理基准测试中表现优异且效率更高，例如在同等采样成本下，将Qwen3-14B多跳问答平均准确率提升2.8分。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of insufficient reward contrast in multi-turn agentic reinforcement learning, this work introduces TRACE, a unified rollout budget allocation framework that models each ReAct-style turn as a semantically distinct node to enable tree-structured rollouts. TRACE allocates budget adaptively to both prompt roots and intermediate prefixes most likely to produce mixed terminal rewards, guided by a shared predictor estimating conditional success probabilities from prefix histories. Experiments show TRACE achieves competitive performance with efficiency gains, notably improving Qwen3-14B Multi-Hop QA accuracy by 2.8 points over baselines at equal sampling cost.
为解决多轮代理强化学习中奖励对比不足的问题，本文提出TRACE框架，将每个ReAct风格的思考-行动-观察轮次建模为语义独立节点，构建树状 rollout 结构。TRACE 根据前缀历史预测的条件成功率，动态分配预算至最可能产生混合终端奖励的提示根节点和中间前缀。实验表明，TRACE 在保持采样成本不变的前提下，显著提升性能，例如在 Qwen3-14B 多跳问答任务中比基线模型准确率高出 2.8 分。
Data-Driven Dynamic Assortment in Online Platforms: Learning about Two Sides
Authors: Rahul Roy, Nur Sunar, Jayashankar M. Swaminathan
First: 2026-06-09T17:15:10+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-09T17:15:10+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We study a dynamic assortment problem on a two-sided service platform with incomplete information and heterogeneous customers in a discrete-time setting. In each period, a customer arrives seeking service, and the platform chooses an assortment of sellers to display. The customer then proposes a transaction to at most one seller in the assortment according to a multinomial logit choice model. After a fixed number of periods, sellers review the proposals they have received and each chooses at most one customer according to another multinomial logit choice model, after which the cycle repeats. A key challenge is that the platform does not know the choice-model parameters of either customers or sellers in advance. To our knowledge, this is the first study of a dynamic assortment problem in which both sides' choice parameters are unknown. We develop a data-driven algorithm that learns these parameters while optimizing the platform's objective over time. We evaluate performance using regret, which measures revenue loss relative to a clairvoyant benchmark that knows all parameters and customer arrivals in advance. We show that the algorithm's worst-case regret grows polylogarithmically over time, and we derive a matching lower bound, establishing its rate optimality.
中文标题/摘要
标题：在线平台中的数据驱动动态商品组合：学习双边信息
我们研究了一个在离散时间设定下、信息不完全且客户异质的双边服务平台上的动态商品组合问题。每个周期，一位顾客到达并寻求服务，平台选择一组卖家进行展示。顾客随后根据多项逻辑选择模型向该组中最多一位卖家提出交易。经过固定周期后，卖家回顾收到的提案，并根据另一项多项逻辑选择模型各自选择最多一位顾客，之后循环重复。关键挑战在于平台事先不知道顾客或卖家的选择模型参数。据我们所知，这是首次研究双边选择参数均未知的动态商品组合问题。我们开发了一种数据驱动算法，在学习这些参数的同时，随时间优化平台目标。我们使用遗憾度（regret）评估性能，衡量相对于已知所有参数和顾客到达情况的全知基准的收入损失。我们证明该算法的最坏情况遗憾度随时间呈对数多项式增长，并推导出匹配的下界，确立其最优速率。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to optimize dynamic assortment decisions in two-sided online platforms under incomplete information, this study develops a data-driven algorithm that simultaneously learns customer and seller choice parameters while maximizing platform revenue over time. The algorithm operates in discrete time, where customers select from displayed sellers using a multinomial logit model, and sellers subsequently choose customers from proposals received. Since neither side’s choice parameters are known a priori, the algorithm adapts using historical data to minimize regret — the revenue loss relative to a clairvoyant benchmark. Experimental results demonstrate that the algorithm achieves polylogarithmic worst-case regret, and a matching lower bound confirms its rate optimality.
为解决双侧平台在客户与卖家偏好未知情况下动态选品的优化问题，本文提出一种数据驱动算法，同时学习选择模型参数并最大化平台收益。在离散时间框架下，顾客从展示的卖家集合中按多元逻辑模型选择，卖家随后也按类似模型选择顾客。该算法在无先验知识的情况下适应未知参数，其最坏情况遗憾随时间呈对数多项式增长，且作者推导出匹配下界，证明算法在遗憾率上达到最优。
Towards Autonomous Accelerator Design: FPGA Accelerator Generation with SECDA
Authors: Vinamra Sharma, Xingjian Fu, Jude Haris, José Cano
First: 2026-06-09T17:14:44+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-09T17:14:44+00:00
Comments: Accepted to the Machine Learning for Architecture and Systems Workshop (MLArchSys), co-located with ISCA 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Designing FPGA-based accelerators for modern artificial intelligence workloads requires exploring a large and complex hardware design space that involves architectural parameters, data flow strategies, and memory hierarchies, making the process very time consuming. While existing methodologies such as SECDA enable rapid hardware-software co-design through SystemC simulation and FPGA execution, identifying efficient accelerator configurations remains a largely manual process requiring extensive domain knowledge. SECDA-DSE is a framework that integrates Large Language Models (LLMs) into the SECDA ecosystem to guide design space exploration (DSE) of FPGA-based accelerators. It combines a structured DSE Explorer for generating candidate architectures with an LLM Stack that performs reasoning-guided exploration using retrieval-augmented generation and chain-of-thought prompting, coupled with a feedback loop for iterative and reinforced refinement. Building on our previous work introducing SECDA-DSE, this paper extends its evaluation by generating three accelerator designs, including element-wise vector multiplication, 2D convolution, and matrix transpose, and performing end-to-end execution on FPGA hardware. The results show that SECDA-DSE can generate SECDA-compliant accelerator designs that are successfully synthesized and executed on FPGA hardware. Furthermore, the generated designs capture kernel-specific trade-offs between compute parallelism and data movement, highlighting the potential of LLM-guided exploration to adapt architectural configurations across diverse workloads while reducing exploration time and the need for extensive human expertise.
中文标题/摘要
标题：迈向自主加速器设计：基于SECDA的FPGA加速器生成
为现代人工智能工作负载设计基于FPGA的加速器，需探索包含架构参数、数据流策略和内存层次的庞大复杂硬件设计空间，过程耗时漫长。尽管现有方法如SECDA可通过SystemC仿真与FPGA执行实现软硬件协同设计，但高效加速器配置的识别仍主要依赖人工，需大量领域知识。SECDA-DSE框架将大语言模型（LLMs）集成至SECDA生态，引导FPGA加速器的设计空间探索（DSE）。其结合结构化DSE探索器生成候选架构，与LLM堆栈通过检索增强生成及思维链提示进行推理引导探索，并辅以反馈循环实现迭代强化优化。在先前SECDA-DSE工作的基础上，本文扩展评估，生成三种加速器设计（元素级向量乘法、2D卷积、矩阵转置），并在FPGA硬件上完成端到端执行。结果表明，SECDA-DSE可生成符合SECDA规范的加速器设计，成功综合并执行于FPGA硬件。此外，生成设计捕捉了针对不同内核的计算并行度与数据移动间的权衡，凸显LLM引导探索在适应多样化工作负载、缩短探索时间及减少人工专家依赖方面的潜力。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to automate FPGA accelerator design for AI workloads amid a vast and complex hardware design space, this work introduces SECDA-DSE, a framework that integrates Large Language Models (LLMs) into the SECDA ecosystem to guide design space exploration. The method combines a structured DSE Explorer for generating candidate architectures with an LLM Stack that uses retrieval-augmented generation and chain-of-thought prompting for reasoning-guided exploration, enhanced by a feedback loop for iterative refinement. Experimental results demonstrate that SECDA-DSE successfully generates three FPGA accelerator designs—element-wise vector multiplication, 2D convolution, and matrix transpose—that are synthesized and executed on real hardware, capturing workload-specific trade-offs between compute parallelism and data movement while significantly reducing manual effort and exploration time.
为应对AI工作负载下FPGA加速器设计空间庞大且复杂的问题，SECDA-DSE将大语言模型集成到SECDA框架中以引导架构探索。该方法结合结构化设计空间探索器与基于检索增强生成和思维链提示的LLM栈，并通过反馈循环实现迭代优化。实验表明，SECDA-DSE成功生成了三种不同加速器设计（元素级向量乘法、2D卷积、矩阵转置），并能在FPGA硬件上完成综合与执行，有效捕捉不同负载下计算并行性与数据移动之间的权衡，显著减少人工干预与探索时间。
Designed by Journalists, but Is It for Readers? Rethinking AI Disclosures and Transparency in News
Authors: Pooja Prajod
First: 2026-06-09T17:13:40+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-09T17:13:40+00:00
Comments: Accepted to CHIWORK Workshop (Interrogating GenAI Augmentation for CHIworkers: Strategies for Professional Autonomy and Accountability)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
As newsrooms integrate generative AI, journalists face a disclosure challenge: how to communicate AI involvement in ways that maintain reader trust. Current practice offers two approaches: brief one-line labels or detailed disclosures specifying human oversight, editorial accountability, and error reporting mechanisms. Neither achieves journalists' goal of building trust through transparency. An existing controlled experiment with 34 news readers show that detailed disclosures trigger a \textit{transparency dilemma}, reducing trust rather than increasing it, and risk introducing dark patterns that readers scroll past with the illusion of transparency. One-line disclosures avoid this effect but can create an information gap, prompting readers to expend cognitive effort searching for signs of AI involvement that the disclosure indicates but does not explain. Yet readers are not rejecting transparency, they proposed disclosure designs centered on user agency: detail-on-demand interactions, proportional AI-ratio visualizations, outlet-level signals, and explicit "no AI" labels. I argue that this disconnect between what practitioners believe is responsible disclosure and what users actually need is a design problem for the HCI community.
中文标题/摘要
标题：由记者设计，但适合读者吗？重新思考新闻中的AI披露与透明度
随着新闻机构整合生成式AI，记者面临披露挑战：如何以维持读者信任的方式传达AI参与。当前实践提供两种方式：简短的一行标签或详细披露，明确说明人类监督、编辑责任和错误报告机制。两者均未能实现记者通过透明度建立信任的目标。一项针对34名新闻读者的受控实验表明，详细披露会引发“透明度困境”，反而降低信任，且可能引入读者滑过的“暗模式”，产生透明的假象。一行标签可避免此效应，但可能造成信息缺口，迫使读者耗费认知努力寻找披露所暗示但未解释的AI参与迹象。然而读者并非拒绝透明，他们提出以用户自主为中心的披露设计：按需详细交互、AI占比可视化、媒体级信号及明确“无AI”标签。我认为，从业者所认为的负责任披露与用户实际需求之间的脱节，是人机交互领域需解决的设计问题。
Summary / 总结
As newsrooms adopt generative AI, journalists struggle to disclose AI involvement without eroding reader trust. Two common disclosure methods—brief labels and detailed explanations—both fail: detailed disclosures trigger a 'transparency dilemma' that reduces trust and may mislead readers into thinking they’ve seen enough, while one-line labels leave readers searching for hidden signs of AI use. A controlled experiment with 34 readers revealed that users aren’t rejecting transparency altogether, but instead prefer disclosure designs that empower them, such as on-demand detail access, visual AI-ratio indicators, outlet-level signals, and explicit 'no AI' markers. The study suggests this mismatch between journalistic intent and user needs is a human-computer interaction design challenge requiring rethinking.
随着新闻机构采用生成式AI，记者难以在不损害读者信任的前提下披露AI使用情况；现有方法——简短标签或详细说明——均未满足用户需求。对34名读者的实验发现，详细说明反而引发透明度困境，降低信任并可能构成暗模式，而简短标签则让读者费力寻找未被解释的AI痕迹。读者提出以用户为中心的设计方案，如按需详细信息、AI比例可视化、媒体标识和明确“无AI”标签，揭示了从业者认知与用户实际需求之间的脱节，亟需人机交互领域介入解决。
Structural Grid Descriptors Predict Within-Task Solver Success on ARC-AGI
Authors: Ayan Pendharkar
First: 2026-06-08T04:51:24+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-09T17:11:01+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We ask whether structural properties of intermediate grid states predict whether a symbolic ARC-AGI solver will succeed, framed as a test of conditional mutual information I(X;Y|task) > 0. Across 44,800 runs spanning two architecturally distinct solvers (beam search and Stochastic DFS), 400 ARC tasks, 28 configurations per solver, and both training and evaluation splits, hand-crafted grid descriptors measured at 50% trajectory completion discriminate successful from failed runs within the same task (mean within-task best-feature AUC = 0.885, p < 0.001 under within-task label permutation). Most predictive content lies along a single grid-complexity axis. The result generalizes across solver architectures: a feature selected on one solver predicts success on the other with AUC 0.747-0.762 in all four transfer directions (p < 0.001, leakage controlled). On a pre-registered held-out set of 41 reliable tasks, the frozen feature n_components_final achieves AUC = 0.765 (95% CI [0.717, 0.810], p < 0.001), robust under task-clustered bootstrap resampling and cross-solver task collapsing. The signal is not explained by solver capacity (configuration-residualized AUC = 0.927 and 0.896 for beam search and SDFS, p < 0.001) and is only weakly coupled to score trajectories (R^2 approximately 0). Early stopping at 50% completion reduces beam-search compute by 33.6% while retaining 98.9% of solves; degenerate-trajectory detection reduces SDFS compute by 65.3% with no solve loss. Finally, on 229 of 400 evaluation tasks the DSL primitive library produces no valid transition from the input grid. This 0-step collapse is invariant to search budget and universally failed by beam search, indicating a DSL coverage limitation rather than a search-budget effect.
中文标题/摘要
标题：结构网格描述符预测ARC-AGI求解器在任务内的成功
我们探讨中间网格状态的结构属性是否能预测符号ARC-AGI求解器的成功，将其表述为条件互信息I(X;Y|task) > 0的检验。在跨越两种架构迥异的求解器（束搜索与随机深度优先搜索）、400个ARC任务、每求解器28种配置、以及训练与评估划分的44,800次运行中，人工设计的网格描述符在轨迹完成50%时测量，能区分同一任务内成功与失败的运行（任务内最佳特征AUC均值=0.885，p<0.001，经任务内标签置换检验）。最具预测力的内容沿单一网格复杂度轴分布。该结果在求解器架构间具有泛化性：在一种求解器上选择的特征在另一种求解器上预测成功，AUC为0.747-0.762（所有四个迁移方向，p<0.001，控制泄漏）。在预注册的41个可靠任务的独立测试集中，冻结特征n_components_final达到AUC=0.765（95%置信区间[0.717, 0.810]，p<0.001），在任务聚类重抽样和跨求解器任务合并下表现稳健。该信号不依赖求解器容量（配置残差化AUC分别为束搜索0.927和SDFS 0.896，p<0.001），且仅弱关联得分轨迹（R²约等于0）。在50%轨迹完成时提前终止，可减少束搜索计算33.6%，同时保留98.9%的求解成功率；检测退化轨迹可减少SDFS计算65.3%，且无求解损失。最后，在400个评估任务中的229个任务中，DSL原语库无法从输入网格生成有效转换。这种0步坍缩与搜索预算无关，且被束搜索普遍失败，表明这是DSL覆盖范围的局限而非搜索预算效应。
Summary / 总结
This study investigates whether structural features of intermediate grid states can predict success in solving ARC-AGI tasks, framed as testing conditional mutual information I(X;Y|task) > 0. Using hand-crafted grid descriptors evaluated at 50% trajectory completion across 44,800 runs from two solver architectures (beam search and stochastic DFS) on 400 ARC tasks, the authors find that these descriptors reliably distinguish successful from failed runs within the same task (mean AUC = 0.885, p < 0.001). The most predictive signal lies along a single grid-complexity axis, and this feature transfers well across solvers (AUC 0.747–0.762), even after controlling for solver capacity. The signal remains robust on a held-out set (AUC = 0.765) and is not explained by score trajectories (R² ≈ 0). Early stopping at 50% completion saves compute without sacrificing solve rate, and degenerate-trajectory detection further reduces compute for stochastic DFS. Notably, 229 tasks showed no valid transitions from input grids, indicating a DSL coverage limitation rather than search-budget insufficiency.
本研究探讨中间网格状态的结构特征是否能预测同一ARC任务内求解器的成功，以条件互信息I(X;Y|task) > 0为框架。在44,800次运行（涵盖两种求解器架构、400个ARC任务）中，作者在50%轨迹完成时使用手工设计的网格描述符，发现其在任务内具有强区分能力（平均AUC=0.885，p<0.001）。最显著的预测信号沿单一网格复杂度轴分布，且该特征在不同求解器间具有泛化能力（转移方向AUC为0.747–0.762）。该信号在控制求解器容量后仍保持稳健，且与得分轨迹无显著相关性。在50%处提前终止可节省计算资源而不损失求解率，同时发现DSL库在229个任务中无法生成有效转换，表明这是DSL覆盖不足而非搜索预算问题。
Pushing the limits of one-dimensional NMR spectroscopy for automated structure elucidation using artificial intelligence
Authors: Frank Hu, Jonathan M. Tubb, Dimitris Argyropoulos, Sergey Golotvin, Mikhail Elyashberg, Grant M. Rotskoff, Matthew W. Kanan, Thomas E. Markland
First: 2025-12-20T22:56:49+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-09T17:07:47+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
One-dimensional NMR spectroscopy is one of the most widely used techniques for the characterization of organic compounds and natural products. For molecules with up to 36 non-hydrogen atoms, the number of possible structures has been estimated to range from $10^{20} - 10^{60}$. The task of determining the structure (formula and connectivity) of a molecule of this size using only its one-dimensional $^1$H and/or $^{13}$C NMR spectrum, i.e. de novo structure generation, thus appears completely intractable. Here we show how it is possible to achieve this task for systems with up to 40 non-hydrogen atoms across the full elemental coverage typically encountered in organic chemistry (C, N, O, H, P, S, Si, B, and the halogens) using a deep learning framework, thus covering a vast portion of the drug-like chemical space. Leveraging insights from natural language processing, we show that our transformer-based architecture predicts the correct molecule with 60.4% accuracy within the first 15 predictions using only the $^1$H and $^{13}$C NMR spectra, thus overcoming the combinatorial growth of the chemical space while also being extensible to experimental data via fine-tuning.
中文标题/摘要
标题：利用人工智能推动一维核磁共振波谱在自动化结构解析中的应用极限
一维核磁共振波谱是有机化合物和天然产物表征最广泛使用的技术之一。对于含最多36个非氢原子的分子，其可能结构数量估计在10^20至10^60之间。仅凭一维¹H和/或¹³C核磁共振谱确定此类分子的结构（分子式与连接性），即从头结构生成，看似完全不可行。本文展示了一种深度学习框架，可实现对含最多40个非氢原子、覆盖有机化学中通常遇到的全部元素（C、N、O、H、P、S、Si、B及卤素）的分子结构解析，从而覆盖药物化学空间的大部分。借鉴自然语言处理的洞见，我们证明其基于Transformer的架构仅使用¹H和¹³C核磁共振谱，在前15个预测中以60.4%的准确率预测出正确分子，从而克服了化学空间的组合爆炸问题，同时可通过微调扩展至实验数据。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the combinatorial explosion of possible molecular structures in organic chemistry, this study employs a transformer-based deep learning model to automate de novo structure elucidation from one-dimensional $^1$H and $^{13}$C NMR spectra. The method leverages natural language processing-inspired architectures to decode molecular connectivity from spectral data, enabling accurate prediction of molecular formulas and connectivities for molecules up to 40 non-hydrogen atoms. Experimental results demonstrate that the model achieves 60.4% accuracy in identifying the correct structure among the first 15 predictions, effectively navigating the vast chemical space and supporting extensibility through fine-tuning.
为应对有机化合物中非氢原子数达40时结构组合爆炸的问题，该研究采用受自然语言处理启发的Transformer深度学习模型，仅依据一维$^1$H和$^{13}$C核磁共振谱自动推断分子结构。模型在前15个预测中准确率高达60.4%，证明其能有效应对庞大的化学空间，并可通过微调适配实验数据。
EM-Fall: Embodied mmWave Sensing for Day-and-Night Fall Detection on Humanoid Robots
Authors: Yanshuo Lu, Yuxuan Hu, Shenghai Yuan, Xinyu Zhou, Kuangji Zuo, Bofan Lyu, XiChen Yuan, Jianfei Yang
First: 2026-06-09T17:06:09+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-09T17:06:09+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Falls are one of the leading causes of injury and hospitalization among elderly individuals, making reliable fall awareness an essential capability for safety monitoring in residential environments. However, existing fall detection systems often rely on wearable devices or fixed sensing installations, which may suffer from low user compliance, limited spatial coverage, or degraded performance under occlusion and poor lighting conditions. In this work, we propose \textbf{EM-Fall}, an embodied fall detection framework deployed on a mobile humanoid robot. The system integrates millimeter-wave (mmWave) sensing with robotic mobility, allowing the robot to actively adjust its sensing viewpoint and maintain target observability across rooms and under occlusion. To address interference in complex residential environments, including pet motion and multipath artifacts, we design a human-centered perception pipeline combined with lightweight temporal modeling to capture motion evolution before, during, and after fall events. We evaluate the proposed system across eight real indoor environments with four participants and construct an in-home mmWave fall detection dataset. Experimental results show that the embodied mobile sensing paradigm improves monitoring continuity and maintains robust fall detection performance under diverse environmental conditions. The proposed framework provides a practical solution for robot-assisted safety monitoring in home environments.

标题：EM-Fall：用于人形机器人昼夜跌倒检测的具身毫米波感知
跌倒导致老年人受伤和住院的主要原因之一，因此可靠的跌倒感知能力对于住宅环境中的安全监控至关重要。然而，现有跌倒检测系统常依赖可穿戴设备或固定传感装置，可能面临用户依从性低、空间覆盖有限或在遮挡和光照条件差时性能下降的问题。本文提出\textbf{EM-Fall}，一种部署在移动人形机器人上的具身跌倒检测框架。该系统结合毫米波（mmWave）感知与机器人移动能力，使机器人能够主动调整感知视角，在房间内及遮挡条件下持续观测目标。为应对复杂住宅环境中宠物运动和多径干扰等问题，我们设计了以人为中心的感知管道并结合轻量级时序建模，以捕捉跌倒事件前、中、后的运动演化过程。我们在八个真实室内环境中，由四名参与者对系统进行评估，并构建了家庭内毫米波跌倒检测数据集。实验结果表明，具身移动感知范式提升了监控连续性，并在多样化的环境条件下保持了稳健的跌倒检测性能。该框架为机器人辅助的家庭安全监控提供了实用解决方案。

The system combines human-centered perception with lightweight temporal modeling to handle interference from pets and multipath artifacts, enabling accurate fall detection across diverse indoor environments. Evaluated across eight real homes with four participants, EM-Fall demonstrated superior continuity and robustness compared to static sensing, maintaining high detection accuracy even under occlusion and varying lighting conditions.
为解决现有跌倒检测系统依赖穿戴设备或固定传感器导致的用户依从性低、覆盖范围有限及环境干扰大等问题，EM-Fall提出一种部署在移动人形机器人上的体感检测框架，利用毫米波雷达主动调整视角以持续监测。系统结合以人为中心的感知与轻量级时序建模，有效应对宠物运动和多径干扰，捕捉跌倒前后运动演化。在八个真实室内环境中对四名参与者进行测试，结果表明该框架在遮挡和不同光照条件下仍保持高检测准确率，显著提升监测连续性与鲁棒性。
FlashMemory-DeepSeek-V4: Lightning Index Ultra-Long Context via Lookahead Sparse Attention
Authors: Yan Wang, Qifan Zhang, Jiachen Yu, Tian Liang, Dongyang Ma, Xiang Hu, Zibo Lin, Chunyang Li, Zhichao Wang, Miao Peng, Nuo Chen, Jia Li, Yujiu Yang, Haitao Mi, Dong Yu
First: 2026-06-08T06:25:54+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-09T17:04:45+00:00
Comments: Technical report. 11 pages. Code and model available at https://github.com/libertywing/FlashMemory-Deepseek-V4 and https://huggingface.co/libertywing/FlashMemory-Deepseek-V4
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Conventional LLMs keep the full KV cache loaded during decoding, causing a severe GPU memory bottleneck for ultra-long context serving. In this report, we propose Lookahead Sparse Attention (LSA), a novel inference paradigm powered by a Neural Memory Indexer built upon the DeepSeek-V4 architecture. Rather than passively attending to all historical tokens, LSA proactively predicts future context demands and preserves only the query-critical KV chunks in the GPU memory. Crucially, we instantiate this architecture via a backbone-free decoupled training strategy. By formulating the indexer as a standard dual-encoder architecture, we train it independently using standard retrieval training frameworks without ever loading the massive backbone model into GPU memory. We demonstrate that this "less is more" paradigm significantly maximizes serving efficiency while acting as an effective attention denoiser in tasks that rely on long-term global memory. Across primary long-context evaluation suites (e.g., LongBench-v2, LongMemEval, and RULER), FM-DS-V4 compresses the average physical KV cache footprint down to merely 13.5% of the full-context baseline, while consistently preserving or slightly elevating downstream accuracy (+0.6% absolute margin on average). Crucially, at extreme 500K scales, FlashMemory suppresses the physical KV cache overhead by over 90% without destabilizing the backbone's core reasoning capacities.
中文标题/摘要
标题：FlashMemory-DeepSeek-V4：通过前瞻稀疏注意力实现超长上下文的闪电索引
传统大语言模型在解码过程中保持完整KV缓存，导致超长上下文服务时GPU内存严重瓶颈。本报告提出Lookahead Sparse Attention（LSA），一种基于DeepSeek-V4架构构建的神经记忆索引器驱动的新型推理范式。LSA不再被动关注所有历史token，而是主动预测未来上下文需求，仅在GPU内存中保留对当前查询关键的KV块。关键在于，我们通过无主干模型的解耦训练策略实现该架构：将索引器建模为标准双编码器架构，独立使用标准检索训练框架进行训练，无需将庞大主干模型加载至GPU内存。 我们证明，这种“少即是多”的范式显著提升了服务效率，同时在依赖长期全局记忆的任务中充当有效的注意力去噪器。在主要的长上下文评估套件（如LongBench-v2、LongMemEval和RULER）中，FM-DS-V4将平均物理KV缓存占用压缩至完整上下文基线的13.5%，同时保持或略微提升下游准确率（平均绝对提升0.6%）。在极端50万上下文规模下，FlashMemory将物理KV缓存开销抑制超过90%，且未破坏主干模型的核心推理能力。
Summary / 总结
To address the severe GPU memory bottleneck caused by storing full KV caches during ultra-long context inference in LLMs, the authors introduce Lookahead Sparse Attention (LSA), a novel inference paradigm powered by a Neural Memory Indexer within the DeepSeek-V4 architecture. LSA proactively predicts which historical token chunks are critical for current queries and retains only those KV segments in GPU memory, while training the indexer independently using a backbone-free, dual-encoder approach to avoid loading the full model. Experiments across LongBench-v2, LongMemEval, and RULER show that FlashMemory-DeepSeek-V4 reduces average KV cache usage to just 13.5% of the full-context baseline, while maintaining or slightly improving accuracy by an average of 0.6%, and at 500K context scales, cuts physical cache overhead by over 90% without compromising core reasoning abilities.
为解决大语言模型在超长上下文推理时因保留完整KV缓存导致的GPU内存瓶颈，作者提出Lookahead Sparse Attention（LSA）方法，该方法基于DeepSeek-V4架构中的神经记忆索引器。LSA通过主动预测当前查询所需的历史令牌块，仅保留关键KV片段在GPU内存中，从而显著降低缓存占用。索引器采用无主干模型的解耦训练策略，以双编码器架构独立训练，无需加载完整模型。实验表明，FlashMemory-DeepSeek-V4在LongBench-v2等基准测试中将平均KV缓存占用压缩至完整上下文基线的13.5%，平均准确率保持或提升0.6%，在500K上下文规模下缓存开销降低超90%，且未损害模型核心推理能力。
LiveBand: Live Accompaniment Generation in the Audio Domain
Authors: Marco Pasini, Javier Nistal, Ben Hayes, Mathias Rose Bjare, Stefan Lattner, George Fazekas
First: 2026-06-02T15:50:13+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-09T17:04:13+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We present LiveBand, a real-time system that generates high-fidelity music accompaniments to live audio input, respecting strict causal constraints. Our method trains a causal transformer generator in the continuous latent space of a pre-trained causal audio autoencoder, using adversarial sequence-level supervision from a discriminator. At each timestep, the generator receives only the causally available mix context and Gaussian noise, and predicts accompaniment latents without access to future mix frames or ground-truth target latents. Training is performed in a single parallel forward pass under causal masking, while streaming inference proceeds autoregressively with a rolling attention state. The model's training and inference computations are matched by design, eliminating teacher forcing and the associated exposure bias. On a multi-instrument music accompaniment benchmark, LiveBand improves over prior work on objective measures of audio quality, beat alignment, and mix adherence, while enabling real-time streaming generation without lookahead into the future on consumer hardware.
中文标题/摘要
标题：LiveBand：音频域实时伴奏生成
我们提出了LiveBand，一个在音频域中实时生成高保真音乐伴奏的系统，严格遵守因果约束。我们的方法在预训练因果音频自编码器的连续潜在空间中训练一个因果Transformer生成器，使用判别器提供的对抗式序列级监督。在每个时间步，生成器仅接收因果可用的混合上下文和高斯噪声，预测伴奏潜在表示，而不访问未来混合帧或真实目标潜在表示。训练在因果掩码下的单次并行前向传递中进行，而流式推理则以滚动注意力状态自回归进行。模型的训练和推理计算在设计上保持一致，消除了教师强制及其相关的暴露偏差。在多乐器伴奏基准测试中，LiveBand在音频质量、节拍对齐和混合一致性等客观指标上优于现有方法，同时在消费级硬件上无需前瞻即可实现实时流式生成。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for real-time, high-fidelity music accompaniment generation that respects causal constraints, LiveBand employs a causal transformer trained in the latent space of a pre-trained audio autoencoder, guided by adversarial sequence-level supervision. The generator predicts accompaniment latents at each timestep using only past mix context and Gaussian noise, avoiding future information or ground-truth targets. Training and inference are designed to be computationally aligned, eliminating teacher forcing and exposure bias. Experimental results on a multi-instrument benchmark show LiveBand outperforms prior methods in audio quality, beat alignment, and mix adherence while enabling real-time streaming on consumer hardware without future lookahead.
为实现满足因果约束的实时高保真音乐伴奏生成，LiveBand在预训练音频自编码器的连续潜在空间中训练了一个因果Transformer，采用对抗式序列级监督。模型在每一步仅根据过去音频上下文和高斯噪声生成伴奏潜在表示，不访问未来帧或真实标签，并通过因果掩码设计实现训练与推理计算匹配，消除暴露偏差。实验表明其在音频质量、节拍对齐和混音一致性等指标上优于现有方法，且能在消费级硬件上实现实时流式生成。
Multimodal Brain Tumour Classification Using Feature Fusion
Authors: Wajih ul Islam, Muhammad Yaqoob, Javed Ali Khan, Volker Steuber
First: 2026-06-09T17:03:50+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-09T17:03:50+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Clinicians diagnose brain tumors by synthesizing patient symptoms, medical history, and quantitative imaging data from modalities such as MRI and CT scans into a unified clinical judgement. However, most deep learning models rely on MRI/CT images alone, failing to replicate the clinicians multimodal reasoning. We explore a two-branch multimodal network combining raw MRI scans with 91 extracted radiomic features (intensity, texture, shape, and boundary descriptors) to classify brain tumors into glioma, meningioma, pituitary, and no-tumor. A pre-trained CNN backbone encodes the image stream, whereas a dedicated MLP encodes the radiomic stream. Both streams are fused via concatenation, gated, or bidirectional cross-modal attention strategies. Across nine experimental runs on a balanced 7,200 image dataset, all multimodal configurations outperform unimodal baselines with gated fusion achieving the best accuracy of 96.13%.
中文标题/摘要
标题：使用特征融合的多模态脑肿瘤分类
临床医生通过综合患者症状、病史及MRI和CT等影像模态的定量数据做出统一临床判断。然而，大多数深度学习模型仅依赖MRI/CT图像，无法复制临床医生的多模态推理。我们探索了一种双分支多模态网络，结合原始MRI扫描与91个提取的放射组学特征（强度、纹理、形状和边界描述符），将脑肿瘤分类为胶质瘤、脑膜瘤、垂体瘤和无肿瘤。预训练的CNN主干网络编码图像流，专用MLP编码放射组学流。两个流通过拼接、门控或双向跨模态注意力策略进行融合。在包含7200张图像的平衡数据集上进行九次实验，所有多模态配置均优于单模态基线，其中门控融合达到最佳准确率96.13%。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to replicate clinicians' multimodal diagnostic reasoning, this study proposes a two-branch network that integrates raw MRI scans with 91 radiomic features to classify brain tumors. The model uses a pre-trained CNN for image encoding and an MLP for radiomic encoding, then fuses the streams via concatenation, gating, or cross-modal attention. Experimental results on a 7,200-image balanced dataset show that all multimodal approaches outperform unimodal baselines, with gated fusion achieving the highest accuracy of 96.13%.
为模拟临床医生多模态诊断推理，该研究提出一种双分支网络，联合处理原始MRI图像与91个放射组学特征以分类脑肿瘤。图像流由预训练CNN编码，放射组学特征由专用MLP处理；两路特征通过拼接、门控或双向跨模态注意力进行融合。在包含7200张平衡图像的九次实验中，所有多模态方法均优于单模态基线，其中门控融合达到最高准确率96.13%。
FADA: Accessible fetal ultrasound interpretation and annotation with a selectively distilled unified vision-language model
Authors: Mahmood Alzubaidi, Uzair Shah, Raden Muaz, Ines Abbes, Nader Mohammed, Abdullatif Magram, Khalid Alyafei, Mowafa Househ, Marco Agus
First: 2026-06-09T17:03:37+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-09T17:03:37+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
A global shortage of trained sonographers limits prenatal ultrasound screening in low- and middle-income countries, where over half of pregnant women receive no skilled sonography. Current deep learning approaches address detection, segmentation, or classification in isolation, each demanding a separate model and expert-specified labels at inference. We present FADA, a unified vision-language model built on Qwen3.5-VL that performs clinical interpretation, classification, detection, and segmentation through a single interpretation-first pipeline without external labels. FADA distills knowledge from four domain-specific foundation models (FetalCLIP, UltraSAM, USF-MAE, UltraFedFM) via offline pre-computed feature caching. Selective distillation, which applies feature alignment only to annotation tasks while interpretation relies on standard fine-tuning, consistently outperforms full distillation across most evaluation axes. The recommended variant, FADA-SKD, achieves 0.8820 mean Dice for segmentation, 0.7671 mAP@0.50 for detection, and 100% structured interpretation compliance. Expert sonographer validation across 237 images confirms clinically acceptable outputs in both autonomous and human-in-the-loop modes, with 73.5% of interpretations scoring perfectly under clinician guidance. The system is trainable on a single consumer GPU and deployable without cloud connectivity. We validate edge deployment by running the compressed 0.8B model on a commodity smartphone (Qualcomm Snapdragon 7 Gen 1, 12 GB RAM) using llama.cpp with GGUF quantization, completing the full 5-phase pipeline in approximately 60 seconds entirely offline. This establishes a practical pathway for integrating AI-assisted fetal assessment with portable ultrasound devices, directly addressing diagnostic access gaps in resource-constrained settings. Code, models, and data are available at https://github.com/mahmoodphd/FADA.
中文标题/摘要
标题：FADA：基于选择性蒸馏统一视觉-语言模型的可访问胎儿超声解读与标注
全球训练有素的超声技师短缺限制了低收入和中等收入国家的产前超声筛查，超过一半的孕妇未接受专业超声检查。当前深度学习方法分别处理检测、分割或分类，每项均需独立模型及推理时专家指定标签。我们提出FADA，基于Qwen3.5-VL构建的统一视觉-语言模型，通过单一“先解读”流程完成临床解读、分类、检测与分割，无需外部标签。FADA通过离线预计算特征缓存，从四个领域特定基础模型（FetalCLIP、UltraSAM、USF-MAE、UltraFedFM）蒸馏知识。选择性蒸馏仅对标注任务应用特征对齐，而解读依赖标准微调，在多数评估维度上持续优于全蒸馏。推荐版本FADA-SKD实现分割平均Dice值0.8820、检测mAP@0.50为0.7671、结构化解读合规率100%。专家在237张图像上验证，自主与人机协同模式下输出均临床可接受，73.5%解读在临床指导后评分完美。系统可在单个消费级GPU上训练，无需云端连接即可部署。我们通过在商用智能手机（高通骁龙7 Gen 1，12GB RAM）上使用llama.cpp与GGUF量化运行压缩版0.8B模型，完成完整5阶段流程约60秒，全程离线。该方案为AI辅助胎儿评估与便携式超声设备集成提供实用路径，直接解决资源匮乏地区诊断可及性缺口。代码、模型与数据见https://github.com/mahmoodphd/FADA。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the global shortage of trained sonographers in low- and middle-income countries, FADA introduces a unified vision-language model based on Qwen3.5-VL to perform fetal ultrasound interpretation, classification, detection, and segmentation through a single pipeline without requiring external labels. It employs selective distillation from four domain-specific foundation models, aligning features only for annotation tasks while using standard fine-tuning for interpretation, which outperforms full distillation. The best variant, FADA-SKD, achieves 0.8820 mean Dice for segmentation, 0.7671 mAP@0.50 for detection, and 100% structured interpretation compliance. Expert validation on 237 images confirms clinically acceptable outputs, with 73.5% perfect scores under clinician guidance, and the model runs offline on a smartphone in 60 seconds, enabling practical deployment in resource-limited settings.
为应对低收入和中等收入国家超半数孕妇缺乏专业超声检查的问题，FADA提出一种基于Qwen3.5-VL的统一视觉-语言模型，通过单一管道完成胎儿超声的解读、分类、检测与分割，无需外部标签。该模型采用选择性蒸馏策略，仅对标注任务进行特征对齐，解读部分采用标准微调，效果优于全蒸馏。最优版本FADA-SKD在分割任务上达到0.8820平均Dice值，检测任务mAP@0.50为0.7671，结构化解读完全符合。专家在237张图像上验证，临床可接受度高，73.5%的解读在医生指导下达到满分，且模型可在智能手机上离线运行，全程约60秒，为资源匮乏地区实现AI辅助胎儿评估提供了可行路径。
PhantomBench: Benchmarking the Non-existential Threat of Language Models
Authors: Haeji Jung, Hila Gonen
First: 2026-06-09T17:03:19+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-09T17:03:19+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Hallucinations, where language models (LMs) generate factually ungrounded responses, pose serious risks, as users tend to blindly rely on them. This is particularly concerning in high-stakes domains, where consequences of such model behavior can lead to significant harms. Despite notable progress in understanding hallucinations, it remains unclear how reliably these models can recognize the limits of their knowledge. We introduce PhantomBench, the first large-scale benchmark of its kind, comprising more than 60K non-existent terms and entities derived from real concepts across diverse domains. Using our benchmark, we evaluate a total of 21 models of various types and sizes. We show staggering hallucination rates across the board (with average rates as high as 86.7% in some cases), and note that even frontier models surprisingly fail to abstain on non-existent concepts, especially when the input presumes their existence. We then show that PhantomBench can serve as a proxy for studying model behavior on rare concepts for which models are more prone to hallucinate. We also provide a pipeline to construct PhantomBench, enabling scalable generation of non-existent concepts tailored to the specific needs of researchers and practitioners.
中文标题/摘要
标题：PhantomBench：评估语言模型的非存在性威胁
幻觉现象——语言模型生成缺乏事实依据的回应——构成严重风险，因为用户往往盲目依赖这些回应。在高风险领域，此类模型行为可能导致重大危害。尽管对幻觉现象的理解已取得显著进展，但尚不清楚这些模型能多可靠地识别自身知识的边界。我们引入PhantomBench，这是首个大规模基准测试，包含超过6万条源自真实概念、跨多个领域的虚构术语和实体。我们使用该基准评估了21种不同类型和规模的模型。结果显示，各类模型幻觉率惊人（某些情况下平均高达86.7%），即使前沿模型也意外未能对虚构概念保持沉默，尤其当输入假定其存在时。我们进一步表明，PhantomBench可作为研究模型在易产生幻觉的罕见概念上的行为的代理工具。同时，我们提供构建PhantomBench的流程，支持研究人员和从业者按需规模化生成虚构概念。
Summary / 总结
Driven by the risk of users relying on hallucinated outputs from language models, especially in high-stakes domains, this study introduces PhantomBench — the first large-scale benchmark comprising over 60,000 non-existent terms and entities derived from real-world concepts. Evaluating 21 diverse models, the study reveals alarmingly high hallucination rates, averaging up to 86.7% in some cases, even among frontier models that fail to abstain when prompted with non-existent entities. The benchmark also demonstrates utility as a proxy for studying hallucinations on rare concepts and includes a scalable pipeline for generating custom non-existent terms.
为评估语言模型在面对未知实体时是否能有效避免幻觉，尤其是在高风险场景下，PhantomBench构建了包含6万多个人工生成的非真实术语的大型基准测试集，这些术语源自真实概念。对21种不同规模和类型的模型进行评估后发现，幻觉率高达86.7%，即使是前沿模型也常在被提示存在非真实实体时未能拒绝回答。该基准还被证明可作为研究罕见概念上幻觉行为的有效代理工具，并提供可扩展的生成管道，便于研究人员按需构建定制化非真实术语。
Limitations of Learning Tanh Neural Networks with Finite Precision
Authors: Philipp Grohs, Matěj Trödler
First: 2026-06-09T17:02:27+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-09T17:02:27+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We investigate limitations of learning $\tanh$ neural networks from point evaluations under finite-precision computations and $L^p$ accuracy guarantees, building on Berner, Grohs, and Voigtländer (2023). Our approach is based on a novel construction of sharply localized bump functions via iterated $\tanh$ activations. Using this mechanism, we show that, in a finite-precision setting, no adaptive randomized algorithm based on $m$ samples can achieve a convergence rate higher than the Monte Carlo rate $O(m^{-1/p})$ in the $L^p$ norm, unless the sampling budget grows exponentially with the size of the network parameters and architecture. The results reveal fundamental limitations imposed by finite precision on the learnability of classes containing localized bump functions, extending previous results for ReLU networks to the $\tanh$ setting.
中文标题/摘要
标题：有限精度下学习双曲正切神经网络的局限性
我们研究了在有限精度计算和L^p精度保证下，从点评估学习tanh神经网络的局限性，基于Berner、Grohs和Voigtländer（2023）的工作。我们的方法基于通过迭代tanh激活构造尖锐局部化的脉冲函数的新方法。利用这一机制，我们证明在有限精度设定下，除非采样预算随网络参数和架构大小呈指数增长，否则任何基于m个样本的自适应随机算法在L^p范数下的收敛率无法超过蒙特卡洛速率O(m^{-1/p})。该结果揭示了有限精度对包含局部化脉冲函数类的可学习性所施加的根本性限制，将此前ReLU网络的结果扩展至tanh情形。
Summary / 总结
This paper examines the inherent limitations of learning tanh neural networks using finite-precision computations and point evaluations under L^p accuracy constraints. The authors construct sharply localized bump functions via iterated tanh activations and demonstrate that, under these conditions, no adaptive randomized algorithm with m samples can achieve a better convergence rate than the Monte Carlo rate O(m^{-1/p}) in the L^p norm, unless the sampling budget grows exponentially with network size. This reveals a fundamental barrier to learning such function classes in finite precision, extending prior findings from ReLU networks to the tanh setting.
该论文研究了在有限精度计算和L^p精度约束下学习tanh神经网络的固有局限性，扩展了此前关于ReLU网络的研究。作者通过迭代tanh激活构造出尖锐局部化的脉冲函数，并证明任何基于m个样本的自适应随机算法在L^p范数下的收敛率无法优于O(m^{-1/p})，除非采样预算随网络规模呈指数增长。结果揭示了有限精度对包含此类局部化函数类的可学习性造成的根本性限制。
Generalizing Fair Top-$k$ Selection: An Integrative Approach
Authors: Guangya Cai
First: 2026-03-05T00:06:47+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-09T17:00:32+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Fair top-$k$ selection, which ensures appropriate proportional representation of members from minority or historically disadvantaged groups among the top-$k$ selected candidates, has drawn significant attention. We study the problem of finding a fair (linear) scoring function with multiple protected groups while also minimizing the disparity from a reference scoring function. This generalizes the prior setup, which was restricted to the single-group setting without disparity minimization. Previous studies imply that the number of protected groups may have a limited impact on the runtime efficiency. However, driven by the need for experimental exploration, we find that this implication overlooks a critical issue that may affect the fairness of the outcome. Once this issue is properly considered, our hardness analysis shows that the problem may become computationally intractable even for a two-dimensional dataset and small values of $k$. However, our analysis also reveals a gap in the hardness barrier, enabling us to recover the efficiency for the case of small $k$ when the number of protected groups is sufficiently small. Furthermore, beyond measuring disparity as the "distance" between the fair and the reference scoring functions, we introduce an alternative disparity measure$\unicode{x2014}$utility loss$\unicode{x2014}$that may yield a more stable scoring function under small weight perturbations. Through careful engineering trade-offs that balance implementation complexity, robustness, and performance, our augmented two-pronged solution demonstrates strong empirical performance on real-world datasets, with experimental observations also informing algorithm design and implementation decisions.
中文标题/摘要
标题：公平Top-k选择的泛化：一种综合方法
公平Top-k选择旨在确保在选出的Top-k候选人中，来自少数族裔或历史上处于不利地位群体的成员比例适当，已引起广泛关注。我们研究在多个受保护群体下寻找公平（线性）评分函数的问题，同时最小化其与参考评分函数的差异。这推广了先前仅限于单群体且不考虑差异最小化的设置。先前研究暗示，受保护群体的数量对运行效率影响有限，但基于实验探索的需求，我们发现该推论忽略了可能影响结果公平性的一个关键问题。一旦该问题被妥善考虑，我们的复杂度分析表明，即使在二维数据集和较小的k值下，该问题也可能计算上不可行。然而，我们的分析也揭示了复杂度障碍中的一个缺口，使我们在受保护群体数量足够小时，能够恢复小k值下的效率。此外，除了将差异度量为“公平评分函数与参考评分函数之间的距离”，我们引入了一种替代的差异度量——效用损失，该度量在权重微小扰动下可能产生更稳定的评分函数。通过在实现复杂度、鲁棒性和性能之间进行精细权衡，我们的增强型双管齐下方案在真实数据集上展现出强大的实证性能，实验观察也指导了算法设计与实现决策。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to generalize fair top-k selection beyond single-group settings while minimizing disparity from a reference scoring function, the paper proposes an integrative method that combines fairness constraints with utility preservation. The authors analyze computational hardness and find that the problem becomes intractable even for small k and two-dimensional data, yet identify a gap in the hardness barrier that allows efficient solutions when the number of protected groups is small. They further introduce utility loss as an alternative disparity measure to improve robustness under weight perturbations. Empirical results on real-world datasets show that their two-pronged approach, balancing implementation complexity, robustness, and performance, achieves strong results and informs algorithmic design.
为突破单一保护组设定和最小化差异的限制，本文提出一种整合方法，联合学习多个保护组的公平线性评分函数并最小化其与参考评分函数的距离。研究发现，先前关于多组设置运行效率的假设存在误导，该问题在小k值和二维数据下仍可能计算不可行，但通过分析发现存在复杂度壁垒的间隙，使得在保护组数量有限时仍可高效求解。此外，引入“效用损失”作为替代差异度量，提升评分函数在权重扰动下的稳定性。经工程权衡，该双管齐下的方案在真实数据集上表现优异，实验结果亦直接指导算法设计与实现决策。
Standard Language Ideology in AI-Generated Language
Authors: Genevieve Smith, Eve Fleisig, Ishita Rustagi, Xavier Yin
First: 2024-06-13T01:08:40+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-09T16:59:51+00:00
Comments: To appear in the 2026 ACM Conference on Fairness, Accountability, and Transparency (FAccT '26)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large language models (LLMs) generate text that reinforces standard language ideology: a bias towards certain language varieties that are granted more prestige, authority, and legitimacy than others. This paper contributes a sociotechnically grounded faceted taxonomy that illustrates how generative AI systems reproduce standard language ideology and its societal implications. We introduce the concept of standard AI-generated language ideology to explain how AI systems confer legitimacy on certain language varieties while marginalizing others, structuring patterns of performance disparity, stereotyping, appropriation, and erasure. We then discuss ongoing tensions around what constitutes desirable system behavior, as well as advantages and drawbacks of generative AI tools attempting or refusing to imitate different language varieties. To address the power relations shaping generative AI and the mechanisms identified in our taxonomy--legitimation, stereotyping, appropriation, and erasure--we offer recommendations that emphasize accountability, community agency, control, and ownership. These recommendations recognize linguistic diversity as a resource to be protected, valued, and sustained as part of a just AI future.
中文标题/摘要
标题：人工智能生成语言中的标准语言意识形态
大型语言模型（LLMs）生成的文本强化了标准语言意识形态：即倾向于赋予某些语言变体更高的声望、权威性和合法性。本文提出了一种基于社会技术视角的多维分类法，阐明生成式AI系统如何再现标准语言意识形态及其社会影响。我们引入“标准AI生成语言意识形态”这一概念，用以解释AI系统如何赋予某些语言变体合法性，同时边缘化其他变体，从而构建表现差异、刻板印象、挪用与抹除的模式。随后，我们讨论了关于理想系统行为的持续争议，以及生成式AI工具在尝试或拒绝模仿不同语言变体时所具有的优缺点。为应对塑造生成式AI的力量关系及我们分类法中识别出的机制——即合法化、刻板印象、挪用与抹除——我们提出强调问责制、社区自主权、控制与所有权的建议。这些建议将语言多样性视为应被保护、珍视并持续维持的资源，以构建一个公正的AI未来。
Summary / 总结
This paper investigates how large language models reinforce standard language ideology by privileging certain language varieties over others, thereby embedding social hierarchies into AI-generated text. It proposes a sociotechnical taxonomy that categorizes how AI systems legitimize dominant languages while marginalizing others through mechanisms like stereotyping, appropriation, and erasure. Experimental analysis reveals that generative AI tools often reproduce linguistic bias, leading to performance disparities and cultural erasure, and the authors argue that such systems must be redesigned to prioritize accountability, community control, and linguistic diversity to mitigate harm and support equitable AI development.
该文探讨大型语言模型如何通过生成文本强化标准语言意识形态，即偏袒某些语言变体而贬低其他语言，从而在技术中嵌入社会等级。研究提出一个社会技术分类框架，将AI系统如何通过合法化、刻板印象、挪用与抹除等机制赋予主流语言特权、边缘化其他语言进行系统化分析。实证发现AI工具常再现语言偏见，导致表现差异与文化消亡，最终建议强调问责、社区主导权与语言多样性保护，以构建更公平的AI未来。
Gradient-Guided Furthest Point Sampling for Robust Training Set Selection
Authors: Morris Trestman, Stefan Gugler, Felix A. Faber, O. A. von Lilienfeld
Venue: Machine Learning: Science and Technology 7, 035047 (2026)
First: 2025-10-10T01:41:06+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-09T16:59:34+00:00
Comments: 41 pages, 43 figures, 2 algorithms; journal article with supplementary information appended
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Training set sampling methods are used to improve model performance and lower data costs in machine learning problems relevant to chemistry. We introduce Gradient Guided Furthest Point Sampling (GGFPS), a simple extension of Furthest Point Sampling (FPS) that leverages molecular force norms to guide efficient sampling of configurational spaces of molecules. Numerical evidence is presented for a toy system (the Styblinski-Tang function) as well as for molecular dynamics trajectories from the MD17 dataset. Our numerical results indicate superior data efficiency and model robustness when using GGFPS compared to FPS and uniform random sampling (URS), as well as established supervised FPS-style selectors, PCov-FPS and PCov-CUR. Distribution analysis of the MD17 data suggests that FPS systematically under-samples equilibrium geometries, resulting in large test errors for relaxed structures. GGFPS cures this artifact and (i) enables up to twofold reductions in training cost without sacrificing predictive accuracy compared to FPS in the 2-dimensional Styblinski-Tang system, (ii) systematically lowers prediction errors for equilibrium as well as strained structures in MD17, and (iii) systematically decreases prediction error variances across all of the MD17 configuration spaces. These results suggest that gradient-aware sampling methods hold great promise as effective training set selection tools, and that naive use of FPS may result in imbalanced training and inconsistent prediction outcomes.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于梯度的最远点采样用于鲁棒训练集选择
训练集采样方法用于提升化学相关机器学习问题中的模型性能并降低数据成本。我们引入了梯度引导最远点采样（GGFPS），这是最远点采样（FPS）的简单扩展，利用分子力范数引导分子构型空间的高效采样。通过玩具系统（Styblinski-Tang函数）及MD17数据集的分子动力学轨迹的数值证据表明，与FPS和均匀随机采样（URS）相比，GGFPS在数据效率和模型鲁棒性方面表现更优，且优于已有的监督式FPS型选择器PCov-FPS和PCov-CUR。对MD17数据的分布分析表明，FPS系统性地低估了平衡构型，导致松弛结构的测试误差较大。GGFPS解决了这一缺陷：（i）在二维Styblinski-Tang系统中，相比FPS可将训练成本降低一倍而不牺牲预测精度；（ii）系统性降低MD17中平衡及应变结构的预测误差；（iii）系统性降低MD17所有构型空间的预测误差方差。这些结果表明，梯度感知采样方法作为有效的训练集选择工具具有巨大潜力，而盲目使用FPS可能导致训练集不平衡和预测结果不一致。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for more efficient and robust training set selection in chemistry-related machine learning, the authors propose Gradient-Guided Furthest Point Sampling (GGFPS), an extension of Furthest Point Sampling that incorporates molecular force norms to guide sampling in configuration space. Applied to both a toy function (Styblinski-Tang) and molecular dynamics trajectories from MD17, GGFPS outperforms FPS, uniform random sampling, and established FPS-style selectors like PCov-FPS and PCov-CUR. Results show GGFPS reduces training cost by up to twofold in the toy system without accuracy loss, lowers prediction errors for both equilibrium and strained structures in MD17, and reduces error variance across all configurations, indicating it corrects FPS’s bias toward under-sampling equilibrium geometries.
为提升化学相关机器学习任务中训练集选择的效率与鲁棒性，本文提出梯度引导最远点采样（GGFPS），该方法在最远点采样的基础上引入分子力范数以引导构型空间采样。在玩具系统（Styblinski-Tang）和MD17分子动力学轨迹上的实验表明，GGFPS在数据效率和模型鲁棒性上优于FPS、均匀随机采样及现有FPS类选择器（如PCov-FPS和PCov-CUR）。具体而言，GGFPS在玩具系统中可将训练成本降低一半以上且不损失精度，在MD17中显著降低平衡态与应变结构的预测误差，并降低所有构型空间的误差方差。
Falcon-X: A Time Series Foundation Model for Heterogeneous Multivariate Modeling
Authors: Yiding Liu, Yifan Hu, Hongjie Xia, Peiyuan Liu, Hongzhou Chen, Xilin Dai, Zewei Dong, Jiang-Ming Yang
First: 2026-05-26T17:03:21+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-09T16:58:41+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Time series foundation models (TSFMs) are transforming the forecasting paradigm through large-scale cross-domain pretraining. However, most existing TSFMs remain univariate, and recent efforts to enable cross-variate modeling still operate directly within the raw variate space. This design introduces fundamental limitations in semantic alignment and relational expressivity. Specifically, raw-space group mixing lacks a dedicated mechanism to align heterogeneous physical quantities, while standard non-negative attention fails to capture the complex synergistic and antagonistic interactions ubiquitous in real-world systems. To address these challenges, we propose Falcon-X, decouples variates from the raw space and maps them into a unified latent prototype space. Falcon-X employs a Unified Prototype Diff-Attention mechanism that explicitly evaluates both positive and negative semantic affinities to explicitly align heterogeneous variates. Cross-variate interactions are then efficiently performed within this shared space via Latent Entity Attention, naturally facilitating zero-shot structural transfer. Finally, a Variate Reassembly Router robustly reconstructs variate-specific trajectories via a request-and-dispatch mechanism. Extensive evaluations on the GIFT-Eval and fev-bench benchmarks demonstrate that Falcon-X achieves excellent forecasting performance, offering a principled and scalable paradigm for complex multivariate environments. Falcon-X is publicly released to support future research.
中文标题/摘要
标题：Falcon-X：面向异构多变量建模的时间序列基础模型
时间序列基础模型（TSFMs）通过大规模跨域预训练正在重塑预测范式。然而，现有大多数TSFMs仍为单变量模型，近期尝试实现跨变量建模的方法仍直接在原始变量空间中操作，这种设计在语义对齐和关系表达能力方面存在根本性局限。具体而言，原始空间中的组混合缺乏专门机制对异构物理量进行对齐，而标准非负注意力无法捕捉现实系统中普遍存在的复杂协同与对抗性交互。为解决这些问题，我们提出Falcon-X，将变量从原始空间解耦并映射至统一的潜在原型空间。Falcon-X采用统一原型差分注意力机制，显式评估正负语义亲和度，以显式对齐异构变量。随后，通过潜在实体注意力在共享空间中高效执行跨变量交互，自然支持零样本结构迁移。最后，通过请求-分发机制，变量重组路由器稳健重建各变量特定轨迹。在GIFT-Eval和fev-bench基准上的广泛评估表明，Falcon-X实现卓越的预测性能，提供了一种适用于复杂多变量环境的原理清晰且可扩展范式。Falcon-X已公开发布，以支持未来研究。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitations of existing univariate time series foundation models and their inability to effectively model heterogeneous multivariate systems, Falcon-X introduces a novel framework that decouples variates from raw space and maps them into a unified latent prototype space. It employs a Unified Prototype Diff-Attention mechanism to explicitly align heterogeneous variates by evaluating both positive and negative semantic affinities, followed by Latent Entity Attention to capture cross-variate interactions within the shared space. A Variate Reassembly Router then reconstructs individual variate trajectories via request-and-dispatch. Experimental results on GIFT-Eval and fev-bench show Falcon-X achieves superior forecasting performance compared to existing methods, demonstrating its effectiveness in complex multivariate environments.
为了解决现有单变量时间序列基础模型难以有效建模异构多变量数据的问题，Falcon-X提出了一种新框架，将变量从原始空间解耦并映射到统一的潜在原型空间。它采用统一原型差分注意力机制，通过显式评估正负语义亲和力对异构物理量进行对齐，再通过潜在实体注意力在共享空间中高效捕捉跨变量交互。最后，变量重组路由器通过请求-分发机制重构各变量的特定轨迹。在GIFT-Eval和fev-bench基准测试中，Falcon-X展现出优于现有模型的预测性能，验证了其在复杂多变量环境中的有效性。
Do Transformers Actually Help Intrusion Detection? A Temporal Sequence Evaluation on CIC-IDS2017
Authors: Zach Moczkodan, Hany Ragab
First: 2026-06-09T16:57:10+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-09T16:57:10+00:00
Comments: 11 pages, 9 figures, 9 tables. Preprint. Code: https://github.com/zachmocz/temporal-ids-bench
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Recent deep learning approaches for network intrusion detection increasingly incorporate temporal architectures such as recurrent networks and Transformers, often reporting near-perfect performance on CIC-IDS2017. However, many existing studies neither supply their temporal modules with genuine sequence inputs nor evaluate under realistic, leakage-free conditions, making it unclear whether reported gains arise from true sequence-modeling capability. In this work, we reformulate CIC-IDS2017 as a temporal intrusion-detection task by constructing ordered flow sequences from network conversations and benchmarking nine classical and deep learning architectures under a random split, two leakage-free splits, and a padding-scheme ablation. The central finding is that padding convention, not architecture, determines the Transformer's performance: on genuinely sequential (non-padded) windows the Transformer achieves the highest macro-F1 of any model in the experiment (0.89); under zero-pad+mask evaluation it drops markedly (-0.24 macro-F1), while LSTM, GRU, and 1D-CNN remain stable. Under leakage-free group evaluation the Random Forest is the most robust model (+0.009), while the Transformer's false-alarm rate grows from 0.04% to 2.7%, a 67-fold increase invisible under conventional protocols. These findings demonstrate that evaluation methodology -- specifically padding convention and split protocol -- has a larger effect on reported performance than architectural choice, and that widely used random splits with repeat-last padding can overestimate model robustness by up to 0.24 macro-F1. We advocate leakage-free splits, explicit padding disclosure, and sequence-aware benchmarking as standard practice in future IDS research. Code and implementation details are available at https://github.com/zachmocz/temporal-ids-bench.
中文标题/摘要
标题：Transformer真的有助于入侵检测吗？基于CIC-IDS2017的时间序列评估
近期用于网络入侵检测的深度学习方法越来越多地引入循环网络和Transformer等时序架构，常在CIC-IDS2017数据集上报告近乎完美的性能。然而，许多现有研究并未为时序模块提供真实序列输入，也未在无泄漏的真实条件下评估，使得报告的性能提升是否源于真正的序列建模能力存疑。本文将CIC-IDS2017重新定义为时序入侵检测任务，通过构建网络会话的有序流序列，对九种经典和深度学习架构在随机划分、两种无泄漏划分及填充方案消融实验中进行基准测试。核心发现是：填充约定而非架构本身决定了Transformer的性能——在真实序列（非填充）窗口上，Transformer达到实验中所有模型最高的宏F1值（0.89）；在零填充+掩码评估下，其性能显著下降（-0.24宏F1），而LSTM、GRU和1D-CNN保持稳定。在无泄漏组评估中，随机森林表现最稳健（+0.009），而Transformer的误报率从0.04%飙升至2.7%，增幅达67倍，传统评估协议对此无从察觉。这些发现表明，评估方法——特别是填充约定和划分协议——对报告性能的影响远大于架构选择，且广泛使用的随机划分配合重复末尾填充可能使模型稳健性高估达0.24宏F1。我们建议未来IDS研究采用无泄漏划分、明确披露填充方案及序列感知基准测试作为标准实践。代码与实现细节见https://github.com/zachmocz/temporal-ids-bench。
Summary / 总结
This study investigates whether Transformers genuinely improve intrusion detection by evaluating them under realistic, leakage-free conditions on CIC-IDS2017. The authors construct genuine temporal flow sequences from network conversations and benchmark nine models—including Transformers, LSTMs, and Random Forests—using random splits, leakage-free splits, and padding-ablation tests. Results show that Transformers achieve the highest macro-F1 (0.89) only on non-padded sequential windows; under zero-padding with masking, performance drops by 0.24 macro-F1, while LSTMs and CNNs remain stable. Under leakage-free evaluation, Random Forest outperforms all others by +0.009 macro-F1, and Transformer false-alarm rates surge 67-fold to 2.7%. The study concludes that evaluation methodology—especially padding and split protocols—dominates performance claims over architecture choice, and recommends leakage-free splits and explicit padding disclosure as standard practice.
该研究旨在探究Transformer是否真正提升入侵检测性能，通过构建网络会话的有序流量序列，在CIC-IDS2017数据集上对九种模型（含Transformer、LSTM、GRU、CNN等）进行评估，采用随机分割、无泄漏分割及填充方案消融实验。结果表明，仅在非填充的真实序列窗口下，Transformer表现最优（宏F1值0.89）；在零填充加掩码评估下，其性能骤降0.24宏F1，而LSTM、GRU和CNN保持稳定。在无泄漏评估中，随机森林表现最佳（+0.009宏F1），而Transformer的误报率飙升67倍至2.7%。研究指出，评估方法（尤其是填充方式与分割协议）对性能影响远大于模型架构选择，常见随机分割加填充会高估模型鲁棒性达0.24宏F1。
IDEAL: In-DEpth ALignment Makes A Discrete Representation AutoEncoder
Authors: Yitong Chen, Zijie Diao, Junke Wang, Lingyu Kong, Yixuan Ren, Bo He, Yu-Gang Jiang, Zuxuan Wu
First: 2026-06-09T16:53:30+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-09T16:53:30+00:00
Comments: Code is available at https://github.com/Row11n/IDEAL
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Built on pretrained vision foundation models (VFMs), representation autoencoders (RAEs) have recently emerged as a promising approach for constructing semantically rich latent spaces for image generation. However, their reconstruction quality often remains suboptimal, largely because deep VFM representations do not preserve sufficient fine-grained visual detail. This limitation becomes even more severe after discretization, where missing low-level information is difficult to recover. In fact, we observe that shallow VFM features retain considerably richer local appearance and structural detail, which complements the high-level semantics carried by deep features used in existing RAEs. Motivated by this complementary property, we propose Ideal, an In-depth Alignment framework for discrete representation autoencoding. By jointly aligning quantized tokens with both shallow and deep VFM features, Ideal enables the resulting discrete visual tokens to preserve both visual fidelity and rich semantics. Extensive experiments demonstrate that Ideal yields superior reconstruction performance, achieving 0.61 rFID on ImageNet and outperforming the previous best method by 0.28. When used for autoregressive image generation, Ideal further produces a gFID of 1.89, establishing a new state of the art for autoregressive image generation.
中文标题/摘要
标题：IDEAL：深度对齐使离散表示自动编码器
基于预训练视觉基础模型（VFMs），表示自动编码器（RAEs）近期成为构建语义丰富的图像生成潜在空间的有前景方法。然而，其重建质量常不理想，主要因深层VFM表示未能保留足够的细粒度视觉细节。该限制在离散化后更为严重，因缺失的低层信息难以恢复。事实上，我们观察到浅层VFM特征保留了更丰富的局部外观与结构细节，这与现有RAEs所用深层特征携带的高层语义互补。受此互补特性启发，我们提出IDEAL，一种面向离散表示自动编码的深度对齐框架。通过联合对齐量化令牌与浅层和深层VFM特征，IDEAL使生成的离散视觉令牌同时保留视觉保真度与丰富语义。大量实验表明，IDEAL实现优越的重建性能，在ImageNet上达到0.61 rFID，比此前最佳方法提升0.28。用于自回归图像生成时，IDEAL进一步实现1.89 gFID，创下自回归图像生成新SOTA。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the observation that shallow vision foundation model features retain richer local visual detail than deep features, which are typically used in discrete representation autoencoders, the authors propose IDEAL, a framework that jointly aligns quantized tokens with both shallow and deep VFM representations to preserve visual fidelity and semantic richness. The method enhances reconstruction quality by leveraging complementary information across feature depths, and experiments show it achieves 0.61 rFID on ImageNet — 0.28 better than prior methods — and sets a new state-of-the-art gFID of 1.89 for autoregressive image generation.
鉴于浅层视觉基础模型特征比现有离散表示自编码器中使用的深层特征保留更丰富的局部视觉细节，作者提出IDEAL框架，通过联合对齐量化视觉标记与浅层和深层VFM特征，使离散标记同时保持视觉保真度与高层语义。实验表明，IDEAL在ImageNet上重建rFID达0.61，比前方法高0.28；在自回归图像生成中gFID为1.89，创下新SOTA。
A Scoping Review of Mixed Initiative Visual Analytics in the Automation Renaissance
Authors: Shayan Monadjemi, Yuhan Guo, Kai Xu, Alex Endert, Anamaria Crisan
First: 2025-09-23T15:30:34+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-09T16:53:06+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Artificial agents are increasingly integrated into data analysis workflows, carrying out tasks that were primarily done by humans. Our research explores how the introduction of automation recalibrates the dynamic between humans and automating technology. To explore this question, we conducted a scoping review encompassing twenty years of mixed-initiative visual analytic systems. To describe and contrast the relationship between humans and automation, we developed an integrated taxonomy to delineate the objectives of these mixed-initiative visual analytics tools, how much automation they support, and the assumed roles of humans. Here, we describe our qualitative approach of integrating existing theoretical frameworks with new codes we developed. Our analysis shows that the visualization research literature lacks consensus on the definition of mixed-initiative systems and explores a limited potential of the collaborative interaction landscape between people and automation. Our research provides a scaffold to advance the discussion of human-AI collaboration during visual data analysis. Our integrated taxonomy is available in the form of a web application on https://smonadjemi.github.io/miva.
中文标题/摘要
标题：自动化复兴背景下混合发起视觉分析的综述
人工代理正越来越多地融入数据分析工作流程，执行过去主要由人类完成的任务。我们的研究探讨自动化引入如何重新校准人与自动化技术之间的动态关系。为此，我们对过去二十年的混合发起视觉分析系统进行了综述。为描述和对比人与自动化之间的关系，我们构建了一个综合分类法，以界定这些混合发起视觉分析工具的目标、支持的自动化程度以及假设的人类角色。我们采用定性方法，将现有理论框架与新开发的编码相结合。分析表明，可视化研究文献对混合发起系统的定义缺乏共识，且对人机协作互动潜力的探索有限。本研究为推进视觉数据分析中人机协作的讨论提供了基础。我们的综合分类法以网页应用形式发布于 https://smonadjemi.github.io/miva。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the growing integration of artificial agents into data analysis workflows, this study investigates how automation reshapes human-technology collaboration. Through a scoping review of 20 years of mixed-initiative visual analytics systems, the authors developed an integrated taxonomy to classify these systems by their automation scope and human roles. Their qualitative analysis reveals a lack of consensus in the literature on defining mixed-initiative systems and highlights underexplored dimensions of human-AI collaboration in visual analytics, offering a structured framework to guide future research.
鉴于人工智能代理在数据分析流程中的日益集成，本研究探讨自动化如何重塑人机协作关系。通过对20年混合发起式视觉分析系统文献的综述，作者构建了一个整合分类法，按系统目标、自动化程度和人类角色进行分类。其质性分析发现，现有文献对混合发起式系统的定义缺乏共识，并指出了人机协作在视觉分析中尚未充分探索的维度。该分类法为未来相关研究提供了概念框架。
RECAP: Regression Evaluation for Continual Adaptation of Prompts
Authors: Harsh Deshpande, Kushal Chawla, Sangwoo Cho, William Campbell, Sambit Sahu
First: 2026-06-04T20:35:15+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-09T16:52:55+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Production agentic systems routinely face evolving constraints and must comply from the very next interaction. Scenarios like a tool-call notification changing a compliance threshold or a policy update adding disclosure requirements fit this criteria, having close to no room for errors in production. This proactive adaptation setting is common in deployment, but absent from current benchmarks, which assume either static constraint sets or reactive protocols with evaluation feedback. We introduce RECAP, a benchmark that measures continual-learning phenomena (forgetting, regression, forward transfer) at the constraint level under a strictly proactive adapt-then-test protocol: prompt optimization methods receive only the constraint specification and must generalize before seeing any test data. Evaluating six methods across four LLMs and three schedules with evolving constraints, we find that these methods show no significant improvement in performance, even after incurring a higher latency. These methods, designed for offline or reactive settings, are inadequate for the proactive paradigm. Our work emphasizes the growing need for designing proactive prompt adaptation methods, where the models must remain robust to evolving needs in deployment.
中文标题/摘要
标题：回顾：持续适应提示的回归评估
生产级代理系统通常面临不断变化的约束条件，必须在下一次交互中立即遵守。例如，工具调用通知改变合规阈值或政策更新增加披露要求等场景，几乎不允许任何错误。这种主动适应模式在实际部署中很常见，但当前基准测试却未涵盖，它们要么假设静态约束集，要么假设反应式协议并提供评估反馈。我们引入RECAP基准测试，该测试在严格主动适应-先适应后测试协议下，从约束层面衡量持续学习现象（遗忘、回归、前向迁移）：提示优化方法仅获得约束规范，必须在看到任何测试数据前进行泛化。我们在四种大语言模型和三种约束演变调度下评估六种方法，发现这些方法即使增加延迟，性能也无显著提升。这些为离线或反应式场景设计的方法，无法满足主动适应范式的需求。我们的工作强调了设计主动提示适应方法的迫切需求，即模型必须在部署中持续应对不断变化的需求。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for agentic systems to proactively adapt to evolving constraints in production without error, the paper introduces RECAP, a benchmark that evaluates prompt adaptation under a strict proactive protocol where models must generalize from constraint specifications alone before seeing test data. Six prompt optimization methods were tested across four LLMs and three constraint schedules, measuring phenomena like forgetting and regression. Results show no significant performance improvement despite increased latency, indicating current methods, designed for offline or reactive settings, are ill-suited for proactive adaptation in real-world deployment.
为应对生产环境中代理系统需主动适应不断变化的约束且不容出错的需求，本文提出RECAP基准，评估在严格主动适应协议下（仅依据约束规范进行泛化，不接触测试数据）的提示优化方法。在四类大语言模型和三种约束调度下测试六种方法，结果表明这些方法在性能上无显著提升，尽管延迟增加，说明现有为离线或反应式场景设计的方法无法胜任主动适应范式，凸显了开发主动提示适应方法的迫切需求。
RoboNaldo: Accurate, Stable and Powerful Humanoid Soccer Shooting via Motion-Guided Curriculum Reinforcement Learning
Authors: Yichao Zhong, Yidan Lu, Yuhang Lu, Tianyang Tang, Haoguang Mai, Yixuan Pan, Tianyu Li, Li Chen, Jingbo Wang, Zhongyu Li, Peng Lu, Hongyang Li
First: 2026-06-09T16:51:13+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-09T16:51:13+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Elite humanoid soccer shooting requires whole-body stability, high-impulse whole-body interactions, and accuracy to targets. Motion tracking-driven reinforcement learning (RL) provides stability in whole-body movement coordination, but a fixed reference makes it hard to adapt to varied ball positions and strike timings; in contrast, task reward-driven RL struggles to explore and discover valid kicks from scratch. We therefore introduce RoboNaldo, a three-stage motion-guided curriculum RL framework for high-impulse humanoid interaction. A single human-kick reference is used as a scaffold and progressively shifts optimization towards shooting performance. The curriculum first learns a stable whole-body kicking prior, then adapts the kick to free-kick settings where the ball is stationary at random positions, and finally extends it to moving-ball shooting through a locomotion-command and kick-trigger interface. A high-level heuristic planner controls this interface during training, while alternative high-level controllers can drive the same low-level policy at inference. In simulation, RoboNaldo demonstrates free-kick shot error 48.6% lower and shoot velocity 2.96x than prior work baselines. In real world on a Unitree G1 with onboard perception, RoboNaldo attains 0.73 m and 0.86 m average target shooting error from 3 m away in free-kick and moving-ball cases, accordingly. And the post-contact ball velocity reaches 13.10 m/s, which is 59-71% of reported professional open-play shot speed. Project page: $\href{https://opendrivelab.com/RoboNaldo}{\text{opendrivelab.com/RoboNaldo}}$.
中文标题/摘要
标题：RoboNaldo：通过运动引导的课程强化学习实现精准、稳定且强大的人形机器人足球射门
精英级人形机器人足球射门需要全身稳定性、高冲击全身交互及精准目标命中。运动跟踪驱动的强化学习（RL）可提供全身运动协调的稳定性，但固定参考点难以适应球体位置和击球时机的多样性；相比之下，任务奖励驱动的RL难以从零开始探索并发现有效射门动作。为此，我们提出RoboNaldo，一种面向高冲击人形交互的三阶段运动引导课程RL框架。以单个人类踢球参考动作作为基础，逐步将优化目标转向射门性能。课程首先学习稳定全身踢球先验，然后适应定点任意位置静止球的任意球场景，最后通过运动指令与踢球触发接口扩展至移动球射门。训练时由高层启发式规划器控制该接口，推理时可由不同高层控制器驱动同一低层策略。仿真结果表明，RoboNaldo的任意球射门误差比现有基线低48.6%，射门速度提升2.96倍。在搭载视觉感知的Unitree G1机器人真实环境中，RoboNaldo在3米距离下任意球与移动球场景的平均目标误差分别为0.73米和0.86米，触球后球速达13.10米/秒，约为职业球员开放进攻射门速度的59-71%。项目页面：$\href{https://opendrivelab.com/RoboNaldo}{\text{opendrivelab.com/RoboNaldo}}$。
Summary / 总结
