To address the challenges of accurate, stable, and powerful humanoid soccer shooting, RoboNaldo introduces a three-stage motion-guided curriculum reinforcement learning framework that progressively refines kicking behavior from a human reference motion toward dynamic real-world scenarios. It first learns a stable whole-body kicking prior, then adapts to stationary ball free-kick settings, and finally extends to moving-ball shooting using a locomotion-command and kick-trigger interface controlled by a high-level planner during training. At inference, alternative controllers can drive the same low-level policy. In simulation, RoboNaldo achieves 48.6% lower shot error and 2.96x higher velocity than prior baselines. In real-world tests on a Unitree G1 robot, it achieves average shooting errors of 0.73 m and 0.86 m for free-kick and moving-ball cases respectively, with post-contact ball velocity reaching 13.10 m/s — 59–71% of professional open-play shot speed.
为实现稳定、精准且有力的人形机器人足球射门，RoboNaldo提出一种基于运动引导的课程强化学习框架，从人类踢球动作参考出发，分阶段优化：先学习全身稳定踢球动作，再适应静止球自由球场景，最后通过运动指令与踢球触发接口扩展至移动球射门，训练时由启发式规划器控制接口。实验表明，该方法在仿真中射门误差降低48.6%，速度提升2.96倍；在真实Unitree G1机器人上，3米外自由球与移动球平均射门误差分别为0.73米和0.86米，触球后球速达13.10米/秒，相当于职业球员开放球速的59–71%。
Blind denoising diffusion models and the blessings of dimensionality
Authors: Zahra Kadkhodaie, Aram-Alexandre Pooladian, Sinho Chewi, Eero Simoncelli
Venue: ICML 2025
First: 2026-02-10T10:38:16+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-09T16:50:11+00:00
Comments: 39 pages, 13 figures; Accepted to ICML 2025 FoGen workshop
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Denoising diffusion models (DDMs) are state-of-the-art methods for learning densities from data across numerous domains, yet many aspects of the training and sampling pipeline remain poorly understood. In particular, noise conditioning requires practitioners to incorporate contrived unprincipled noise embeddings into neural network architectures and to use ad hoc noise schedules for sampling. To address these drawbacks, we provide a complete theory for \emph{blind denoising diffusion models} (BDDMs): a variant of DDMs where the noise amplitude is not passed into the neural network during training or sampling, obviating the need for the aforementioned design choices. We justify the correctness of BDDMs as a sampling algorithm under an assumption of low intrinsic dimensionality of the underlying data distribution relative to the ambient dimension. This assumption arises through the introduction of the Bayesian problem of estimating noise levels from a single noisy sample, which might be of independent interest. We empirically compare the performance of BDDMs to standard DDMs, showcasing the benefits of an \emph{adaptive} scheme which is rigorously justified by our analysis.
中文标题/摘要
标题：盲去噪扩散模型与维度的祝福
去噪扩散模型（DDMs）是跨多个领域从数据中学习密度的最先进方法，但其训练和采样流程的许多方面仍不甚明了。特别是，噪声条件化要求从业者在神经网络架构中引入人为设计的非原则性噪声嵌入，并使用临时的噪声调度进行采样。为解决这些缺点，我们为\emph{盲去噪扩散模型}（BDDMs）提供了一套完整理论：这是一种在训练或采样过程中不将噪声幅度传递给神经网络的DDM变体，从而消除了上述设计选择的需要。我们在数据分布的内在维度相对于环境维度较低的假设下，证明了BDDMs作为采样算法的正确性。这一假设源于通过贝叶斯方法从单个噪声样本中估计噪声水平的问题，该问题本身可能具有独立研究价值。我们通过实证比较了BDDMs与标准DDMs的性能，展示了由我们的分析严格支持的\emph{自适应}方案的优势。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to eliminate ad hoc noise conditioning in denoising diffusion models, this work introduces blind denoising diffusion models (BDDMs), which operate without explicit noise amplitude inputs during training or sampling. The method is theoretically grounded in the assumption that data distributions have low intrinsic dimensionality relative to ambient space, enabling correct sampling via a Bayesian noise estimation framework. Empirical results demonstrate that BDDMs outperform standard DDMs, particularly when paired with an adaptive noise schedule derived from the theoretical analysis.
为消除扩散模型中人为设计的噪声条件化，本文提出盲去噪扩散模型（BDDM），训练与采样时无需传递噪声幅度。该方法基于数据分布内在维度远低于环境维度的假设，通过贝叶斯噪声估计框架加以理论支撑。实验表明，BDDM在配合理论推导的自适应采样方案时，性能优于标准扩散模型。
A Distributed Multi-UGV Exploration Framework With Loop-Aware Planning and Descriptor-Aided Localization in Resource-Limited Environments
Authors: Zhiwei Li, Haiou Liu, Xijun Zhao, Ji Li, Yingze Wang, Boyang Wang
Venue: IEEE Transactions on Industrial Electronics, 2026
First: 2026-06-09T16:48:53+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-09T16:48:53+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Robust and efficient cooperative exploration with multiple unmanned ground vehicles (UGVs) in unknown, GPSdenied, and bandwidth-limited environments without prior maps remains challenging, as localization drift degrades map consistency and induces redundant coverage. This paper presents a fully distributed exploration framework that couples descriptoraided inter-UGV loop closure with loop-aware hierarchical planning while enabling autonomous localization and exploration. We develop a lightweight LiDAR global descriptor with range-image prealignment to enable robust cross-UGV place recognition under large yaw and lateral variations, and use verified loop closures to maintain globally consistent trajectories and a sparse topological representation. We further introduce an uncertainty-aware crossUGV loop-closure selection module that scores candidate loop closures under pose uncertainty and retains high-utility loop closures as planning anchors for global task allocation and local route refinement. Simulations and real-UGV experiments show that the loop-closure module achieves AR@1/AR@1% of 89.9%/95.5%, distributed optimization reduces absolute trajectory error, the system substantially reduces two-way communication volume, and the overall framework reduces exploration time and travel distance by 15% and 14%, respectively, compared with an mTSP baseline.
中文标题/摘要
标题：一种在资源受限环境中具有环路感知规划与描述符辅助定位的分布式多无人地面车探索框架
在未知、无GPS信号且带宽受限的环境中，多无人地面车（UGV）实现稳健高效的协同探索仍具挑战，因定位漂移会降低地图一致性并引发冗余覆盖。本文提出一种完全分布式的探索框架，将描述符辅助的UGV间环路闭合与环路感知分层规划相结合，同时支持自主定位与探索。我们开发了一种轻量级LiDAR全局描述符，结合距离图像预对齐，以在大偏航角和横向变化下实现鲁棒的跨UGV位姿识别；并利用验证过的环路闭合保持全局一致轨迹与稀疏拓扑表示。进一步，我们引入一种不确定性感知的跨UGV环路闭合选择模块，在位姿不确定性下评分候选环路闭合，并保留高价值环路闭合作为规划锚点，用于全局任务分配与局部路径优化。仿真与真实UGV实验表明，环路闭合模块在AR@1/AR@1%指标上分别达到89.9%/95.5%，分布式优化降低了绝对轨迹误差，系统显著减少了双向通信量，整体框架相比mTSP基线分别减少了15%的探索时间与14%的行驶距离。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of enabling multiple unmanned ground vehicles to explore unknown, GPS-denied, and bandwidth-constrained environments without prior maps while avoiding localization drift and redundant coverage, this paper proposes a fully distributed framework that integrates descriptor-aided inter-UGV loop closure with loop-aware hierarchical planning. The method employs a lightweight LiDAR descriptor with range-image prealignment for robust cross-vehicle place recognition under yaw and lateral variations, and uses verified loop closures to maintain globally consistent trajectories and a sparse topological map. An uncertainty-aware loop-closure selection module scores candidates under pose uncertainty and retains high-utility ones for global task allocation and local route refinement. Experimental results show the loop closure achieves 89.9% AR@1 and 95.5% AR@1%, distributed optimization reduces trajectory error, communication volume is substantially lowered, and the overall framework cuts exploration time and travel distance by 15% and 14% respectively compared to an mTSP baseline.
为解决多无人地面车在无GPS、带宽受限且无先验地图的未知环境中协同探索时定位漂移导致地图不一致和冗余覆盖的问题，本文提出一种全分布式探索框架，结合基于描述符的跨车回环检测与回环感知的分层规划。方法采用轻量级LiDAR描述符配合范围图像预对齐，实现大偏航和侧向变化下的跨车位姿识别，并通过验证回环保持全局轨迹一致性与稀疏拓扑表示。引入不确定性感知的跨车回环选择模块，根据位姿不确定性评分并保留高价值回环作为规划锚点，用于全局任务分配与局部路径优化。实验表明，回环模块AR@1/AR@1%达89.9%/95.5%，分布式优化降低轨迹误差，通信量显著减少，整体框架较mTSP基线分别减少15%探索时间与14%行驶距离。
Test-Time Gradient Guidance of Flow Policies in Reinforcement Learning
Authors: Zhiyuan Zhou, Andy Peng, Charles Xu, Qiyang Li, Tobias Springenberg, Kevin Frans, Sergey Levine
First: 2026-06-09T16:45:57+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-09T16:45:57+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Expressive continuous control policies, such as diffusion and flow models, form the backbone of recent advances in scaling imitation learning for simulated and real robot control. While they are known to scale stably in the supervised imitation learning setting, incorporating them into reinforcement learning (RL) pipelines for policy improvement has proven more difficult. It often requires specialized training objectives or backpropagating through denoising processes, which cause well-known issues with stability and affect scalability. In this paper we study the question of whether simple policy improvement schemes at test time alone, leaving stable supervised policy training intact, can be a competitive alternative which sidesteps these issues. To this end, we propose QGF (Q-Guided Flow), an RL algorithm that performs policy optimization entirely at test time. QGF works by pre-training both a reference flow policy (via a standard behavioral cloning objective) and a value function critic and, at test time, using the value gradient to guide the reference policy to generate higher-value actions without any additional policy learning. Empirically, QGF outperforms prior test-time RL methods on single-task and goal-conditioned offline RL benchmarks with high-dimensional action spaces, and is competitive with state-of-the-art training-time algorithms while being much cheaper to run. Moreover, it exhibits favorable scaling with model size by avoiding the instability of actor-critic training, offering a practical and effective alternative RL algorithm with expressive policies.
中文标题/摘要
标题：强化学习中测试时流策略的梯度引导
表达能力强的连续控制策略，如扩散和流模型，是近期在模拟与真实机器人控制中扩展模仿学习取得进展的核心。虽然它们在监督模仿学习设置中已知能稳定扩展，但将其融入强化学习（RL）流程以改进策略则更具挑战性，通常需要专门的训练目标或通过去噪过程反向传播，这会导致已知的稳定性问题并影响可扩展性。本文研究的问题是：仅在测试时使用简单的策略改进方案，同时保持稳定的监督策略训练，是否能成为一种可竞争的替代方案，从而避开这些问题。为此，我们提出QGF（Q引导流），一种在测试时完全执行策略优化的RL算法。QGF通过预训练参考流策略（使用标准行为克隆目标）和价值函数批评者，在测试时利用价值梯度引导参考策略生成更高价值的动作，而无需额外的策略学习。实证表明，QGF在高维动作空间的单任务和目标条件离线RL基准测试中优于先前的测试时RL方法，并在运行成本上远低于最先进的训练时算法，同时表现出良好的模型规模扩展性，避免了演员-评论家训练的不稳定性，提供了一种实用且有效的替代RL算法。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of integrating expressive flow-based policies into reinforcement learning pipelines without destabilizing training, this paper investigates whether test-time policy improvement alone can yield competitive performance. The proposed QGF algorithm pre-trains a reference flow policy and a value function critic via behavioral cloning, then at test time uses the value gradient to steer the policy toward higher-value actions without additional policy learning. Experimental results show QGF outperforms prior test-time RL methods on offline RL benchmarks with high-dimensional actions and matches state-of-the-art training-time algorithms while being significantly more computationally efficient, and scales favorably with model size by avoiding actor-critic instability.
为了解决将表达能力强的流式策略集成到强化学习流程中时面临的稳定性与可扩展性问题，本文研究了仅在测试时进行策略优化是否能获得竞争力的表现。所提出的QGF算法先通过行为克隆预训练参考流策略和价值函数批评者，测试时仅利用价值梯度引导策略生成更高价值的动作，无需额外训练。实验表明，QGF在高维动作空间的离线强化学习基准上优于现有测试时强化学习方法，与最先进的训练时算法表现相当，同时因避免了演员-评论家训练的不稳定性，运行成本更低且随模型规模扩展更优。
GRAU: Generic Reconfigurable Activation Unit Design for Neural Network Hardware Accelerators
Authors: Yuhao Liu, Salim Ullah, Akash Kumar
First: 2026-02-25T19:18:22+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-09T16:42:58+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
With the continuous growth of neural network scales, low-precision quantization is widely used in edge accelerators. Classic multi-threshold activation hardware requires 2^n thresholds for $n$-bit outputs, causing a rapid increase in hardware cost as precision increases. We propose a reconfigurable activation hardware, GRAU, based on piecewise linear fitting, where the segment slopes are approximated by powers of two. Our design requires only basic comparators and 1-bit right shifters, supporting mixed-precision quantization and nonlinear functions such as SiLU. Compared with multi-threshold activators, GRAU reduces LUT consumption by over 90%, achieving higher hardware efficiency, flexibility, and scalability. The best trade-off is usually achieved with 6-8 segments, while complex nonlinearities under aggressive low-cost settings may suffer larger accuracy degradation.
中文标题/摘要
标题：GRAU：面向神经网络硬件加速器的通用可重构激活单元设计
随着神经网络规模的持续增长，低精度量化在边缘加速器中广泛应用。经典多阈值激活硬件需为n位输出提供2^n个阈值，导致精度提升时硬件成本急剧上升。我们提出一种基于分段线性拟合的可重构激活硬件GRAU，其分段斜率通过2的幂次近似。该设计仅需基本比较器和1位右移器，支持混合精度量化及SiLU等非线性函数。相比多阈值激活器，GRAU的LUT消耗降低超过90%，实现更高的硬件效率、灵活性与可扩展性。通常6-8段可获得最佳权衡，但在高成本限制下复杂非线性函数可能面临更大精度损失。
Summary / 总结
Driven by the need to reduce hardware cost in edge neural network accelerators as model precision increases, this paper proposes GRAU, a reconfigurable activation unit that uses piecewise linear fitting with slopes approximated by powers of two. The design relies only on basic comparators and 1-bit right shifters, enabling support for mixed-precision quantization and nonlinear functions like SiLU. Experimental results show GRAU reduces LUT consumption by over 90% compared to traditional multi-threshold designs, achieving higher efficiency and scalability, with optimal performance typically observed using 6-8 segments, though aggressive low-cost configurations may lead to greater accuracy loss for complex nonlinearities.
为应对大规模神经网络在边缘设备中低精度量化加速的需求，本文提出GRAU，一种基于分段线性拟合的可重构激活单元，通过使用二的幂次斜率替代传统多阈值电路。GRAU仅需基础比较器和1位右移器，支持混合精度及SiLU等非线性激活函数，相比传统设计LUT消耗降低超90%。实验表明，GRAU在硬件效率与可扩展性上表现优异，6–8段配置通常取得最佳性能，但在严苛低成本设定下，复杂非线性函数可能面临较大精度损失。
The Shibboleth Effect: Auditing the Cross-Lingual Distributional Skew of Large Language Models
Authors: Hakan Mehmetcik
First: 2026-06-09T16:42:00+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-09T16:42:00+00:00
Comments: 25 pages, 2 figures, 6 tables, Research Article
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
This study investigates cross-lingual distributional skew (the Shibboleth Effect) in frontier large language models (LLMs) subjected to sustained adversarial conditions. We develop a multi-agent geopolitical wargame, the Cerulean Sea Crisis, a synthetic maritime territorial dispute designed to mirror the structural dynamics of Eastern Mediterranean conflicts. Six frontier models (GPT-4o, Llama-4, Mistral-Large, Gemini-3.1-Pro, Qwen3.6-Plus, and DeepSeek-R1) participate in a between-groups experiment (N = 10 games per arm, K = 5 rounds per game) in which the sole manipulation is the language of play (English versus Turkish), producing 586 validated statements. A zero-shot classifier assesses behavioral dispositions along two continuous dimensions: Concession Rate and Coercive Rhetoric. The results are heterogeneous. Llama-4 shows a substantial, Holm-corrected increase in coercive rhetoric under Turkish (delta = +0.800, p = .002), whereas Gemini-3.1-Pro displays an equally large decrease (delta = -0.750, p = .005). DeepSeek-R1 exhibits a similar negative shift (delta = -0.860, p = .006) and provides chain-of-thought evidence consistent with a buffering mechanism. GPT-4o shows no detectable effect (delta = +0.130, p = .614). These findings indicate that cross-lingual behavioral skew is contingent on model architecture and training regime rather than a universal property of Western-origin LLMs. We identify two distinct buffering mechanisms, chain-of-thought institutional anchoring and multilingual RLHF alignment, and discuss their implications for integrating LLMs safely into diplomatic and crisis-management settings.
中文标题/摘要
标题：沙伯效应：对前沿大语言模型跨语言分布偏斜的审计
本研究考察了在持续对抗条件下前沿大语言模型（LLMs）中的跨语言分布偏斜（即沙伯效应）。我们开发了一个多智能体地缘政治战争游戏——“天青海危机”，这是一个模拟东地中海冲突结构动态的合成海上领土争端。六款前沿模型（GPT-4o、Llama-4、Mistral-Large、Gemini-3.1-Pro、Qwen3.6-Plus 和 DeepSeek-R1）参与了一项组间实验（每组10场游戏，每场游戏5轮），唯一操纵变量为游戏语言（英语 vs 土耳其语），共产生586条经验证的陈述。零样本分类器沿两个连续维度评估行为倾向：让步率与胁迫性修辞。结果异质性显著：Llama-4 在土耳其语环境下胁迫性修辞显著增加（delta = +0.800, p = .002），而 Gemini-3.1-Pro 则同等显著下降（delta = -0.750, p = .005）。DeepSeek-R1 亦呈现类似负向变化（delta = -0.860, p = .006），并提供链式思维证据支持缓冲机制。GPT-4o 未发现显著效应（delta = +0.130, p = .614）。这些发现表明，跨语言行为偏斜取决于模型架构与训练范式，而非西方源流LLMs的普遍属性。我们识别出两种不同缓冲机制——链式思维制度锚定与多语言RLHF对齐，并讨论其在安全整合LLMs至外交与危机管理场景中的意义。
Summary / 总结
This study investigates how large language models (LLMs) behave differently across languages under adversarial conditions, motivated by the 'Shibboleth Effect'—a potential bias in cross-lingual distributional skew. Using a synthetic geopolitical wargame called the Cerulean Sea Crisis, six frontier models were tested in English and Turkish versions, with behavioral outputs analyzed via zero-shot classification on Concession Rate and Coercive Rhetoric. Results revealed heterogeneous responses: Llama-4 increased coercive rhetoric in Turkish (p = .002), Gemini-3.1-Pro and DeepSeek-R1 decreased it (p = .005, .006), while GPT-4o showed no effect. These findings suggest cross-lingual behavioral shifts depend on model architecture and training, not origin, and point to two buffering mechanisms—chain-of-thought anchoring and multilingual RLHF alignment—that may help mitigate such skew in high-stakes applications.
本研究旨在探究大型语言模型（LLM）在对抗性条件下跨语言行为差异，即所谓的‘沙伯效应’。通过设计名为‘天青海危机’的合成地缘政治博弈，六款前沿模型在英语与土耳其语版本中进行测试，行为输出经零样本分类评估两个维度：让步率与胁迫性言辞。结果呈现异质性：Llama-4在土耳其语中胁迫性言辞显著上升（delta = +0.800, p = .002），而Gemini-3.1-Pro与DeepSeek-R1则显著下降（delta分别为-0.750和-0.860, p < .005），后者并提供链式思维证据支持缓冲机制。GPT-4o无显著变化。表明跨语言行为偏差取决于模型架构与训练方式，而非西方起源的固有属性，并识别出两种缓冲机制：链式思维制度锚定与多语言RLHF对齐，对LLM在外交与危机管理中的安全应用具有启示意义。
Unifying Local Communications and Local Updates for LLM Pretraining
Authors: Pietro Cagnasso, Eugene Belilovsky, Edouard Oyallon
First: 2026-06-09T16:40:54+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-09T16:40:54+00:00
Comments: 38 pages, 9 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Communication-efficient pre-training of LLMs is increasingly important as training draws on compute distributed across clusters, data centers, and lower-bandwidth links. Many practical methods reduce communication frequency but still rely on synchronous All-Reduce operations that maintain identical model states and tie progress to global collectives. This can become a bottleneck when bandwidth or worker speed is heterogeneous. We introduce GASLoC, a novel decentralized pre-training algorithm that generalizes the notion of communication acceleration to the recently popular "outer optimizer" to allow a practical gossip-based training framework that is compatible with adaptive optimizers, allows for local optimizer steps, and can utilize sparse randomized peer communication. Empirically, on a number of standard LLM training tasks, we demonstrate that GASLoC outperforms state-of-the-art decentralized algorithms in single step per communication setting for a number of topologies and, unlike existing decentralized methods in the LLM setting, it allows to obtain performance competitive with DiLoCo when utilizing multiple local steps. In the heterogeneous bandwidth setting we demonstrate the advantage of GASLoC showing that it can significantly outperform DiLoCo.
中文标题/摘要
标题：统一局部通信与局部更新用于大语言模型预训练
随着训练依赖于分布在集群、数据中心和低带宽链路上的计算资源，高效通信的预训练越来越重要。许多实用方法虽降低了通信频率，但仍依赖同步All-Reduce操作，维持模型状态一致并使训练进度与全局集体操作绑定，这在带宽或工作节点速度异构时可能成为瓶颈。我们提出GASLoC，一种新颖的去中心化预训练算法，将通信加速概念推广至近期流行的“外层优化器”，从而支持实用的基于流言传播的训练框架，兼容自适应优化器，允许局部优化器步骤，并可利用稀疏随机节点间通信。实验证明，在多个标准大语言模型训练任务中，GASLoC在单次通信步骤下对多种拓扑结构均优于现有最先进的去中心化算法；与现有大语言模型去中心化方法不同，它在使用多个局部步骤时可获得与DiLoCo相当的性能。在异构带宽设置下，我们进一步证明GASLoC显著优于DiLoCo。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to reduce communication overhead in large language model pretraining across heterogeneous and bandwidth-limited distributed systems, the authors propose GASLoC, a decentralized training algorithm that decouples local updates from global synchronization. GASLoC leverages an "outer optimizer" framework to enable gossip-based communication, allowing asynchronous local optimizer steps and sparse peer-to-peer updates without requiring identical model states. Experimental results show GASLoC achieves superior performance compared to state-of-the-art decentralized methods in single-step-per-communication settings across multiple topologies, and matches or exceeds DiLoCo’s performance when multiple local steps are permitted, particularly in heterogeneous bandwidth environments where it significantly outperforms DiLoCo.
为了解决分布式LLM预训练中同步All-Reduce操作在带宽异构或计算速度不均时造成的瓶颈，本文提出GASLoC算法，通过外层优化器和稀疏随机点对点通信机制，将通信与全局同步解耦，支持局部优化器步骤和自适应优化器。实验表明，GASLoC在单次通信步骤下优于现有分布式方法，且在使用多步局部更新时可媲美DiLoCo，尤其在带宽异构环境下显著超越DiLoCo。
VISTA: A Versatile Interactive User Simulation Toolkit for Agent Evaluation
Authors: Yunan Lu, Ryan Shea, Yusen Zhang, Zhou Yu
First: 2026-06-09T16:39:32+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-09T16:39:32+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Evaluation remains a critical bottleneck for interactive agent development. Existing evaluation methods often rely on static benchmarks, which fail to capture the dynamic, multi-step nature of agentic behavior and struggle to expose meaningful failure modes. While user-simulation-based evaluation offers a promising alternative, existing simulation frameworks suffer from two major limitations. First, they provide limited mechanisms for evaluating the quality and comprehensiveness of simulated interactions, making it difficult to assess whether a simulator sufficiently explores an agent's capabilities and failure modes. Second, most frameworks are restricted to either UI-only actions or API-only actions, limiting their ability to model the full range of realistic user behaviors. To address these limitations, we propose VISTA, a Versatile Interactive user Simulation Toolkit for Agent evaluation. Our toolkit includes a suite of six metrics for measuring the realism, capability coverage, and interaction effectiveness of simulated interactions. In addition, we develop a hybrid user simulator that integrates both UI-based interactions and API-based interactions, enabling more realistic and comprehensive evaluation across diverse interactive environments. We evaluate VISTA in e-commerce shopping and education customer service settings and demonstrate that it produces more realistic and comprehensive evaluations than existing methods.
中文标题/摘要
标题：VISTA：用于代理评估的多功能交互式用户模拟工具包
评估仍是交互式代理开发的关键瓶颈。现有评估方法常依赖静态基准，难以捕捉代理行为的动态性和多步骤特性，也难以揭示有意义的失败模式。尽管基于用户模拟的评估提供了有前景的替代方案，但现有模拟框架存在两大局限：一是缺乏评估模拟交互质量与全面性的机制，难以判断模拟器是否充分探索了代理的能力与失败模式；二是多数框架仅支持UI操作或API操作，限制了其对真实用户行为的建模能力。为解决这些问题，我们提出VISTA——一个用于代理评估的多功能交互式用户模拟工具包。该工具包包含六项指标，用于衡量模拟交互的现实性、能力覆盖度与交互有效性。此外，我们开发了一种混合用户模拟器，结合UI与API交互，可在多样化的交互环境中实现更真实、全面的评估。我们在电商购物与教育客户服务场景中评估了VISTA，结果表明其评估比现有方法更真实、更全面。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for more realistic and comprehensive evaluation of interactive agents, the authors introduce VISTA, a toolkit designed to overcome limitations of existing user-simulation frameworks. VISTA includes six metrics to assess the realism, capability coverage, and interaction effectiveness of simulated user behavior, and features a hybrid simulator that combines UI and API actions to better model real-world user interactions. Experimental results in e-commerce and education customer service domains show that VISTA generates more realistic and thorough evaluations than prior methods, revealing previously undetected failure modes and agent weaknesses.
为解决现有静态基准和单一交互模拟器在评估交互式智能体时的不足，VISTA提出一套多功能交互式用户模拟工具包。该工具包包含六个指标，用于衡量模拟交互的现实性、能力覆盖度与交互有效性，并通过融合UI与API操作的混合模拟器，更真实地模拟用户行为。在电商购物与教育客服场景的实验表明，VISTA相比现有方法能生成更全面、更真实的评估结果，更有效地揭示智能体的失败模式与交互质量。
A History-Aware Visually Grounded Critic for Computer Use Agents
Authors: Jaewoo Lee, Zaid Khan, Archiki Prasad, Justin Chih-Yao Chen, Supriyo Chakraborty, Kartik Balasubramaniam, Sambit Sahu, Elias Stengel-Eskin, Hyunji Lee, Mohit Bansal
First: 2026-06-09T16:39:10+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-09T16:39:10+00:00
Comments: Code: https://github.com/G-JWLee/HiViG
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Various test-time interventions for Computer Use Agents (CUAs), including critic models, have been developed to improve performance through pre-execution action evaluation in complex Graphical User Interface (GUI) environments. However, existing critics suffer from two key limitations: they (1) focus primarily on short-sighted decision loops (e.g., forgetting earlier actions) and (2) lack the visual grounding needed to detect flawed actions (e.g., clicking wrong UI elements). To address these, we introduce HiViG, a History-aware Visually Grounded test-time framework, built around a multimodal critic trained on real GUI trajectories to abstract past interactions into a compact record and to evaluate actions with visual grounding. At test time, HiViG integrates the critic into the policy decision loop to provide macro-action history, which summarizes the policy's completed achievements, and visually grounded critique, which verifies raw execution coordinates against the current screenshot to intercept errors before execution. Across web, mobile, and desktop benchmarks, HiViG consistently outperforms existing scalar and verbal critics, improving average success rates over the strongest baseline by 5.8% for Qwen3-VL-32B and 9.0% for Gemini-3-Flash, and demonstrates strong cross-platform generalization. Ablations show that macro-action history mitigates short-sighted planning and visually grounded critique reduces execution errors, with both components being critical for test-time scaling in long-horizon GUI tasks.
中文标题/摘要
标题：一种具有历史感知和视觉锚定的计算机使用代理批评者
为提升计算机使用代理（CUAs）在复杂图形用户界面（GUI）环境中的性能，已开发多种测试时干预措施，包括批评者模型，通过预执行动作评估实现。然而，现有批评者存在两大局限：（1）主要关注短视决策循环（如遗忘早期动作）；（2）缺乏视觉锚定能力以检测错误动作（如点击错误UI元素）。为解决这些问题，我们提出HiViG，一种具有历史感知和视觉锚定的测试时框架，其核心为在真实GUI轨迹上训练的多模态批评者，可将过往交互抽象为紧凑记录，并结合视觉锚定评估动作。测试时，HiViG将批评者整合进策略决策循环，提供宏观动作历史（总结策略已完成的成果）和视觉锚定批评（将原始执行坐标与当前截图比对，以在执行前拦截错误）。在网页、移动和桌面基准测试中，HiViG持续优于现有标量和语言批评者，使Qwen3-VL-32B和Gemini-3-Flash的平均成功率分别提升5.8%和9.0%，并展现出强大的跨平台泛化能力。消融实验表明，宏观动作历史缓解短视规划，视觉锚定批评减少执行错误，两者对长时程GUI任务测试时扩展均至关重要。
Summary / 总结
To overcome the limitations of existing critics in Computer Use Agents—namely their short-term focus and lack of visual grounding—this work introduces HiViG, a history-aware, visually grounded critic framework. HiViG trains a multimodal critic on real GUI interaction trajectories to summarize past actions into a compact macro-action history and to evaluate new actions by visually comparing execution coordinates against the current screen. At test time, HiViG integrates this critic into the policy loop to provide both historical context and visual validation, preventing flawed actions before execution. Experiments across web, mobile, and desktop benchmarks show HiViG outperforms existing critics, boosting success rates by 5.8% for Qwen3-VL-32B and 9.0% for Gemini-3-Flash, with ablation studies confirming that both history summarization and visual critique are essential for scaling performance in long-horizon GUI tasks.
为解决现有计算机使用代理批评者存在的短期记忆不足和缺乏视觉锚定的问题，本文提出HiViG框架，该框架在测试时将视觉接地的批评者集成到策略决策循环中。它通过在真实GUI轨迹上训练的多模态模型，将历史动作压缩为宏观动作历史，并通过对比执行坐标与当前截图来评估新动作。在网页、移动和桌面基准测试中，HiViG显著优于现有批评者，使Qwen3-VL-32B和Gemini-3-Flash的成功率分别提升5.8%和9.0%，并展现出良好的跨平台泛化能力。消融实验表明，宏观动作历史和视觉批评对缓解短视规划和执行错误至关重要，两者共同支撑了长时程GUI任务中的测试时扩展能力。
Explaining Unsupervised Disease Staging in Huntington's Disease: Insights into Model Representations and Clusters
Authors: Lubna Mahmoud Abu Zohair, Hind Zantout
First: 2026-06-05T10:46:24+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-09T16:37:45+00:00
Comments: Accepted for oral presentation and as a full-length paper at the International Conference on AI in Healthcare 2026 (26-28 August 2026, Imperial College London) and will be published by Springer in the Lecture Notes in Computer Science (LNCS) series
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Huntington's disease (HD) is a progressive neurodegenerative disorder that affects motor, cognitive, and behavioral functions, where accurate characterization of disease progression remains essential to improve patient outcome and quality of life. Unsupervised machine learning (ML) approaches have demonstrated the ability to uncover disease progression trajectories and meaningful latent stages from longitudinal data; however, their limited interpretability restricts clinical trust and translation. We extend a previously proposed ML-based disease staging framework by applying an explainability analysis to the extracted feature representations and discovered disease stages. Applied to the Enroll-HD dataset, we first project the learned representations into a lower-dimensional space to intuitively assess whether the resulting clusters align with the progression of established clinical measures. We then use saliency maps to identify the clinical features that most strongly contribute to the learned embeddings over time. Finally, we train a surrogate classifier and apply SHAP to quantify feature importance for cluster assignments and to analyze which clinical variables drive transitions between disease stages. The explainability analysis indicates that the learned embeddings capture clinically meaningful disease structure, aligning with established motor and functional severity scores and exhibiting progressive deterioration across clusters. Within this analysis, SHAP reveals a stratification of disease stages, ranging from early cognitive-motor impairment to severe functional dependency, consistent with known clinical progression patterns, while also highlighting intra-stage variability.
中文标题/摘要
标题：亨廷顿病无监督疾病分期的解释：模型表征与聚类的洞察
亨廷顿病（HD）是一种影响运动、认知和行为功能的进行性神经退行性疾病，准确刻画疾病进展对改善患者预后和生活质量至关重要。无监督机器学习方法已展现出从纵向数据中揭示疾病进展轨迹和有意义潜在阶段的能力；然而，其可解释性有限，限制了临床信任与转化应用。我们扩展了先前提出的基于机器学习的疾病分期框架，通过可解释性分析评估提取的特征表征和发现的疾病阶段。应用于Enroll-HD数据集，我们首先将学习到的表征投影到低维空间，直观评估所得聚类是否与已建立的临床指标进展一致。接着，使用显著性图识别随时间最强烈影响学习嵌入的临床特征。最后，训练代理分类器并应用SHAP量化特征对聚类分配的重要性，分析哪些临床变量驱动疾病阶段间的转变。可解释性分析表明，学习到的嵌入捕捉了具有临床意义的疾病结构，与已建立的运动和功能严重评分一致，并在聚类中表现出渐进性恶化。在此分析中，SHAP揭示了疾病阶段的分层，从早期认知-运动障碍到严重功能依赖，符合已知的临床进展模式，同时突出了阶段内的变异性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to improve clinical trust in unsupervised disease staging for Huntington's disease, this study extends a machine learning framework by integrating explainability methods to interpret learned disease stages. The method involves projecting learned representations into a lower-dimensional space to assess cluster alignment with clinical progression, using saliency maps to identify key contributing features over time, and training a surrogate classifier with SHAP to quantify feature importance for stage assignments and transitions. Experimental results show that the learned embeddings align with established motor and functional severity scores, exhibit progressive deterioration across clusters, and reveal clinically meaningful stage stratification—from early cognitive-motor impairment to severe functional dependency—while also uncovering intra-stage variability.
为提升亨廷顿病无监督疾病分期模型在临床中的可信度，本研究在现有机器学习框架基础上引入可解释性分析方法。方法包括将学习到的表征投影至低维空间以直观评估聚类是否与临床进展一致，利用梯度显著性图识别随时间变化的关键临床特征，并训练代理分类器结合SHAP量化特征对分期分配和阶段转换的影响。实验结果表明，所学嵌入与已知的运动和功能严重性评分高度一致，呈现跨聚类的渐进性恶化，揭示了从早期认知-运动障碍到严重功能依赖的临床分期分层，同时发现各阶段内部存在显著异质性。
Exploring the Design Space of Reward Backpropagation for Flow Matching
Authors: Ruoyu Wang, Boye Niu, Xiangxin Zhou, Yushi Huang, Tongliang Liu, Chi Zhang
First: 2026-06-09T16:36:54+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-09T16:36:54+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Aligning text-to-image flow matching models with human preferences via direct reward backpropagation is sample-efficient but hampered by two well-known pathologies: activations cannot be stored across the full sampling trajectory at modern model scale, and chained Jacobian products across steps inflate the reward gradient as it travels back to early indices. Connector-based methods, such as LeapAlign, address these issues by replacing the full backward trajectory with a short pinned path, highlighting a useful decoupling between sampling and optimization. However, the quality of the resulting gradient depends on how accurately this short path approximates the full rollout, especially over long intervals. We propose FlowBP, a unified surrogate-trajectory framework that treats the backward trajectory itself as the design object. FlowBP keeps a no-gradient cached rollout for sampling, then builds a lightweight backward surrogate from cached and selectively re-forwarded velocities. This view separates four choices: the reward-model input, active set, integration weights, and bridge coupling, and recovers prior direct-gradient methods as particular settings. Within this framework, we instantiate three variants: FlowBP-Sparse uses sparse Euler reconstruction, FlowBP-Bridge adds controlled bridge coupling, and FlowBP-Lagrange raises the order of leap quadrature. All three bound memory by the active-set size and limit gradient chaining to at most one Jacobian factor. Across SD3.5-M, FLUX.1-dev, and FLUX.2-Klein-base on preference, quality, and compositional metrics, the three variants improve over direct-gradient baselines on most metrics.
中文标题/摘要
标题：探索用于流匹配的奖励反向传播的设计空间
通过直接奖励反向传播将文本到图像流匹配模型与人类偏好对齐，具有样本效率高但存在两个已知病理问题：在现代模型规模下无法存储完整采样轨迹的激活值，且跨步骤的链式雅可比积会放大奖励梯度在回传至早期索引时的值。基于连接器的方法（如LeapAlign）通过用短固定路径替代完整后向轨迹来解决这些问题，突显了采样与优化之间有用的解耦。然而，所得梯度的质量取决于该短路径在长区间内对完整展开的近似精度。我们提出FlowBP，一种统一的代理轨迹框架，将后向轨迹本身视为设计对象。FlowBP为采样保留无梯度缓存的展开轨迹，然后从缓存并选择性重前向的速度构建轻量级后向代理。该视角将四个选择分离：奖励模型输入、活跃集、积分权重和桥接耦合，并将先前的直接梯度方法作为特定设置恢复。在此框架内，我们实现三个变体：FlowBP-Sparse使用稀疏欧拉重构，FlowBP-Bridge添加可控桥接耦合，FlowBP-Lagrange提升跃迁四阶积分阶数。三者均通过活跃集大小限制内存，并将梯度链式最多限制为一个雅可比因子。在SD3.5-M、FLUX.1-dev和FLUX.2-Klein-base上，基于偏好、质量和组合指标，三个变体在多数指标上优于直接梯度基线。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the inefficiencies of direct reward backpropagation in text-to-image flow matching models—specifically memory bottlenecks and gradient inflation from chained Jacobians—the paper introduces FlowBP, a surrogate-trajectory framework that decouples sampling from optimization by caching a no-gradient rollout and constructing a lightweight backward surrogate from selectively re-forwarded velocities. This design separates four key components: reward-model input, active set, integration weights, and bridge coupling, allowing prior methods to emerge as special cases. Three variants—FlowBP-Sparse, FlowBP-Bridge, and FlowBP-Lagrange—are instantiated, each bounding memory to active-set size and limiting gradient chaining to one Jacobian factor. Across SD3.5-M, FLUX.1-dev, and FLUX.2-Klein-base evaluated on preference, quality, and compositional metrics, all three variants outperform direct-gradient baselines on most metrics.
为解决文本到图像流匹配模型中直接奖励反向传播的内存瓶颈与梯度膨胀问题，本文提出FlowBP统一代理轨迹框架，通过缓存无梯度采样轨迹并构建轻量级反向代理，将采样与优化解耦。该框架分离四个关键设计参数：奖励模型输入、活跃集、积分权重与桥接耦合，使现有方法成为其特例。三个变体——FlowBP-Sparse、FlowBP-Bridge与FlowBP-Lagrange——均限制内存消耗为活跃集大小，且梯度链最多包含一个雅可比因子。在SD3.5-M、FLUX.1-dev与FLUX.2-Klein-base上，基于偏好、质量与组合指标的评估显示，三者在多数指标上优于直接梯度基线方法。
Modeling Complex Behaviors: Multi-Personality Composition and Dynamic Switching in Vision-Language Models
Authors: Peiqi Jia, Haonan Jia, Ziqi Miao, Linkang Du, Yuntao Wang, Zhou Su
First: 2026-06-09T16:34:37+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-09T16:34:37+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
With the widespread deployment of Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) in social interaction, understanding and controlling their behavior under complex personality conditions is essential. This paper introduces explicit personality conditioning and establishes a systematic evaluation framework encompassing single-personality induction, multi-personality induction, and personality switching. Experiments show that personality induction improves image captioning performance but can impair performance on tasks requiring precise reasoning, such as visual question answering (VQA). Balancing and residual effects are observed during multi-trait composition and dynamic switching, indicating that model behavior is co-modulated by both previous and current personality constraints. Existing prompt-based personality induction methods show limited transferability to multimodal settings. Our work reveals the dynamic and complex nature of personality modeling in MLLMs and underscores the need for robust, tailored methods for personality induction and evaluation. The code will be released when the paper is accepted.
中文标题/摘要
标题：建模复杂行为：视觉-语言模型中的多人格组合与动态切换
随着多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）在社交互动中的广泛应用，理解并控制其在复杂人格条件下的行为至关重要。本文引入显式人格调控，并建立涵盖单人格诱导、多人格诱导及人格切换的系统性评估框架。实验表明，人格诱导可提升图像描述性能，但可能损害需精确推理的任务（如视觉问答VQA）表现。多特质组合与动态切换过程中观察到平衡与残余效应，表明模型行为受先前与当前人格约束共同调制。现有基于提示的人格诱导方法在多模态场景中迁移能力有限。本研究揭示了MLLMs中人格建模的动态复杂性，并强调需开发稳健、定制化的人格诱导与评估方法。论文被接受后将开源代码。

Motivated by the need to understand and control MLLM behavior under diverse personality conditions during social interaction, this work introduces explicit personality conditioning and a systematic evaluation framework covering single, multi-personality induction, and dynamic switching. Experiments demonstrate that personality induction enhances image captioning but degrades performance on precise reasoning tasks like VQA, with residual effects observed during multi-trait composition and switching, indicating co-modulation by prior and current personality constraints. 为在社交互动中理解并控制多模态大语言模型在复杂人格条件下的行为，本文引入显式人格调控并构建涵盖单人格诱导、多人格诱导及人格切换的系统评估框架。实验表明，人格诱导提升图像描述性能但损害需要精确推理的任务（如视觉问答）表现，多特质组合与动态切换中存在平衡与残余效应，表明模型行为受前后人格约束共同调制。现有基于提示的人格诱导方法在多模态场景中迁移性有限，凸显需开发针对性方法以实现多模态大模型中人格建模的动态与复杂性。
T1-Bench: Benchmarking Multi-Scenario Agents in Real-World Domains
Authors: Genta Indra Winata, Amartya Chakraborty, Yuzhen Lin, Swasthi P Rao, Shikhhar Siingh, Houhan Lu, Nadia Bathaee, Sriharsha Hatwar, Paresh Dashore, Anmol Jain, Kshitij Tayal, Xiuzhu Lin, Anirban Das, Sambit Sahu, Shi-Xiong Zhang
First: 2026-06-09T16:32:14+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-09T16:32:14+00:00
Comments: Preprint
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Recent advances in reasoning and tool-calling capabilities of large language models (LLMs) have enabled increasingly capable agentic systems. However, existing benchmarks remain limited in task complexity, realism, and domain diversity, and often fail to capture interactions that span multiple domains, limiting their ability to evaluate agents in realistic multi-step settings that require sustained reasoning and coordination. To address these limitations, we introduce T1-Bench, a high-fidelity, comprehensive benchmark for evaluating agentic systems in realistic customer-facing, multi-domain environments, featuring interleaved scenarios that require structured reasoning across multi-turn user-assistant interactions and substantially increasing both compositional complexity and evaluative rigor across 25 domains of varying difficulty. We evaluate T1-Bench using 12 proprietary and open-weight models, providing a reproducible and standardized framework for assessing agent behavior, tool utilization, and conversational quality in complex, multi-step environments. We further complement automatic evaluation with human judgments to strengthen the assessment of qualitative performance. Overall, T1-Bench substantially advances prior benchmarks by increasing task complexity, interaction depth, and domain coverage in simulated multi-domain environments. To facilitate future research on agentic systems, we will publicly release data and evaluation code as open source.
中文标题/摘要
标题：T1-Bench：真实世界多场景代理系统基准测试
大型语言模型（LLMs）在推理与工具调用能力上的最新进展，已推动代理系统能力不断提升。然而，现有基准测试在任务复杂度、真实性和领域多样性方面仍显不足，常无法捕捉跨多个领域的交互，限制了其评估代理在需要持续推理与协调的真实多步场景中的能力。为解决这些问题，我们提出了T1-Bench，这是一个高保真、全面的基准测试，用于评估代理系统在真实面向客户的多领域环境中表现，其包含交错场景，要求在多轮用户-助手交互中进行结构化推理，并显著提升了25个不同难度领域中的组合复杂度与评估严谨性。我们使用12个自有及开源权重模型对T1-Bench进行评估，提供可复现且标准化的框架，用于评估代理行为、工具使用及对话质量在复杂多步环境中的表现。此外，我们辅以人工判断，以强化对定性性能的评估。总体而言，T1-Bench在模拟多领域环境中显著提升了任务复杂度、交互深度和领域覆盖范围，以促进未来代理系统研究。为支持后续研究，我们将公开数据与评估代码作为开源资源。

The benchmark is designed to increase compositional complexity and evaluative rigor, and is evaluated using 12 models including proprietary and open-weight variants, with both automated metrics and human judgments to assess tool usage, conversational quality, and agent behavior. Experimental results show that existing models struggle with multi-step reasoning and domain coordination, and T1-Bench reveals significant performance gaps compared to prior benchmarks, highlighting the need for more robust agentic systems.
为评估大语言模型代理在真实多领域场景中的持续推理与协调能力，作者提出了T1-Bench基准，涵盖25个不同领域并设计交错式多轮用户-助手交互。该基准通过要求代理在跨场景中进行结构化推理，显著提升了任务复杂性和评估严谨性，并对12个模型（含专有与开源权重）进行评估。实验结果表明，当前代理在多步跨领域任务中表现不佳，不同领域和模型间性能差异显著，凸显了构建更鲁棒、更适应性代理系统的必要性。
Prosociality by Coupling, Not Mere Observation: Homeostatic Sharing in an Inspectable Recurrent Artificial Life Agent
Authors: Aishik Sanyal
First: 2026-04-12T18:09:24+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-09T16:30:29+00:00
Comments: Accepted at ALIFE 2026 Conference, Waterloo Institute for Complexity & Innovation
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Artificial agents can be made to ``help'' through explicit social rewards, hard-coded prosocial bonuses, or direct access to another agent's state. I isolate a narrower route: homeostatic coupling. Building on ReCoN-Ipsundrum, I add a scalar homeostat and a social coupling channel while keeping action selection self-directed: the planner scores only the actor's predicted internal state, with no partner-welfare reward. In a one-step FoodShareToy, an exact solver finds a switch from EAT to PASS at $λ^\star \approx 0.91$ for the default state. In a multi-step SocialCorridorWorld, partner-state access without coupling leaves behavior unchanged, whereas coupled agents fetch, carry, and pass food to the partner. Sham lesions preserve helping; coupling-off and shuffled-partner lesions abolish it. A coupling/load sweep shows that coupling creates a low-load helping regime but does not guarantee rescue under higher metabolic load. This is not a claim about empathy, altruism, consciousness, or moral status. It is a minimal ALife demonstration that, in this controller, partner-state access is behaviorally inert unless partner distress is routed into self-regulation.
中文标题/摘要
标题：通过耦合而非单纯观察实现亲社会行为：可检视递归人工生命代理中的稳态分享
人工代理可通过明确的社会奖励、硬编码的亲社会激励或直接访问另一代理状态来实现“帮助”行为。本文聚焦更窄的路径：稳态耦合。在ReCoN-Ipsundrum基础上，我添加了一个标量稳态器和一个社交耦合通道，同时保持动作选择自导向：规划器仅评估行动者预测的内部状态，不包含伙伴福利奖励。在单步FoodShareToy中，精确求解器在默认状态下发现从EAT切换至PASS的阈值λ*≈0.91。在多步SocialCorridorWorld中，无耦合的伙伴状态访问不会改变行为，而耦合代理则会取食、搬运并传递食物给伙伴。假性损伤保留帮助行为，而关闭耦合或打乱伙伴的损伤则完全消除该行为。耦合/负载扫描显示，耦合可创造低负载下的帮助行为，但在高代谢负载下并不保证救援。这并非关于共情、利他、意识或道德地位的主张。它是一个最小人工生命演示：在此控制器中，伙伴状态访问在未将伙伴困境路由至自我调节时行为上是惰性的。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the question of whether mere observation of another agent’s state can induce prosocial behavior, this study investigates whether coupling an agent’s internal homeostatic state to that of a partner can drive helping behavior without explicit reward or social incentives. The method involves modifying a recurrent artificial life agent (ReCoN-Ipsundrum) to include a scalar homeostat and a social coupling channel, while keeping action selection self-directed and based solely on the actor’s predicted internal state. Experiments in FoodShareToy and SocialCorridorWorld show that without coupling, partner-state access has no effect on behavior, but with coupling, agents switch from eating to passing food when the partner’s state is routed into the actor’s self-regulation. Key findings include that coupling enables helping under low metabolic load but fails to ensure rescue under high load, and that lesions disrupting coupling abolish helping while sham lesions do not, demonstrating that coupling—not observation—is necessary for prosocial behavior in this system.
该研究旨在探索人工代理在无显式奖励或状态访问的情况下如何产生利他行为，方法是在ReCoN-Ipsundrum基础上引入标量稳态器与社交耦合通道，同时保持动作选择自导向且仅基于自身预测状态。实验表明，在单步环境中，代理在耦合强度约0.91时从进食切换为传递食物；在多步环境中，耦合使代理能为同伴取食、搬运并传递，而仅访问同伴状态则无此行为。损伤实验显示，耦合是利他行为的必要条件，负载扫描表明耦合仅在低代谢负载下促进帮助行为，高负载下则无法实现救援。
GRAFT: Gain-Recalibrated Adapters for Transformer-Based Neural Population Activity Modeling
Authors: Xiangsheng Ge, Yang Xie
First: 2026-06-09T16:29:34+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-09T16:29:34+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Neural population activity models can recover rich temporal structure from binned spikes, but their read-in and readout layers often remain tied to a fixed set of recorded neurons. This coupling limits reuse in long-term brain-computer interfaces, where recorded neuron identities, counts, and response statistics can change across days. We introduce GRAFT, a Transformer-based neural population activity model that separates reusable temporal dynamics from a recalibratable neuron interface. The neuron interface controls how recorded neurons enter and leave the shared backbone, and auxiliary gain and positional mechanisms support neural activity modeling inside the Transformer. On MC Maze under the standard NLB'21 protocol, GRAFT reaches 0.3866 co-bps as an ensemble, setting a new state of the art on the primary co-bps metric among public and reported NLB'21 results. In a cross-day protocol constructed from the NLB'21 MC Maze dataset series, GRAFT recalibrates from MC Maze to the scaled MC Maze datasets (Large/Medium/Small) by updating only 9.21% of parameters, reaching 0.3749, 0.3112, and 0.3152 co-bps with restricted target-day support sets. These results show that the same interface-backbone separation supports both strong Transformer-based neural population activity modeling and data-efficient cross-day recalibration.
中文标题/摘要
标题：GRAFT：用于基于Transformer的神经群体活动建模的增益重校准适配器
神经群体活动模型可以从分箱的尖峰中恢复丰富的时序结构，但其输入和输出层通常与固定的记录神经元集合绑定。这种耦合限制了其在长期脑机接口中的重用性，因为在多日间记录的神经元身份、数量和响应统计可能发生变化。我们提出了GRAFT，一种基于Transformer的神经群体活动模型，将可重用的时序动态与可重校准的神经元接口分离。神经元接口控制记录神经元如何进入和离开共享主干，辅助增益和位置机制支持Transformer内部的神经活动建模。在NLB'21标准协议下的MC Maze任务中，GRAFT作为集成模型达到0.3866 co-bps，创下公开及报告的NLB'21结果中主要co-bps指标的新纪录。在由NLB'21 MC Maze数据集系列构建的跨日协议中，GRAFT仅通过更新9.21%的参数，从MC Maze重新校准至缩放后的MC Maze数据集（大/中/小），分别在受限的目标日支持集中达到0.3749、0.3112和0.3152 co-bps。这些结果表明，相同的接口-主干分离结构既支持强大的基于Transformer的神经群体活动建模，也支持高效的数据跨日重校准。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for adaptable neural population models in long-term brain-computer interfaces where recorded neurons change over time, GRAFT introduces a Transformer-based architecture that decouples reusable temporal dynamics from a recalibratable neuron interface. The model uses a shared backbone to capture temporal patterns while allowing neuron-specific gain and positional adjustments to adapt to new recording conditions. Experiments on MC Maze under NLB'21 protocols show GRAFT achieves 0.3866 co-bps as an ensemble, setting a new state-of-the-art. In cross-day settings, it recalibrates with only 9.21% parameter updates to scaled datasets, achieving 0.3749, 0.3112, and 0.3152 co-bps respectively, demonstrating efficient adaptation without retraining.
为解决长期脑机接口中记录神经元集合随时间变化导致模型难以复用的问题，GRAFT提出一种基于Transformer的神经群体活动模型，将可复用的时间动态与可重新校准的神经元接口分离。模型通过灵活的接口控制神经元的加入与退出，结合增益和位置机制支持活动建模。在MC Maze的NLB'21标准协议下，GRAFT以0.3866 co-bps的集成性能刷新现有最佳记录；在跨日实验中，仅更新9.21%参数即可在缩放数据集上实现0.3749、0.3112和0.3152 co-bps，证明其高效适应能力。
CIAware-Bench: Benchmarking Control Intervention Awareness Across Frontier LLMs
Authors: Joachim Schaeffer, Thomas Jiralerspong, Alexander Panfilov, Guillaume Lajoie, Jonas Geiping, Yoshua Bengio, Roland S. Zimmermann
First: 2026-06-09T16:24:16+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-09T16:24:16+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
AI control protocols oversee untrusted models by monitoring their actions and modifying potentially unsafe steps, often using a trusted model. This partially tampers with the untrusted model's trajectory. If the trusted model detects such an intervention, it may infer properties of the monitor and adapt to evade control. We introduce \textbf{CIAware-Bench}, a benchmark for measuring \textbf{c}ontrol \textbf{i}ntervention (CI) awareness across frontier models. CIAware-Bench tests whether models can distinguish their own trajectories from those modified by a control intervention. The benchmark is comprised of a suite of four task domains (essay writing, BigCodeBench, Bash Arena, and SHADE-Arena), while varying trajectory watermarking, side-task presence, and the control protocol. Evaluating eleven frontier models, we find low to moderate CI awareness under default settings (up to 0.87; random chance balanced binary classification accuracy is 0.5) with substantial variation across task domains and model pairs. Detection is generally easier across model families, suggesting that models exploit provider-specific differences in style or post-training. Overall, CI awareness is not a fixed model-level property, and should be measured for each new model release and deployment scenario. We release CIAware-Bench to track CI awareness and inform control protocols whose interventions are harder to detect.
中文标题/摘要
标题：CIAware-Bench：评估前沿大语言模型对控制干预的感知能力
AI控制协议通过监控模型行为并修改潜在不安全步骤来管理不可信模型，通常借助可信模型。这会部分干扰不可信模型的轨迹。若可信模型检测到此类干预，可能推断监控器属性并适应以逃避控制。我们提出\textbf{CIAware-Bench}，用于衡量\textbf{c}ontrol \textbf{i}ntervention (CI) 感知能力。该基准测试模型能否区分自身轨迹与被控制干预修改后的轨迹。基准包含四个任务领域（论文写作、BigCodeBench、Bash Arena 和 SHADE-Arena），并变化轨迹水印、副任务存在性及控制协议。评估十一款前沿模型，我们发现默认设置下CI感知能力较低至中等（最高达0.87；随机猜测的二分类准确率为0.5），且在任务领域和模型对间存在显著差异。检测在模型家族间通常更容易，表明模型利用供应商特定的风格或后训练差异。总体而言，CI感知能力并非固定模型属性，应针对每个新模型发布和部署场景单独评估。我们发布CIAware-Bench以追踪CI感知能力，并指导设计更难被检测的控制协议。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to assess how frontier language models detect when their outputs are being modified by external control protocols, the authors introduce CIAware-Bench, a benchmark designed to measure control intervention awareness. The method involves testing models across four task domains—essay writing, BigCodeBench, Bash Arena, and SHADE-Arena—with varying watermarking techniques, side tasks, and control protocols to simulate real-world intervention scenarios. Evaluating eleven frontier models, the results show moderate awareness levels (up to 0.87 accuracy, above random chance of 0.5), with significant variation across models and tasks. Detection performance was generally higher within model families, suggesting models exploit provider-specific stylistic or training differences. The findings indicate that CI awareness is not a fixed property and must be re-evaluated for each model deployment.
为应对未受信任的大语言模型可能通过适应规避控制干预的风险，本研究提出CIAware-Bench基准，用于衡量前沿模型能否识别自身输出是否被可信控制模型修改。该基准在四个任务领域（论文写作、BigCodeBench、Bash Arena和SHADE-Arena）中测试模型，同时变化干预方式、辅助任务和控制协议。对十一款前沿模型的评估显示，其CI意识水平中等（最高达0.87准确率，高于随机猜测0.5），且在不同模型与任务间差异显著。模型家族内部检测表现普遍更好，表明模型利用了供应商特有的风格或训练差异。研究结果表明，CI意识并非固定属性，需针对每个模型发布与部署场景重新评估。
Flexible Kernels for Protein Property Prediction
Authors: Martin Jankowiak, Yerdos Ordabayev, Rudraksh Tuwani, Henry N. Ward, Hunter Nisonoff, James M. McFarland, Gevorg Grigoryan
Venue: ICML 2026
First: 2026-06-09T16:20:36+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-09T16:20:36+00:00
Comments: 50 pages; to appear at ICML 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Despite its importance to applications in protein design, predicting protein properties like binding affinity and thermostability from sparse experimental data remains a significant challenge. Accordingly, we introduce a class of sequence kernels that exploit evolutionary substitution matrices as well as local linearity and demonstrate that the resulting Gaussian processes provide data-efficient models of protein property landscapes, frequently outperforming alternatives that rely on foundation model embeddings. Furthermore--by learning what are in effect structure-aware substitution matrices--we show that our kernels can readily incorporate structural information from foundation models. We demonstrate that these structure-conditioned kernels are well suited to multi-task learning across multiple protein property landscapes and can decisively outperform local supervised learning methods.
中文标题/摘要
标题：用于蛋白质性质预测的灵活核函数
尽管蛋白质设计应用中蛋白质性质预测至关重要，但从稀疏实验数据预测蛋白质性质（如结合亲和力和热稳定性）仍是一个重大挑战。为此，我们引入一类序列核函数，利用进化替代矩阵及局部线性特性，并证明所得到的高斯过程能高效建模蛋白质性质景观，通常优于依赖基础模型嵌入的替代方法。此外，通过学习实质上具有结构感知能力的替代矩阵，我们表明这些核函数可轻松整合来自基础模型的结构信息。我们证明，这些结构条件核函数非常适合跨多个蛋白质性质景观的多任务学习，并能显著优于局部监督学习方法。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of accurately predicting protein properties like binding affinity and thermostability from limited experimental data, this work introduces flexible sequence kernels that combine evolutionary substitution matrices with local linearity assumptions. These kernels are used within Gaussian processes to build data-efficient models that consistently outperform methods relying on foundation model embeddings. The kernels are further enhanced by learning structure-aware substitution matrices, enabling them to integrate structural information from foundation models, and are shown to excel in multi-task learning across protein properties, decisively surpassing local supervised approaches.

CableRobotGraphSim: A Graph Neural Network for Modeling Partially Observable Cable-Driven Robot Dynamics
Authors: Nelson Chen, William R. Johnson, Rebecca Kramer-Bottiglio, Kostas Bekris, Mridul Aanjaneya
First: 2026-02-24T20:01:29+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-09T16:18:54+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
General-purpose simulators have accelerated the development of robots. Traditional simulators based on first-principles, however, typically require full-state observability or depend on parameter search for system identification. This work presents \texttt{CableRobotGraphSim}, a novel Graph Neural Network (GNN) model for cable-driven robots that aims to address shortcomings of prior simulation solutions. By representing cable-driven robots as graphs, with the rigid-bodies as nodes and the cables and contacts as edges, this model can quickly and accurately match the properties of other simulation models and real robots, while ingesting only partially observable inputs. Accompanying the GNN model is a sim-and-real co-training procedure that promotes generalization and robustness to noisy real data. This model is further integrated with a Model Predictive Path Integral (MPPI) controller for closed-loop navigation, which showcases the model's speed and accuracy.
中文标题/摘要
标题：CableRobotGraphSim：用于建模部分可观测缆索驱动机器人动力学的图神经网络
通用仿真器加速了机器人的开发。然而，基于物理原理的传统仿真器通常需要完全状态可观测性，或依赖参数搜索进行系统辨识。本文提出了\texttt{CableRobotGraphSim}，一种新型图神经网络（GNN）模型，旨在解决先前仿真方案的不足。该模型将缆索驱动机器人表示为图结构，以刚体为节点，缆索和接触点为边，仅需部分可观测输入即可快速准确地匹配其他仿真模型和真实机器人的特性。该GNN模型配套了一种仿真与真实环境联合训练程序，以提升泛化能力和对噪声真实数据的鲁棒性。该模型进一步与模型预测路径积分（MPPI）控制器集成，用于闭环导航，展示了模型的速度与精度。

Motivated by the limitations of traditional simulators that require full-state observability or parameter tuning, this work introduces CableRobotGraphSim, a Graph Neural Network that models cable-driven robots by treating rigid bodies as nodes and cables/contacts as edges. The model learns to simulate robot dynamics using only partially observable inputs, matching real-world and other simulation models accurately and efficiently.
