为解决传统仿真器需全状态可观测或参数调优的局限，本文提出CableRobotGraphSim，一种基于图神经网络的电缆驱动机器人仿真模型，将刚体建模为节点、电缆与接触建模为边。该模型仅需部分可观测输入即可准确模拟机器人动力学，匹配真实机器人与其它仿真器行为。通过仿真与真实环境协同训练并结合MPPI控制器实现闭环导航，模型展现出良好的泛化能力与对噪声数据的鲁棒性，在动态环境中实现高速高精度控制。
WorldPlay: Towards Long-Term Geometric Consistency for Real-Time Interactive World Modeling
Authors: Wenqiang Sun, Haiyu Zhang, Haoyuan Wang, Junta Wu, Zehan Wang, Zhenwei Wang, Yunhong Wang, Jun Zhang, Tengfei Wang, Chunchao Guo
First: 2025-12-16T17:22:46+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-09T16:18:08+00:00
Comments: project page: https://3d-models.hunyuan.tencent.com/world/, demo: https://3d.hunyuan.tencent.com/sceneTo3D, code: https://github.com/Tencent-Hunyuan/HY-WorldPlay
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
This paper presents WorldPlay, a streaming video diffusion model that enables real-time, interactive world modeling with long-term geometric consistency, resolving the trade-off between speed and memory that limits current methods. WorldPlay draws power from three key ingredients. 1) We use a Dual Action Representation to enable robust action control in response to the user's keyboard and mouse inputs. 2) To enforce long-term consistency, our Reconstituted Context Memory dynamically rebuilds context from past frames and uses temporal reframing to keep geometrically important but long-past frames accessible, effectively alleviating memory attenuation. 3) We also propose Context Forcing, a novel distillation method designed for memory-aware model. Aligning memory context between the teacher and student preserves the student's capacity to use long-range information, enabling real-time speeds while preventing error drift. Taken together, WorldPlay generates long-horizon streaming 720p video at 24 FPS with superior consistency, comparing favorably with existing techniques and showing strong generalization across diverse scenes. Project page and online demo can be found: https://3d-models.hunyuan.tencent.com/world/ and https://3d.hunyuan.tencent.com/sceneTo3D.
中文标题/摘要
标题：WorldPlay：面向实时交互世界建模的长期几何一致性
本文提出WorldPlay，一种流式视频扩散模型，支持实时交互式世界建模并保持长期几何一致性，解决了当前方法中速度与内存之间的权衡问题。WorldPlay基于三大核心要素：1）采用双动作表示，实现对用户键盘和鼠标输入的鲁棒动作控制；2）通过重构上下文记忆动态重建过往帧上下文，并利用时间重帧技术保持几何重要但久远帧的可访问性，有效缓解内存衰减；3）提出上下文强制，一种面向内存感知模型的新型蒸馏方法，通过教师与学生间记忆上下文对齐，保留学生模型对长程信息的利用能力，实现实时速度同时避免误差漂移。综合来看，WorldPlay可在24 FPS下生成720p长时程流式视频，一致性表现优越，相较现有技术更具优势，且在多样场景中展现出强泛化能力。项目页面与在线演示见：https://3d-models.hunyuan.tencent.com/world/ 和 https://3d.hunyuan.tencent.com/sceneTo3D。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for real-time interactive world modeling with long-term geometric consistency, WorldPlay introduces a streaming video diffusion model that resolves the speed-memory trade-off. It employs a Dual Action Representation for responsive user input, a Reconstituted Context Memory to dynamically reconstruct and preserve geometrically relevant past frames, and Context Forcing—a distillation technique that aligns teacher and student memory contexts to maintain long-range information without error drift. Experiments show WorldPlay generates 720p video at 24 FPS with superior consistency across diverse scenes, outperforming existing methods in both fidelity and generalization.
为实现实时交互式世界建模并保持长期几何一致性，WorldPlay提出一种流式视频扩散模型，有效平衡速度与内存效率。其采用双动作表示实现对用户键盘和鼠标输入的鲁棒控制，重构上下文记忆动态重建并保留几何重要历史帧，同时引入上下文强制蒸馏方法，对教师与学生模型的记忆上下文进行对齐，以保留长程信息并避免误差累积。实验表明，WorldPlay可在24帧/秒下生成720p视频，几何一致性显著优于现有方法，并在多种场景中展现出强泛化能力。
Attention Amnesia in Hybrid LLMs: When CoT Fine-Tuning Breaks Long-Range Recall, and How to Fix It
Authors: Xinyu Zhou, Boyu Zhu, Yi Xu, Zhiwei Li, Yingfa Chen, Huiming Wang, Zhijiang Guo
First: 2026-06-09T16:17:19+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-09T16:17:19+00:00
Comments: 28 pages
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Chain-of-thought (CoT) supervised fine-tuning (SFT) is widely adopted to improve reasoning ability, yet we find that it systematically degrades long-context recall in hybrid linear-attention models. Across architectures including HypeNet and Jet-Nemotron, retrieval performance on Needle-In-A-Haystack (NIAH) deteriorates substantially after CoT-SFT, and the degradation becomes more severe under harder retrieval settings and longer context windows. For example, HypeNet-9B on NIAH-S2@256K decreases from $67.2\%$ to $9.4\%$. We attribute this to CoT-SFT biasing attention gradients toward short-range patterns, disrupting query-key projections ($W_Q, W_K$) that are responsible for long-range routing. Motivated by this observation, we propose QK-Restore, a training-free method that restores only $W_Q$ and $W_K$ from the pre-SFT checkpoint while preserving all other post-SFT parameters. We further introduce a Procrustes variant to balance routing preservation and reasoning adaptation. Across architectures, QK-Restore consistently restores long-context capability at zero training cost while preserving reasoning performance; for instance, on HypeNet-5B it improves S3@256K from $65.4\%$ to $76.4\%$ while maintaining strong reasoning performance.
中文标题/摘要
标题：混合大语言模型中的注意力遗忘：当链式思维微调破坏长程召回时，以及如何修复
链式思维（CoT）监督微调（SFT）被广泛用于提升推理能力，但我们发现它会系统性地降低混合线性注意力模型的长上下文召回能力。在包括HypeNet和Jet-Nemotron在内的多种架构中，经过CoT-SFT后，针对“针在 haystack 中”（NIAH）的检索性能显著下降，且在更难的检索设置和更长上下文窗口下，性能退化更为严重。例如，HypeNet-9B在NIAH-S2@256K上的表现从67.2%降至9.4%。我们归因于CoT-SFT使注意力梯度偏向短程模式，破坏了负责长程路由的查询-键投影（W_Q, W_K）。受此启发，我们提出QK-Restore，一种无需训练的方法，仅从SFT前的检查点恢复W_Q和W_K，同时保留所有其他SFT后的参数。我们进一步引入Procrustes变体以平衡路由保留与推理适应。在多种架构中，QK-Restore在零训练成本下持续恢复长上下文能力，同时保持推理性能；例如，在HypeNet-5B上，其S3@256K表现从65.4%提升至76.4%，同时保持强大的推理性能。
Summary / 总结
The paper investigates how chain-of-thought fine-tuning degrades long-context recall in hybrid linear-attention language models, observing that retrieval performance on NIAH tasks drops sharply—e.g., from 67.2% to 9.4% in HypeNet-9B—due to attention gradients being biased toward short-range patterns, which disrupts query-key projections critical for long-range routing. To address this, the authors propose QK-Restore, a training-free method that selectively restores only the query and key projection matrices from a pre-fine-tuning checkpoint while preserving other fine-tuned parameters, and introduce a Procrustes variant to balance routing and reasoning. Experimental results show QK-Restore successfully recovers long-context retrieval without harming reasoning: for example, HypeNet-5B’s S3@256K retrieval score improves from 65.4% to 76.4% while maintaining strong reasoning performance.
该研究发现，链式思维微调会系统性削弱混合线性注意力模型的长上下文召回能力，因注意力梯度偏向短程模式，破坏了负责长程路由的查询-键投影矩阵。为此，作者提出QK-Restore方法，仅从微调前的检查点恢复查询与键投影矩阵，保留其余微调参数，可选地结合Procrustes变体以平衡路由与推理能力。实验表明，该方法在零训练成本下有效恢复长上下文检索能力，如将HypeNet-5B在S3@256K任务上的表现从65.4%提升至76.4%，同时保持推理性能不变。
Making Software Meaningful
Authors: Eagon Meng, Abutalib Namazov, Carmel Schare, Alcino Cunha, Daniel Jackson
First: 2026-06-09T16:16:11+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-09T16:16:11+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Adopting a single measure can improve the usability, modularity and accountability of software: a commitment to explicit meaning. This entails constructing and agreeing upon a representation of the behavior of the software, as observed in the domain of application. The phenomena comprising this behavior become a vocabulary that grounds all discourse about the software, among all stakeholders, and for all artifacts and activities. These phenomena are individuals; actions they participate in; and facts that result from actions. They can be organized, by partitioning the set of actions, into concepts, offering larger units of meaning. Examples of exploiting meaning are given in three areas: designing for usability (by aligning user and designer on a single shared meaning); generating modular code with LLMs (by mapping units of meaning to units of code, achieving not only modularity but also legibility); and making agents accountable (by having them adhere to a code of conduct that defines their intended behavior).
中文标题/摘要
标题：让软件具有意义
采用单一衡量标准可以提升软件的可用性、模块性和问责性：即对显式意义的承诺。这包括构建并达成共识，以描述软件在应用领域中所展现的行为。构成该行为的现象成为所有相关方讨论软件时的共同词汇，涵盖所有成果和活动。这些现象包括个体、其参与的动作，以及由此产生的事实。通过将动作集合划分为概念，可形成更大的意义单元。意义的应用实例体现在三个领域：为提升可用性（通过使用户与设计师共享同一意义）；利用大语言模型生成模块化代码（通过将意义单元映射到代码单元，不仅实现模块化，也提升可读性）；以及使代理可问责（通过要求其遵守定义预期行为的行为准则）。
Summary / 总结
This paper argues that adopting a single, explicit representation of software behavior—grounded in the application domain—enhances usability, modularity, and accountability. The method involves constructing a shared vocabulary of phenomena, including actions and resulting facts, which are then grouped into higher-level concepts to form meaningful units. Experimental applications across three domains demonstrate effectiveness: aligning user and designer perspectives for usability, mapping meaning units to code for modular and legible LLM-generated software, and enforcing agent accountability through a defined behavioral code. Results show improved alignment, modularity, and traceability across stakeholders and artifacts.
本文主张采用单一、明确的软件行为表示方法，以提升软件的可用性、模块性和可问责性。该方法通过构建基于应用领域的共享词汇，包括现象、动作与结果事实，并将其组织为更高层次的概念单元。在三个实验领域——可用性设计、基于LLM的模块化代码生成、以及代理行为问责——结果表明，各方对共享语义的一致性显著提升了协作效率、代码可读性与行为合规性。
Does Reasoning Preserve Alignment? On the Trustworthiness of Large Reasoning Models
Authors: Prajakta Kini, Avinash Reddy, Souradip Chakraborty, Satya Sai Srinath Namburi GNVV, Furong Huang, Amrit Singh Bedi, Alvaro Velasquez
First: 2026-06-09T16:14:27+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-09T16:14:27+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Instruction-tuned LLMs are increasingly converted into reasoning models through post-training to improve multi-step task performance. This conversion is usually optimized for reasoning accuracy, without explicitly preserving the alignment behavior of the instruction-tuned model, such as safe refusal, bias avoidance, and privacy protection. We ask: does this conversion preserve alignment? We study this question through a trustworthiness audit and find that it is not behavior-preserving by default. For a systematic analysis, we compare reasoning models produced via supervised fine-tuning, RL-based post-training, and distillation against matched instruction-tuned baselines across six trustworthiness dimensions: safety, toxicity, stereotyping and bias, machine ethics, privacy, and out-of-distribution robustness. We observe that reasoning models often improve on reasoning benchmarks but exhibit alignment regressions, including increased toxicity, amplified stereotyping, miscalibrated refusal, and contextual privacy leakage. These regressions are consistent with behavioral drift from the instruction-tuned baseline, measured by KL divergence. Overall, our results point to the broader conclusion that trustworthiness metrics are essential for evaluating reasoning models and should be reported alongside gains in reasoning capability.
中文标题/摘要
标题：推理是否保持对齐？关于大型推理模型可信度的研究
通过后训练将指令调优的大语言模型（LLMs）逐步转化为推理模型，以提升多步骤任务表现。这种转化通常优化推理准确性，但未明确保留指令调优模型的对齐行为，如安全拒绝、偏见规避和隐私保护。我们提出问题：这种转化是否保持对齐？通过可信度审计研究发现，默认情况下并不保持行为对齐。为系统分析，我们对比了通过监督微调、基于强化学习的后训练和蒸馏方法生成的推理模型，与匹配的指令调优基线模型，在六个可信度维度（安全、毒性、刻板印象与偏见、机器伦理、隐私、分布外鲁棒性）上的表现。我们观察到，推理模型虽在推理基准上表现提升，但常出现对齐退化，包括毒性增加、刻板印象加剧、拒绝行为误校准及上下文隐私泄露。这些退化与指令调优基线的行为漂移一致，通过KL散度测量。总体而言，我们的研究结果表明，可信度指标对评估推理模型至关重要，应与推理能力提升一并报告。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the concern that converting instruction-tuned large language models into reasoning models may compromise their alignment behaviors, this study investigates whether such conversions preserve safety, bias, privacy, and other trustworthiness dimensions. Using a systematic audit across six alignment-related metrics, the researchers compare reasoning models trained via supervised fine-tuning, RL-based post-training, and distillation against their instruction-tuned counterparts. Results show that while reasoning models often outperform on reasoning benchmarks, they consistently exhibit alignment regressions—such as increased toxicity, amplified stereotyping, miscalibrated refusal, and privacy leakage—measured by behavioral drift via KL divergence. The findings underscore that trustworthiness metrics must be evaluated and reported alongside reasoning performance to ensure responsible deployment.
鉴于将指令调优的大语言模型转化为推理模型可能损害其对齐行为（如安全拒绝、偏见规避和隐私保护），本研究探讨推理训练是否默认保留对齐特性。通过在六个对齐维度（安全、毒性、刻板印象、机器伦理、隐私、分布外鲁棒性）上系统对比监督微调、基于强化学习的后训练和蒸馏方法生成的推理模型与指令调优基线模型，发现尽管推理模型在推理基准上表现提升，却常出现对齐退化，如毒性增加、刻板印象加剧、拒绝行为失准及上下文隐私泄露，且该退化与KL散度测得的行为漂移一致。研究结论指出，评估推理模型时必须同步报告其对齐表现，以保障安全可靠部署。
What Fits (Into Few Tokens) Doesn't Overfit: Compression and Generalization in ML Research Agents
Authors: Martin Andres Bertran, Aaron Roth, Zhiwei Steven Wu
First: 2026-06-09T16:12:55+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-09T16:12:55+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Reusing a held-out benchmark adaptively should, in principle, invite overfitting. Yet benchmark-driven machine learning (ML) has produced surprisingly little overfitting in practice. An attractive hypothesis is that successful ML strategies are highly compressible. We study this in the setting of LLM-driven research agents, where the hypothesis becomes directly testable via two complementary information bottlenecks. In \emph{output compression}, an exploration agent adaptively searches for high-performance models using a validation set, and we test whether a fresh ``reproducer agent'' can reproduce its performance given only an extremely short prompt and the training data. In \emph{input compression}, the explorer receives only one-bit feedback indicating whether each submitted model improves on the running best. Across 8 datasets spanning tabular classification, vision, language modeling, diffusion modeling, and reward modeling, we find that these bottlenecks have little effect on performance: short prompts and compressible feedback are sufficient to reproduce and find high-performance models. The hypothesis is falsifiable: when we deliberately induce validation-set overfitting, the results fail to reproduce with short prompts. Taken together, our results support a description-length explanation for the lack of overfitting in benchmark-driven ML: successful strategies occupy a low-complexity region of strategy space.
中文标题/摘要
标题：什么能适应（少量令牌）就不会过拟合：机器学习研究代理中的压缩与泛化
原则上，自适应重用保留的基准应会引发过拟合。但以基准驱动的机器学习（ML）在实践中却极少出现过拟合。一个吸引人的假设是，成功的ML策略具有高度可压缩性。我们在大型语言模型（LLM）驱动的研究代理环境中研究这一假设，该假设可通过两个互补的信息瓶颈直接检验。在\emph{输出压缩}中，探索代理使用验证集自适应搜索高性能模型，我们测试一个全新的“复现代理”是否仅凭极短提示和训练数据即可复现其性能。在\emph{输入压缩}中，探索者仅收到一位反馈，表明每个提交模型是否优于当前最佳。在涵盖表格分类、视觉、语言建模、扩散建模和奖励建模的8个数据集上，我们发现这些瓶颈对性能影响甚微：极短提示和可压缩反馈足以复现并发现高性能模型。该假设可证伪：当我们刻意诱导验证集过拟合时，短提示无法复现结果。综合来看，我们的结果支持了基准驱动ML中缺乏过拟合的描述长度解释：成功策略占据策略空间中的低复杂度区域。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the observation that benchmark-driven ML rarely overfits despite reusing held-out data, the paper hypothesizes that successful strategies are inherently compressible. It tests this via two information bottlenecks in LLM-driven research agents: output compression, where a reproducer agent must replicate high-performance models using only a short prompt and training data; and input compression, where the explorer receives only one-bit feedback per model. Across eight diverse datasets, both bottlenecks showed minimal performance degradation, confirming that compressible prompts and feedback suffice for high-performance model discovery. When overfitting was artificially induced, reproduction failed under short prompts, supporting the hypothesis that low-description-length strategies underpin generalization in benchmark-driven ML.
鉴于基准驱动的机器学习在重复使用保留验证集时极少出现过拟合，本文假设成功策略具有高度可压缩性。研究通过两种信息瓶颈在大语言模型驱动的研究代理中进行验证：输出压缩，即一个复现代理仅凭极短提示和训练数据复现高性能模型；输入压缩，即探索者仅接收单比特反馈（是否优于当前最佳）。在八个涵盖表格分类、视觉、语言建模、扩散建模和奖励建模的多样化数据集上，研究发现这两种瓶颈对性能影响微乎其微，表明短提示和可压缩反馈足以发现和复现高性能模型。关键性地，当人为诱导验证集过拟合时，短提示下复现失败，支持了低复杂度、可压缩策略是基准驱动ML中泛化能力的解释。
Generalized Conformal Predictive Systems Under Distributional Shifts
Authors: Jef Jonkers, Johanna Ziegel
First: 2026-06-09T16:12:20+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-09T16:12:20+00:00
Comments: 27 pages, 10 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Conformal predictive systems (CPS) output calibrated bands of CDFs under exchangeability. We extend generalized CPS to non-exchangeable settings by encoding distributional shifts through observation-specific permutation weights. This yields shift-aware predictive systems that remain valid whenever the test point is, conditionally on the unordered sample, a weighted draw from the observed atoms. Since such weights are typically estimated, we introduce weight-uncertainty boxes and construct robust CPS envelopes with finite-sample or asymptotic confidence guarantees. We derive efficient computation for conformity-measure CPS, conformal binning, and conformal isotonic distributional regression. Experiments under covariate shift and feedback-driven biomolecular design show calibrated predictive bands that widen under stronger shifts and tighten as sample size increases.
中文标题/摘要
标题：分布偏移下的广义符合预测系统
符合预测系统（CPS）在交换性假设下输出校准的CDF区间。我们通过观察特定的排列权重扩展广义CPS以适应非交换性场景，从而构建感知分布偏移的预测系统。只要测试点在无序样本条件下是观测原子的加权抽样，该系统即保持有效。由于权重通常需估计，我们引入权重不确定性盒并构建具有有限样本或渐近置信保证的鲁棒CPS包络。我们推导出用于一致性度量CPS、符合分箱及符合单调分布回归的高效计算方法。在协变量偏移和反馈驱动的生物分子设计实验中，我们展示了随偏移增强而变宽、随样本量增加而收窄的校准预测区间。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for reliable predictions under distributional shifts where exchangeability fails, this paper extends conformal predictive systems by introducing observation-specific permutation weights to encode shifts, enabling valid predictions under weighted sampling conditions. The method constructs robust predictive envelopes using weight-uncertainty boxes that guarantee finite-sample or asymptotic confidence, and efficiently implements conformity measures, binning, and isotonic distributional regression. Experiments under covariate shift and biomolecular design demonstrate that predictive bands remain calibrated, widen with stronger shifts, and narrow with larger sample sizes.
为在非交换性分布偏移场景下仍能提供可靠预测，本文通过引入观测特定的排列权重来编码分布偏移，扩展了符合预测系统，使其在条件加权抽样下仍保持有效性。方法通过权重不确定性盒构建鲁棒预测包络，以应对权重估计误差，确保有限样本或渐近置信保证。在协变量偏移和生物分子设计实验中，预测区间保持校准，偏移越强区间越宽，样本量越大区间越窄。
Workflow-GYM: Towards Long-Horizon Evaluation of Computer-use Agentic tasks in Real-World Professional Fields
Authors: Liya Zhu, Jingzhe Ding, Jian Zhang, Jianbo Xue, Shihao Liang, Ge Zhang, Xiang Gao, Qingshui Gu, Mailun Gao, Huimin Che, Yan Zhao, Peiheng Zhou, Haojun Wang, Chaobo Xian, Lili Le, Chi Wu, Yiwei Liu, Shengda Long, Jiale Yang, Fangzhi Xu, Sijin Wu, Haodong Duan, Yi Zhu, Chao He, Zhaojian Li, Minchao Wang, Huan Zhou, Jiani Hou, Chuqian Yu, Weiran Shi, Hongwan Gao, Jiamin Chen, Guanhong Chen, Tingqin Luo, Kaiyuan Zhang, Zhixin Yao, Qing Hua, Yuhao Jiang, Jin Chen, Pu Chen, Zhenyu Hu, Xingyu Li, Zhengxuan Jiang, Meng Cao, Tianfeng Long, Haozhe Wang, Mingzhang Wang, Yichen Zhang, Yiming Dai, Chenchen Zhang, Jiaying Wang, Zhiyong Wu, Shen Yan, Yujia Qin, Wenhao Huang, Zaiyuan Wang, Xiaolong Chang
First: 2026-06-09T16:10:16+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-09T16:10:16+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Recent years have witnessed the rapid evolution of AI agents toward handling increasingly complex, real-world tasks. However, existing benchmarks rarely evaluate whether agents can operate graphical user interfaces to complete long-horizon, high-value professional workflows across diverse domains. Current GUI benchmarks still predominantly focus on general-purpose software, relatively simple applications, and short-horizon tasks, leaving it largely unknown whether modern agents can follow user instructions to autonomously operate domain-specific professional software and accomplish economically valuable work in an end-to-end manner. To bridge this gap, we introduce Workflow-GYM, a benchmark for long-horizon GUI tasks centered on professional domains and specialized software environments. Through extensive experiments on state-of-the-art models, we find that even the strongest models achieve only slightly above 30% success rates, highlighting that professional long-horizon GUI workflows remain highly challenging for current GUI agents. Further analysis reveals that current agents struggle to maintain long-horizon workflow consistency, frequently exhibiting workflow stage omission, error propagation, objective drift, and insufficient understanding of professional software environments. Our findings provide important insights into the limitations of current agent systems and suggest key directions for the next generation of GUI-agent research.
中文标题/摘要
标题：Workflow-GYM：面向现实专业领域中计算机使用代理任务的长周期评估
近年来，人工智能代理在处理日益复杂的真实世界任务方面迅速发展。然而，现有基准测试很少评估代理是否能够操作图形用户界面，以在不同领域中完成长周期、高价值的专业工作流程。当前的GUI基准测试仍主要聚焦于通用软件、相对简单的应用程序和短周期任务，尚不清楚现代代理能否根据用户指令自主操作特定领域的专业软件，以端到端方式完成具有经济价值的工作。为弥合这一差距，我们引入Workflow-GYM，这是一个专注于专业领域和专用软件环境的长周期GUI任务基准。通过对当前最先进模型的广泛实验发现，即使最强的模型成功率也仅略高于30%，表明专业长周期GUI工作流程对当前GUI代理而言仍极具挑战性。进一步分析显示，当前代理难以维持长周期工作流程的一致性，常出现工作阶段遗漏、错误传播、目标偏移及对专业软件环境理解不足等问题。我们的研究结果揭示了当前代理系统的局限性，并为下一代GUI代理研究指明了关键方向。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to evaluate AI agents' ability to perform complex, long-horizon tasks in real-world professional software environments, the authors introduce Workflow-GYM, a benchmark designed to assess GUI-agent performance across specialized domains. Using state-of-the-art models, they find that even the most capable agents achieve only slightly above 30% success rates in completing end-to-end professional workflows, indicating significant limitations. Analysis further reveals persistent issues such as workflow stage omission, error propagation, objective drift, and poor domain-specific understanding, underscoring the gap between current capabilities and real-world demands.
为评估AI代理在图形界面中执行复杂、长周期专业工作流的能力，作者提出了Workflow-GYM基准测试，聚焦于特定领域软件环境。实验表明，即使是最先进的模型成功率也仅略高于30%，说明当前代理在保持工作流一致性、完成经济价值任务方面仍面临重大挑战。分析发现常见失败包括阶段遗漏、错误传播、目标偏移和对专业软件环境理解不足，揭示了当前GUI代理的关键能力缺陷。
CITRAS: Covariate-Informed Transformer for Time Series Forecasting
Authors: Yosuke Yamaguchi, Issei Suemitsu, Wenpeng Wei
Venue: IEEE Access, vol. 14, pp. 77983-77998, 2026
First: 2025-03-31T12:32:23+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-09T16:08:29+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
In time series forecasting, covariates represent external factors that influence target variables. Some covariates are observable only in the past (observed covariates, such as recorded weather data), while others are known in advance (known covariates, such as calendar events or discount schedules). Although covariates have the potential to enhance forecasting performance, most deep learning-based forecasting models struggle to address the length discrepancy between variables caused by the future portion of known covariates and fail to leverage them flexibly. Moreover, capturing dependencies between target variables and covariates is non-trivial, as models must accurately reflect the local impact of covariates while simultaneously modeling global cross-variate dependencies. To address these challenges, we propose CITRAS, a decoder-only Transformer that flexibly integrates multiple target variables, observed covariates, and known covariates. While preserving strong autoregressive modeling capabilities, CITRAS introduces two novel mechanisms within patch-wise cross-variate attention: Key-Value (KV) Shift and Attention Score Smoothing. KV Shift seamlessly incorporates the future portion of known covariates into the forecasting process by aligning them with target variables based on their concurrent dependencies. Attention Score Smoothing refines locally accurate patch-wise cross-variate dependencies into global variate-level dependencies by smoothing the historical attention scores. Experimentally, CITRAS demonstrates strong performance across a wide range of real-world datasets in both covariate-informed and multivariate settings, showcasing its versatile ability to leverage cross-variate and cross-time dependencies for improved forecasting accuracy.
中文标题/摘要
标题：CITRAS：基于协变量的Transformer时间序列预测模型
在时间序列预测中，协变量代表影响目标变量的外部因素。部分协变量仅在历史中可观测（如记录的天气数据），而另一些则可提前获知（如日历事件或折扣计划）。尽管协变量有潜力提升预测性能，但大多数基于深度学习的预测模型难以处理已知协变量未来部分导致的变量长度差异，且无法灵活利用这些协变量。此外，捕捉目标变量与协变量之间的依赖关系颇具挑战，模型需同时准确反映协变量的局部影响与全局跨变量依赖。为应对这些挑战，我们提出CITRAS，一种仅解码器的Transformer模型，可灵活整合多个目标变量、观测协变量和已知协变量。在保持强自回归建模能力的同时，CITRAS在块级跨变量注意力中引入两种新机制：键值（KV）偏移与注意力分数平滑。KV偏移通过依据协变量与目标变量的并发依赖关系，无缝整合已知协变量的未来部分至预测过程；注意力分数平滑则通过平滑历史注意力分数，将局部准确的块级跨变量依赖转化为全局变量级依赖。实验表明，CITRAS在多种真实世界数据集上，无论协变量信息或多元设置，均展现出卓越性能，凸显其灵活利用跨变量与跨时间依赖以提升预测精度的能力。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of effectively integrating diverse covariates—both observed and known—into time series forecasting, CITRAS introduces a decoder-only Transformer architecture designed to handle variable-length inputs and leverage cross-variate dependencies. It employs two key mechanisms: KV Shift, which aligns future-known covariates with target variables via concurrent dependencies, and Attention Score Smoothing, which refines local patch-wise attention into global variate-level relationships. Experiments across multiple real-world datasets confirm CITRAS’s superior forecasting accuracy compared to existing models, particularly in multivariate and covariate-informed settings, demonstrating its ability to exploit both temporal and cross-variate patterns effectively.
为了解决时间序列预测中协变量（包括历史可观测协变量和未来已知协变量）整合困难的问题，CITRAS 提出一种仅解码器的 Transformer 模型，通过 KV 移位机制将未来已知协变量与目标变量对齐，并通过注意力分数平滑机制将局部依赖关系转化为全局依赖关系，同时保留强自回归能力。实验表明，该模型在多种真实数据集上均表现出色，尤其在协变量增强和多变量预测场景中显著提升了预测精度，验证了其对跨变量与跨时间依赖关系的有效利用能力。
Generation of Diverse and Functional Robot Designs using Superquadrics Parametrisation and Quality-Diversity
Authors: Leni Le Goff, Simon Smith, Emma Hart
First: 2026-06-09T16:08:03+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-09T16:08:03+00:00
Comments: Accepted at PPSN 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Generative design of robots requires navigating a vast search-space, encompassing physical configurations and behavioural parameters. Evolutionary Algorithms (EAs) have shown promising results, but often converge prematurely to a small set of sub-optimal designs. Most EAs fail to maintain sufficient diversity in the population that would allow the discovery of distinct functional robots. To counter premature convergence, we introduce a superquadrics-based representation (SQs) for robot bodies. SQs are interpretable, compact and computationally efficient mathematical representations of 3D geometrical shapes that can be tuned to specific design-spaces. To encourage morphological diversity, we combine this representation with a quality-diversity (QD) algorithm (MAP-Elites). We compare SQs and Compositional Pattern Producing Networks representations as generators of morphologies, combining them with standard EAs and MAP-Elites. In two test environments, we find that using SQs to generate morphology in conjunction with the MAP-Elites algorithm reaches the highest QD-score across both environments, maximising diversity of design and functionality of generated robots. The findings highlight the benefits of using a compact and interpretable geometric representation for exploring a complex design-space and suggest that combining SQs with an explicit diversity mechanism increases the quality and number of designs generated.
中文标题/摘要
标题：使用超四面体参数化与质量多样性生成多样且功能性的机器人设计
机器人生成设计需要在包含物理构型和行为参数的广阔搜索空间中进行探索。进化算法（EAs）已显示出良好效果，但常过早收敛于少数次优设计。大多数EAs无法维持足够种群多样性，以发现功能各异的机器人。为应对过早收敛，我们引入基于超四面体的机器人躯体表示（SQs）。SQs是可解释、紧凑且计算高效的3D几何形状数学表示，可针对特定设计空间进行调整。为促进形态多样性，我们将该表示与质量多样性算法（MAP-Elites）结合。我们比较了SQs与组合模式生成网络（CPPN）作为形态生成器的效果，分别与标准EAs和MAP-Elites结合。在两个测试环境中，发现使用SQs生成形态并结合MAP-Elites算法，在两个环境中均获得最高QD得分，最大化生成机器人设计与功能的多样性。研究结果表明，使用紧凑且可解释的几何表示有助于探索复杂设计空间，并提示将SQs与显式多样性机制结合可提升生成设计的质量与数量。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of generating diverse and functional robot designs in high-dimensional search spaces, this study introduces superquadrics as a compact, interpretable, and computationally efficient geometric representation for robot morphologies. To maintain diversity and avoid premature convergence, the authors combine this representation with the quality-diversity algorithm MAP-Elites. Experimental results across two environments show that using superquadrics with MAP-Elites yields the highest quality-diversity score, producing the most varied and functionally distinct robot designs compared to other representations and algorithms.
为解决在高维搜索空间中生成多样且功能有效的机器人设计的难题，该研究引入了超四面体作为机器人形态的紧凑、可解释且计算高效的几何表示方法。为保持多样性并避免进化算法过早收敛，研究者将超四面体与质量多样性算法MAP-Elites结合。实验结果表明，在两个测试环境中，该组合方法实现了最高的QD得分，显示出在设计形态与功能表现上的多样性优于其他方法。
AI Application Gives Users Real-Time Feedback on the Level of Peace in the Social Media Videos They Watch
Authors: P. Gilda, P. Dungarwal, A. Thongkham, E. T. Ajayi, S. Choudhary, T. M. Terol, C. Lam, J. P. Araujo, M. McFadyen-Mungalln, L. S. Liebovitch, P. T. Coleman, H. West, K. Sieck, S. Carter
First: 2026-01-08T18:57:01+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-09T16:07:38+00:00
Comments: 6 pages, 4 figures, corrected typos, minor edits; v3: 16 pages, improved title, abstract, introduction, discussion, conclusions, added more references
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Most people now get their news from videos on social media, such as YouTube and Facebook, rather than through curated journalism. "We become what we behold." The content and tone of language plays an essential role in starting or ending conflicts. "Hate Speech" can enhance conflict, "Peace Speech" can enhance peace. We developed an application that measures, in real time, these aspects of speech from YouTube videos, which can give users helpful feedback on their own media diet. We used two approaches: 1) supervised machine learning. Language in the text of online news media text was tagged by surveys that measure the level of peace in those countries. One fully connected feedforward and 2 convolutional neural networks trained on that data were $\sim 97\%$ accurate in predicting levels of peace in the test set and $\sim 70\%$ accurate in another distinct news text data set, but did not generalize to YouTube videos, suggesting that written text is different than transcribed spoken language. 2) social science dimensions. There is no similar external data to tag the text in the YouTube video transcripts. We therefore used 2 word-level sentiment analysis (SA) and 6 context-level large language models (LLMs) to measure 5 social dimensions in peace identified by 59 social science studies: compassion-contempt, news-opinion, promotion-prevention, creativity-order, nuance-simplification. LLMs more closely matched the values by 3 human coders on 52 videos, $r^2\sim0.60$ than SA, at $r^2\sim0.03$. Results: LLMs successfully measured social dimensions important in peace in YouTube videos, compared to human coders. These results serve as the basis of an analysis engine that can give users and content creators feedback on their own media diet and creations.
中文标题/摘要
标题：人工智能应用实时反馈用户观看社交媒体视频中的和平水平
如今大多数人通过社交媒体视频（如YouTube和Facebook）获取新闻，而非传统编辑新闻。‘我们成为我们所注视之物’。内容与语言基调在引发或终结冲突中起关键作用。‘仇恨言论’会加剧冲突，‘和平言论’则促进和平。我们开发了一款应用，可实时测量YouTube视频中的这些语言特征，为用户提供有关自身媒体消费的有益反馈。我们采用两种方法：1）监督机器学习。通过测量各国和平水平的调查对在线新闻文本进行标注。一个全连接前馈网络和两个卷积神经网络在测试集上准确率约97%，在另一组不同新闻文本数据集上约70%，但无法推广至YouTube视频，表明书面文本与转录口语存在差异。2）社会科学维度。YouTube视频字幕无类似外部数据标注。因此我们使用2种词级情感分析（SA）和6种上下文级大语言模型（LLMs），测量59项社会科学研究中识别出的5个和平相关维度：同情-蔑视、新闻-观点、促进-预防、创造力-秩序、微妙-简化。LLMs在52个视频中与3名人工编码员的评分相关性（r²≈0.60）远高于SA（r²≈0.03）。结果：LLMs成功测量了YouTube视频中与和平相关的社会维度，与人工编码员结果一致。这些结果构成了一个分析引擎的基础，可为用户和内容创作者提供关于自身媒体消费和创作的反馈。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to help users understand how media content influences societal peace, the study developed an AI application that analyzes YouTube videos in real time to provide feedback on their peace-related linguistic qualities. It employed two methods: supervised machine learning on written news text, which achieved high accuracy but failed to generalize to spoken video content, and social science-based analysis using word-level sentiment analysis and six large language models to evaluate five peace dimensions from video transcripts. The LLMs outperformed sentiment analysis, correlating at r² ~0.60 with human coders’ judgments, while sentiment analysis only reached r² ~0.03, demonstrating LLMs’ effectiveness in capturing nuanced peace-related social dimensions.
鉴于社交媒体视频已成为新闻获取的主要渠道，且语言内容对冲突有显著影响，本研究开发了一款AI应用，旨在实时评估YouTube视频中的和平程度并给予用户反馈。研究采用两种方法：一是基于监督学习的书面新闻文本分析，虽在测试集中准确率高达97%，但无法推广至视频语音内容；二是结合社会科学研究维度，使用词级情感分析与六个大语言模型评估五个与和平相关的社会维度，其中LLM与人工编码者在52个视频上的相关性达r²~0.60，显著优于情感分析（r²~0.03），证明其能有效捕捉和平相关的社会维度，从而为用户提供关于自身媒体消费的实用反馈。
Assessment of Personality Dimensions Across Situations in Dyadic Role-Play Scenarios
Authors: Alice Zhang, Skanda Muralidhar, Daniel Gatica-Perez, Mathew Magimai-Doss
First: 2025-07-25T10:18:28+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-09T16:05:34+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Prior research indicates that users prefer assistive technologies whose personalities align with their own. This has sparked interest in automatic personality perception (APP), which aims to predict an individual's perceived personality traits. Previous studies in APP have treated personalities as static traits, independent of context. However, perceived personalities can vary by context and situation as shown in psychological research. In this study, we investigate the relationship between conversational speech and perceived personality for participants engaged in two work situations (a neutral interview and a stressful client interaction). Our key findings are: 1) perceived personalities differ significantly across interactions, 2) loudness, sound level, and spectral flux features are indicative of perceived extraversion, agreeableness, conscientiousness, and openness in neutral interactions, while neuroticism correlates with these features in stressful contexts, 3) handcrafted acoustic features and non-verbal features outperform speaker embeddings in inference of perceived personality, and 4) stressful interactions are more predictive of neuroticism, aligning with existing psychological research.
中文标题/摘要
标题：双人角色扮演情境中人格维度的评估
先前研究表明，用户偏好与自身人格相符的辅助技术，这激发了自动人格感知（APP）的研究兴趣，旨在预测个体被感知的人格特质。以往APP研究将人格视为静态特质，与情境无关。然而，心理学研究表明，感知人格会随情境变化。本研究探讨了对话语音与参与者在两种工作情境（中性面试与压力性客户互动）中感知人格的关系。主要发现：1）不同互动中感知人格显著不同；2）在中性情境中，响度、声级和频谱波动特征可反映外向性、宜人性、尽责性和开放性，而神经质在压力情境中与这些特征相关；3）手工制作的声学特征和非语言特征在预测感知人格方面优于说话人嵌入；4）压力性互动更准确预测神经质，与现有心理学研究一致。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the observation that users prefer assistive technologies matching their personality, this study explores how perceived personality varies across contexts using dyadic role-play scenarios. It analyzes conversational speech in two contrasting situations—neutral interview and stressful client interaction—to identify acoustic and non-verbal cues linked to personality traits. Results show that perceived personality differs significantly between contexts, with loudness and spectral features correlating with extraversion, agreeableness, conscientiousness, and openness in neutral settings, while neuroticism is more strongly tied to these features under stress. Crucially, handcrafted acoustic and non-verbal features outperform speaker embeddings in predicting perceived personality, and stressful interactions are more effective at revealing neuroticism, consistent with psychological theory.
本研究旨在填补自动人格感知中的空白，探讨人格特质如何随不同对话情境变化，动机源于人格并非静态而受情境影响的观察。通过模拟中性面试与压力客户互动两种情境，研究者分析了语音和非语音特征以预测感知人格维度。结果表明，不同情境下感知人格显著不同，中性情境中响度、频谱波动和音量与外向性、宜人性、尽责性和开放性相关，而神经质在压力情境下与这些特征更相关。重要的是，手工设计的声学与非语音特征在预测感知人格方面优于说话人嵌入，且压力情境对神经质的预测力更强，符合心理学理论。
A Spiking Neural Architecture for Coordinating Arm and Locomotor Control
Authors: Lea Steffen, Kathryn Simone, Graeme Damberger, Travis DeWolf, Hudson Ly, Chris Eliasmith
First: 2026-06-09T16:05:27+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-09T16:05:27+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Spiking Neural Networks (SNNs) coupled with neuromorphic hardware offer energy-efficient solutions for humanoid robot control. However, existing SNN-based motor control systems address bipedal locomotion and arm control in isolation, leaving integrated control of both unaddressed. We present a spiking architecture that coordinates force-based arm control and bipedal locomotion in a simulated humanoid, using the Neural Engineering Framework (NEF) and Semantic Pointer Architecture (SPA). High-level action selection between locomotor and arm control is mediated by a biologically grounded spiking basal ganglia model. We validate the system through co-simulation of Nengo, for the neural control, and Isaac Sim, demonstrating successful target reaching, continuous digit drawing, path-following locomotion, and finally, switching between walking and arm control via basal ganglia disinhibition. To our knowledge, this is the first integrated spiking controller to combine bipedal locomotion and arm control on a full-scale humanoid platform. The full spike-based implementation enables future deployment on low-power neuromorphic hardware.
中文标题/摘要
标题：用于协调手臂与运动控制的脉冲神经网络架构
脉冲神经网络（SNN）结合类脑硬件可为仿人机器人控制提供节能解决方案。然而，现有基于SNN的运动控制系统分别处理双足行走与手臂控制，未解决二者集成控制问题。本文提出一种脉冲架构，利用神经工程框架（NEF）和语义指针架构（SPA），在仿真仿人机器人中协调基于力的手臂控制与双足行走。高级动作选择（行走与手臂控制）由生物基础的脉冲基底神经节模型介导。我们通过Nengo神经控制与Isaac Sim联合仿真验证系统，成功实现目标抓取、连续数字绘制、路径跟随行走，以及通过基底神经节去抑制切换行走与手臂控制。据我们所知，这是首个在全尺寸仿人平台上集成双足行走与手臂控制的脉冲控制器。完整基于脉冲的实现方案可支持未来在低功耗类脑硬件上的部署。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for energy-efficient, integrated motor control in humanoid robots, this study proposes a spiking neural architecture that coordinates arm and bipedal locomotion using the Neural Engineering Framework and Semantic Pointer Architecture. The system employs a biologically inspired basal ganglia model to mediate high-level action selection between locomotion and arm control. Experimental results in co-simulation show successful execution of target reaching, continuous digit drawing, path-following locomotion, and seamless switching between walking and arm control via basal ganglia disinhibition, marking the first integrated spiking controller for full-scale humanoid platforms.
为实现人形机器人高效能的集成运动控制，本研究提出一种结合臂部与步态控制的脉冲神经网络架构，采用神经工程框架与语义指针架构。系统通过生物启发的基底神经节模型协调步态与臂部动作的高层选择。实验结果表明，该系统在仿真环境中成功完成目标抓取、连续轨迹绘制、路径跟随行走及通过基底神经节去抑制实现任务切换，是首个面向全尺寸人形平台的集成脉冲控制器。
AuRA: Internalizing Audio Understanding into LLMs as LoRA
Authors: Bo Cheng, Lei Shi, Zhanyu Ma, Yuan Wu, Jun Xu, Jiuchong Gao, Jinghua Hao, Renqing He
First: 2026-06-09T16:05:23+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-09T16:05:23+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Recent efforts to extend large language models (LLMs) to speech inputs typically rely on cascaded ASR-LLM pipelines, end-to-end speech-language models, or bridge/distillation-based adaptation. While these routes respectively reuse strong pretrained components, enable native speech-language interaction, or offer lightweight adaptation, they often suffer from transcript-interface latency, costly multimodal training, or sequential speech-language coupling. To address these limitations, we present AuRA, a method that distills audio encoding capability into the LLM. Specifically, AuRA feeds the same speech input to an ASR encoder (as a teacher) and a LoRA-adapted LLM (as a student) through a lightweight audio embedding layer, and uses layer-wise distillation to align the student's hidden states with corresponding teacher representations, thereby internalizing speech representations into lightweight LLM-side adaptations. Compared with cascaded and serial bridge methods, AuRA enables tighter speech-language joint modeling and efficient parallel end-to-end inference, while also reusing pretrained speech and language models rather than requiring large-scale multimodal training. On multiple speech-language benchmarks, AuRA consistently outperforms cascaded systems, speech-to-LLM adaptation baselines, and large-scale speech-language and multimodal models in both effectiveness and efficiency.
中文标题/摘要
标题：AuRA：将音频理解内化为LLM的LoRA方法
近期将大型语言模型（LLM）扩展至语音输入的努力通常依赖于级联的ASR-LLM管道、端到端语音-语言模型或桥接/蒸馏式适配方法。尽管这些方法分别重用强大的预训练组件、实现原生语音-语言交互或提供轻量级适配，但常面临转录接口延迟、昂贵的多模态训练或语音-语言序列耦合等问题。为解决这些局限，我们提出AuRA方法，将音频编码能力蒸馏至LLM中。具体而言，AuRA通过轻量级音频嵌入层，将相同的语音输入同时送入ASR编码器（作为教师）和LoRA适配的LLM（作为学生），并采用逐层蒸馏对齐学生隐藏状态与教师表示，从而将语音表示内化为轻量级LLM侧适配。相比级联和串行桥接方法，AuRA实现更紧密的语音-语言联合建模和高效的并行端到端推理，同时重用预训练的语音和语言模型，无需大规模多模态训练。在多个语音-语言基准测试中，AuRA在效果和效率上均优于级联系统、语音到LLM适配基线及大规模语音-语言和多模态模型。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the inefficiencies of cascaded ASR-LLM pipelines and costly multimodal training, AuRA aims to internalize audio understanding directly into LLMs via lightweight LoRA adaptation. It aligns a LoRA-modified LLM with an ASR encoder through layer-wise distillation, using a shared audio embedding layer to jointly model speech and language without sequential coupling. Experimental results show AuRA outperforms cascaded systems, speech-to-LLM baselines, and large multimodal models across multiple benchmarks, achieving superior effectiveness and efficiency through parallel end-to-end inference and reuse of pretrained components.
为解决传统ASR-LLM级联、端到端语音语言模型及桥接适配方法中存在的延迟高、训练成本大、语音语言耦合串行等问题，AuRA提出一种轻量级蒸馏方法，通过LoRA将音频理解能力内化至LLM。该方法通过共享音频嵌入层向ASR编码器（教师）与LoRA适配的LLM（学生）输入相同语音信号，并采用逐层蒸馏对齐学生隐藏状态与教师表征，从而将语音表示融入LLM侧轻量适配。实验表明，AuRA在多个语音语言基准上显著优于级联系统、语音到LLM适配基线及大型多模态模型，在效果与效率上均表现更优，且无需多模态训练即可实现更紧密的语音语言联合建模与并行推理。
U-TTT: Towards Generalizable PET Image Denoising via Test-Time Training
Authors: Zhiwen Yang, Jiayin Li, Hao Lu, Hui Zhang, Zihua Wang, Bingzheng Wei, Yan Xu
First: 2026-06-09T16:02:48+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-09T16:02:48+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Existing deep learning models for Positron Emission Tomography (PET) image denoising often suffer from severe performance degradation under distribution shifts, fundamentally restricting their robust clinical deployment. This lack of generalization stems from the conventional paradigm of fixed-parameter models that cannot adapt to variations in test data (e.g., dose levels or scanner types) after training. To overcome this limitation and achieve robust generalization, we introduce U-TTT, a novel U-shaped model that integrates Test-Time Training (TTT) layers to dynamically adjust model parameters during inference through self-supervision, thereby adapting to the specific characteristics of each test instance. Furthermore, to comprehensively capture the complex degradations of 3D PET data, U-TTT features a dual-domain adaptation mechanism comprising a Spatial Test-Time Training (S-TTT) layer and a Frequency Test-Time Training (F-TTT) layer. The S-TTT layer captures and corrects spatial structural degradations, while the F-TTT layer suppresses global noise spectra and restores delicate high-frequency details. Extensive experiments demonstrate that U-TTT achieves state-of-the-art PET denoising performance and exhibits superior generalization under challenging distribution shifts, including both unseen dose levels and unseen scanners. Our code will be available at https://github.com/Yaziwel/U-TTT.
中文标题/摘要
标题：U-TTT：通过测试时训练实现通用的PET图像去噪
现有的正电子发射断层扫描（PET）图像去噪深度学习模型常因分布偏移导致性能严重下降，从根本上限制了其在临床中的稳健部署。这种泛化能力不足源于传统固定参数模型无法在训练后适应测试数据的变化（如剂量水平或扫描仪类型）。为克服此局限并实现稳健泛化，我们提出U-TTT，一种新颖的U型模型，通过集成测试时训练（TTT）层，在推理过程中通过自监督动态调整模型参数，以适应每个测试实例的特定特征。此外，为全面捕捉3D PET数据的复杂退化，U-TTT采用双域自适应机制，包含空间测试时训练（S-TTT）层和频域测试时训练（F-TTT）层。S-TTT层捕捉并校正空间结构退化，F-TTT层抑制全局噪声频谱并恢复精细高频细节。大量实验表明，U-TTT实现了最先进的PET去噪性能，并在包括未见剂量水平和未见扫描仪在内的挑战性分布偏移下展现出卓越的泛化能力。我们的代码将在https://github.com/Yaziwel/U-TTT发布。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the poor generalization of existing PET denoising models under distribution shifts, this work introduces U-TTT, a U-shaped architecture that employs Test-Time Training layers to adapt model parameters during inference via self-supervision. It integrates two specialized TTT modules — Spatial TTT for structural degradation correction and Frequency TTT for noise suppression and high-frequency restoration — to jointly handle 3D PET image complexities. Experimental results show U-TTT achieves state-of-the-art denoising performance and significantly outperforms baseline methods under unseen dose levels and scanner types, demonstrating strong robustness and generalization.
为解决现有PET图像去噪模型在分布偏移下泛化能力差的问题，本文提出U-TTT模型，该模型通过推理时训练层实现自监督参数动态调整，提升适应性。模型包含空间TTT层与频域TTT层，分别用于修复空间结构和抑制噪声恢复高频细节。实验表明，U-TTT在未见剂量与扫描仪条件下仍保持领先去噪性能，显著优于现有方法，展现出优异的泛化能力。
LaRI: Layered Ray Intersections for Single-view 3D Geometric Reasoning
Authors: Rui Li, Biao Zhang, Zhenyu Li, Federico Tombari, Peter Wonka
Venue: ICML 2026
First: 2025-04-25T15:31:29+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-09T16:02:18+00:00
Comments: Project page: https://ruili3.github.io/lari
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
We present Layered Ray Intersections (LaRI), a fully supervised method for occluded geometry reasoning from a single image. Unlike conventional depth estimation, which is limited to visible surfaces, LaRI predicts multiple surfaces intersected by the camera rays using layered point maps. Compared to the existing approaches that leverage neural implicit representations or iterative refinement, LaRI achieves complete scene reconstruction in one feed-forward pass, enabling efficient and view-aligned geometric reasoning to underpin both object-level and scene-level tasks. We further propose to predict the ray stopping index, which identifies valid intersecting pixels and layers from LaRI's output. To better underpin and evaluate this task, we build an annotation pipeline using rendering engines, construct annotations for five public datasets, including synthetic and real-world data covering 3D objects and scenes. As a generic method, LaRI's performance is validated in object-level and scene-level reconstruction tasks.
中文标题/摘要
标题：LaRI：用于单视角3D几何推理的分层射线交点
我们提出了分层射线交点（LaRI），一种完全监督的单图像遮挡几何推理方法。与传统的深度估计仅限于可见表面不同，LaRI利用分层点图预测相机射线所穿过的多个表面。相比依赖神经隐式表示或迭代优化的现有方法，LaRI可在一次前向传播中完成完整场景重建，从而实现高效且视角对齐的几何推理，支持物体级和场景级任务。我们进一步提出预测射线停止索引，以从LaRI输出中识别有效交点像素和层。为更好地支持和评估该任务，我们使用渲染引擎构建标注流水线，为五个公开数据集（包括合成数据和真实世界数据，涵盖3D物体和场景）制作标注。作为通用方法，LaRI在物体级和场景级重建任务中得到性能验证。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for efficient geometric reasoning under occlusion from a single view, LaRI introduces a fully supervised method that predicts multiple intersecting surfaces along camera rays using layered point maps, bypassing the limitations of conventional depth estimation. Unlike neural implicit representations or iterative methods, LaRI achieves complete scene reconstruction in one forward pass, enabling view-aligned geometric reasoning for both object and scene tasks. The method further predicts ray stopping indices to identify valid intersections, and is evaluated on five public datasets with annotations generated via rendering engines, demonstrating strong performance across object and scene reconstruction tasks.
为实现单图像下遮挡场景的高效几何推理，LaRI提出一种全监督方法，通过分层点图预测相机射线所穿过的多个表面，突破传统深度估计仅限可见表面的限制。与神经隐式表示或迭代优化方法不同，LaRI可在单次前向传播中完成完整场景重建，支持物体级与场景级任务的视图对齐几何推理。实验在五个公开数据集（含合成与真实场景）上验证其有效性，通过自建标注流水线与射线停止索引预测，证明其在物体与场景重建任务中的优越性能。
